<1>
kaumārabhṛtyam
sūtrasthānam
kiṃvā lehayitavyaṃ ca kiṃvā lehitalakṣaṇam
atilehitadoṣāḥ ke ke ca doṣā alehite
mandīlīḍhasya kiṃ rūpaṃ guḍadoṣāśca tatra ke
ke lehanodbhavā rogāḥ kaśca teṣāmupakramaḥ
etanme bhagavan sarvaṃ vaktumarhasi tattvataḥ
sukhaṃ duḥkhaṃ hi bālānāṃ dṛśyate lehanāśrayam
iti pṛṣṭo mahābhāgaḥ kaśyapo lokapūjitaḥ
praśnaṃ provāca nikhilaṃ prajānāṃ hitakāmyayā
yadannapānaṃ prāyeṇa garbhiṇī strī niṣevate
raso nirvartate tādṛk tridhā cāsyāḥ pravartate
mātṛpuṣṭyarthamekāṃśo dvitīyo garbhapuṣṭaye
tṛtīyaḥ stanapuṣṭyarthaṃ nāryā garbhastu puṣyati
tādṛkprakṛtayastasmādgarbhāt prabhṛti dehinaḥ
vātapittakaphasthūṇāstisraḥ prakṛtayaśca tāḥ
vātikāḥ paittikāḥ kecit kaphinaścaiva dehinaḥ
dvandvaprakṛtayaścānye samasthūṇāstathā'pare
arogāstu samasthūṇā vātikādyāḥ sadāturāḥ
etāḥ prakṛtayaḥ proktā dehināṃ vṛddhajīvaka
etā āśritya tattvajño bheṣajānyupakalpayet
ya etā veda tattvena na sa muhyati bheṣaje
vilaṅgaphalamātraṃ tu jātamātrasya dehinaḥ
bheṣajaṃ madhusarpirbhyāṃ matimānupakalpayet
vardhamānasya tu śiśormāse māse vivardhayet
athāmalakamātraṃ tu paraṃ vidvānna vardhayet
akṣīrā jananī yeṣāmalpakṣīrā'pi vā bhavet
duṣṭakṣīrā prasūtā yā dhātrī vā yasya tādṛśī
duṣprajātābhṛśavyādhipīḍitāyāśca ye sutāḥ
vātikāḥ paittikā ye ca ye ca syuḥ kaphavarjitāḥ
stanyena ye na tṛpyanti pītvā pītvā rudanti ca
anidrā niśi ye ca syurye ca bālā mahāśanāḥ
alpamūtrapurīṣāśca bālā dīptāgnayaśca ye
nirāmayāśca tanavo mṛdvaṅgā ye ca karśitāḥ
varcaḥkarma na kurvanti bālā ye ca tryahāt param
evaṃvidhāñchiśūnāha lehayediti kaśyapaḥ
--- ca mandāgnijaṭharo janaḥ
nidrālurbahuviṇmūtraḥ svalpo yo dṛḍhagātrakaḥ
kalyāṇamātṛko'jīrṇī gurustanyopasevitā
sutaḥ sarvarasāśinyā ūrdhvajatrurujānvitaḥ
āme jvare'tisāre ca kāmalāśothapāṇḍuṣu
hṛdrogaśvāsakāseṣu gudabastyudarāmaye
ānāhe gaṇḍavaisarpe chardyarocakayo
he sarvagraheṣu ca
na lehayedalasake nāhanyahani nāśitam
na durdinapurovāte nāsātmyaṃ nātimātrayā
sevitānyannapānāni garbhiṇyā yānyabha ---
tāni sātmyāni bālasya tasmāttānyupacārayet
deśakālāgnimātrāṇāṃ na ca kuryādvyatikramam
dravyāṇāṃ lehanīyānāṃ vidhiścaivopadekṣyate
vighṛṣya dhaute dṛṣadi prāṅmukhī laghunā'mbunā
āmathya madhusarpirbhyāṃ lehayet kanakaṃ śiśum
suvarṇaprāśanaṃ hyetanmedhāgnibalavardhanam
āyuṣyaṃ maṅgalaṃ puṇyaṃ vṛṣyaṃ varṇyaṃ grahāpaham
māsāt paramamedhāvī vyādhibhirna ca dhṛṣyate
ṣaḍbhirmāsaiḥ śrutadharaḥ suvarṇaprāśanādbhavet
brāhmī maṇḍūkaparṇī ca triphalā citrako vacā
śatapuṣpāśatāvaryau dantī nāgabalā trivṛt
ekaikaṃ madhusarpirbhyāṃ medhājananamabhyaset
kalyāṇakaṃ pañcagavyaṃ medhyaṃ brāhmīghṛtaṃ tathā
samaṅgā triphalā brāhmī dve bale citrakastathā
madhu sarpiriti prāśyaṃ medhāyurbalavṛddhaye
kuṣṭhaṃ vaṭāṅkurā gaurī pippalyastriphalā vacā
sasaindhavairghṛtaṃ pakvaṃ medhājananamuttamam
brāhmī siddhārthakāḥ kuṣṭhaṃ saindhavaṃ sārivā vacā
pippalyaśceti taiḥ siddhaṃ ghṛtaṃ nāmnā'bhayaṃ smṛtam
na piśācā na rakṣāṃsi na yakṣā na ca mātaraḥ
prabādhante kumāraṃ taṃ yaḥ prāśnīyādidaṃ ghṛtam
khadiraḥ pṛśniparṇī ca syatṛ saindhavaṃ bale
kevuketi kaṣāyaḥ syāt pādaśiṣṭo jalāḍhake
ardhaprasthaṃ pacedatra tulyakṣīraṃ ghṛtasya tu
ghṛtaṃ saṃvardhanaṃ nāma lehyaṃ madhuyutaṃ sadā
nirvyādhirvardhate śīghraṃ saṃsarpatyāśu gacchati
paṅgumūkāśrutijaḍā yujyante cāśu karmabhiḥ
svarasasyāḍhake brāhmyā ghṛtaprasthaṃ vipācayet
savatsā'jāgopayasāmāḍhakāḍhakamāvapet
triphalāṃ'śumatī drākṣā vacā kuṣṭhaṃ hareṇavaḥ
pippalīpippalīmūlacavyacitrakanāgaram
tvakpatrabālakośīracandanotpalapadmakam
śatāvarī nāgabalā dantī pāṭhā priyaṅgukā
devadāru haridre dve jīvanīyaśca yo gaṇaḥ
viḍaṅgo guggulurjātiḥ
ekonaviṃśo'dhyāyaḥ
--- śakunī kaṭutiktake
skandaṣaṣṭhīgrahau jñeyau vyāpanne sānnipātike
pūtanā svādukaṭuke śeṣāḥ saṃsṛṣṭadoṣajāḥ
bahuviṇmūtratā svādau kaṣāye mūtraviḍgrahaḥ
tailavarṇe balī tulyā ghṛtavarṇe mahādhanaḥ
yaśasvī dhūmavarṇe tu śuddhe sarvaguṇoditaḥ
tasmāt saṃśodhanaparā nityaṃ dhātrī praśasyate
kaṣāyapānairvamanairvirekaiḥ pathyabhojanaiḥ
vājīkaraṇasiddhaiśca snehaiḥ kṣīraṃ viśudhyati
triphalā satrikaṭukā pāṭhā madhurasā vacā
kolacūrṇaṃ tvaco jambvā devadāru ca peṣitam
sarṣapaprasṛtonmiśraṃ pātavyaṃ kṣaudrasaṃyutam
etat stanyasya duṣṭasya śreṣṭhaṃ śodhanamucyate
śṛṅgaverapaṭolābhyāṃ pippalīcūrṇacūrṇitam
yūṣapathyaṃ vidadhyācca hyannapānaṃ ca yallaghu
dhātakīpuṣpamelā ca samaṅgī maricāni ca
jambūtvacaṃ samadhukaṃ kṣīraśodhanamuttamam
nāḍikā saguḍā siddhā hiṅgujātisusaṃskṛtā
kṣīraṃ māṃsaraso madyaṃ kṣīravardhanamuttamam
vājīkaraṇasiddhaṃ vā kṣīraṃ kṣīravivardhanam
ghṛtatailopasevā ca bastayaśca payaskarāḥ
pāṭhā mahauṣadhaṃ dāru mūrvāmustakavatsakāḥ
sārivāriṣṭakaṭukāḥ kairātaṃ triphalā vacā
guḍūcī madhukaṃ drākṣā daśamūlaṃ sadīpanam
rakṣoghnaśca paṭolaśca gaṇaḥ kṣīraviśodhanaḥ
lābhataḥ kvathitasteṣāṃ kaṣāyaḥ sa tu sevitaḥ
kṣīraṃ śodhayati kṣipraṃ ciravyāpannamapyuta
sakṣaudraḥ kaphasaṃsṛṣṭe saghṛtaḥ śeṣayorbhavet
netyeke śleṣmaṇaḥ sthānāt kṣīraṃ hi kaphasaṃbhavam
masūrāḥ ṣaṣṭikā mudgāḥ kulatthāḥ śālayo ghṛtam
gavyamājaṃ payaḥ kāle lavaṇaṃ cāpyanaudbhidam
āhāravidhiruddiṣṭaḥ stanyaśodhanakālikaḥ
gurvannasnehamāṃsāni divāsvapnaṃ ca varjayet
śodhanādvā svabhāvādvā yasyāḥ kṣīraṃ viśuṣyati
tasyāḥ kṣīraprajanane prayateta vicakṣaṇaḥ
madhurāṇyannapānāni dravāṇi lavaṇāni ca
madyāni sīdhuvarjyāni śākaṃ siddhārthakādṛte
varāhamahiṣādūrdhvaṃ māṃsānāṃ ca raso hitaḥ
laśunānāṃ palāṇḍūnāṃ sevanaṃ śayanaṃ sukham
krodhādhvabhayaśokānāmāyāsānāṃ ca varjanam
a---yā bhavati vatsa iti kṣīravivardhanam
vaṭādīnāṃ ca vṛkṣāṇāṃ kṣīrikāyāśca valkalam
pākyaḥ kaṣāyaḥ kvathitaḥ kṣīraṃ tena punaḥ śṛtam
pākyaṃ guḍaviḍopetaṃ saghṛtaṃ śālimāśayet
api śuṣkastanīnāṃ tat kṣīropajananaṃ param
śāliṣaṣṭikadarbhāṇāṃ kuśagundretkaṭasya ca
sārivāvīraṇekṣūṇāṃ mūlāni kuśakāśayoḥ
peyāni pūrvakalpena śreṣṭhaṃ kṣīravivardhanam
svabhāvanaṣṭe śuṣke vā duṣṭe sādhvīkṣite hitam
avyāhatabalāṅgāyurarogo vardhate sukham
śiśudhātryoranāpattiḥ śuddhakṣīrasya lakṣaṇam
saṃbhavanti mahārogā aśuddhakṣīrasevanāt
teṣāmevopaśāntistu śuddhakṣīraniṣevaṇāt
tṛṇaṃ kīṭaṃ tuṣaṃ śūkaṃ makṣikāṅgamalāṣṭakam
keśorṇāsthyādikaṃ vidyādvajramityupacārataḥ
sahānnapānena yadā dhātrī vajraṃ samaśnute
pacyamānena pākena hyanannatvānna pacyate
apacyamānaṃ viklinnaṃ vāyunā samudīritam
rasena saha saṃpṛktaṃ yāti stanyavahāḥ sirāḥ
sarvasrotāṃsi hi strīṇāṃ vivṛtāni viśeṣataḥ
tat payodharamāsādya kṣipraṃ vikurute striyāḥ
rūpāṇi pītavajrāyāḥ pravakṣyāmyata uttaram
ajīrṇamaratirglāniranimittaṃ vyathā'ruciḥ
parvabhedo'ṅgamardaśca śirorug kṣavathugrahaḥ
kaphotkledo jvarastṛṣṇā viḍbhedo mūtrasaṃgrahaḥ
stambhaḥ srāvaśca kucayoḥ sirājālena saṃtataḥ
śothaśūlarujādāhaiḥ stanaḥ spraṣṭuṃ na śakyate
stanakīlakamityāhurbhiṣajastaṃ vicakṣaṇāḥ
kīlavat kaṭhino'ṅgeṣu bādhamāno hi tiṣṭhati
eṣa pittātmanā śīghraṃ pākaṃ bhedaṃ ca gacchati
kaphācciraṃ kleśayati vātādāśu nivartate
śākhāśirobhistu yadi vimārgānna prapadyate
akṛṣyamāṇaṃ bālena kṣipraṃ nirdhāvati stanāt
nirduhyamānamutpīḍādvajraṃ sakṣīraśoṇitam
athavā'bhyeti sahasā pratyakṣaṃ copalabhyate
ghṛtapānaṃ prathamataḥ śasyate stanakīlake
srotāṃsi mārdavaṃ snehādyānti vajraṃ ca cyāvyate
nirdoho mardanaṃ yuktyā pāyanaṃ ca galena ca
śītāḥ sekāḥ pralepāśca virekaḥ pathyabhojanam
srāvaṇaṃ cāvidagdhasya doṣadehavyapekṣayā
sya pāṭanaṃ kuryānmṛjāṃ vidradhivacca tat
paravaddhitabhoktrī ca parālālitatarpaṇā
paraveśmaratā dhātrī mucyate stanakīlakāt
darśanīyau stanau pīnau sujātau saṃhatau samau
sukarau paryakīlau ca dṛṣṭvā tvīkṣanti durhṛdaḥ
tato rujāmavāpnoti kāryaṃ tantrāvacāraṇam
iti ha smāha bhagavān kaśyapaḥ
parihṛtyāmamāṃsaṃ tu niśi neyaṃ catuṣpatham
etacchrutvā vacastathyamṛṣipatnyaḥ praharṣitāḥ
praśaśaṃsurmahātmānaṃ kaśyapaṃ lokapūjitam
iti kṣīrotpattirnāmādhyāya ūnaviṃśatitamaḥ 19
viṃśatitamo'dhyāyaḥ
athāto dantajanmikamadhyāyaṃ vyākhyāsyāmaḥ 1
iti ha smāha bhagavān kaśyapaḥ 2
atha khalu bhagavan dehināṃ jātānāmabhivardhamānānāṃ katiṣu māseṣu dantāṃ niṣicyante niṣiktāśca kiyatā kālena mūrtībhavanti mūrtībhūtāśca kadodbhidyante kāni caiṣāṃ pūrvarūpāṇi ke copadravāḥ kaścaiṣāmupakramaḥ kiñca dantajanma praśastamapraśastaṃ ca kiṃ kasmācca svamaṅgamabhivardhamānaṃ prāṇasaṃśayāya bhavati kiyantaśca dantāḥ katicaiṣāṃ dvijāḥ kiyatā ca
kālena patanti patitā vā jāyante dantasaṃpadasaṃpacca kīdṛśīti 3
athovāca bhagavān kaśyapaḥ -- iha khalu nṛṇāṃ dvātriṃśaddantāḥ tatrāṣṭau sakṛjjātāḥ svarūḍhadantā bhavanti ataḥ śeṣā dvijāḥ | yāvatsveva ca māseṣu dantā niṣicyante tāvatsvahaḥ sūdbhidyante | yāvatsveva ca māseṣu jātasya sata udbhidyante tāvatsveva ca varṣeṣu patitāḥ punarudbhidyante | tatra madhye dvāvuttarau rājadantasaṃjñau bhavataḥ tau pavitrau tasmāttābhyāṃ khaṇḍe na śrāddhamarhati apapitro hi saḥ | tayorubhayataḥ pārśvayorapi vastau tayorapi daṃṣṭre śeṣāḥ svarūḍhā hānavyā iti cocyante
tathā'dhastāt 4
tatra kumārīṇāmāśutaramalpābādhakaraṃ ca dantajanma suṣiratvāddaṃśānāṃ mṛdusvabhāvācca prakṛṣṭakālamābādhābahulaṃ tu kumārāṇāmācakṣate ghanatvāddaṃśānāṃ sthirasvabhāvācca | dantānāṃ niṣekamūrtitvodbhedavṛddhipatanapunarbhāvanivṛttisthitiparikṣayacalanapatanadṛḍhadurbalatā jātiviśeṣānniṣekāt svabhāvānmātāpitroranukaraṇāt svakarmaviśeṣāccetyācakṣate
maharṣayaḥ tathā'nye'pi śarīravṛddhihrāsaguṇadoṣaprādurbhāvāḥ 5
nṛṇāṃ tu caturthādiṣu māseṣu dantā niṣicyante | tatra sadantajanma ca pūrvamuttaradantajanma ca viraladantajanma ca hīnadantatā ca adhikadantatā ca karāladantatā ca vivarṇadantatā ca sphuṭitadantatā cāmaṅgalyā bhavati | tatra śāntyarthaṃ mārutīmiṣṭiṃ nirvapet sthālīpākamanāhitāgneḥ prājāpatyamityeke tathā'nyeṣvapi svāṅgonādhikabhāveṣu tathā tadghoraṃ
praśāmyati 6
caturvidhaṃ tu dantajanmācakṣate sāmudraṃ saṃvṛtaṃ vivṛtaṃ dantasaṃpaditi | tatra sāmudgaṃ kṣayi nityasaṃpātāt saṃvṛtamadhanyaṃ maliṣṭhaṃ vivṛtaṃ vītamanityalālopahatamasaṃchannadantatvādāśudantavaivarṇyakaramāsannābādhamiti 7
caturthe tu māsi dantā niṣiktā durbalā bhavantyāśukṣayiṇaścāmayabahulāśca pañcame syandanāśca praharṣiṇaścāmayabasulāśca ṣaṣṭhe pratīpāśca malagrāhiṇaśca vivarṇāśca ghuṇadantāśca bhavanti saptame dvipuṭāḥ sphoṭinaśca rājimantaśca khaṇḍāśca rūkṣāśca viṣamāśconnatāśca bhavanti tathā'ṣṭame māsi sarvaguṇasaṃpannā bhavanti | pūrṇatā samatā ghanatā śuklatā snigdhatā ślakṣṇatā nirmalatā nirāmayatā kiñciduttaronnatatā dantabandhanānāṃ ca samatā raktatā snigdhatā bṛhadvanasthiramūlatā ceti dantasaṃpaducyate | hīnolbaṇasitāsitā'pravibhaktadantabandhanatvamapraśastamṛṣayo vadanti | tat svabhāvāddantodūkhalakeṣu yacchoṇitaṃ garbhe niṣiktaṃ tadeva jātasya
samato'bhivardhamānasya krameṇa ---
ekaviṃśatitamo'dhyāyaḥ
rohiṇī svayaṅguptāmūlaṃ dve haridre bṛhatīphalarasairghṛtārdhavat pacet pacyamāne'pāmārgaṃ cāvapet | siddhena karṇapālīmahanyahanti mrakṣayedvimṛdnīyācca āśu vardhate pīnā samā ca pālī bhavati | madhūcchiṣṭasarjarasayavavatsakairaṇḍānyantardhūmaṃ dagdhvā tena bhasmanā mrakṣitāṃ karṇapā
līṃ vimṛdnīyāt āśu vartate pīnā samā ca pālī bhavatīti
tatra ślokāḥ
nābhiṣagrājaputrāṇāmanyeṣāṃ vā mahātmanām
karṇān vidhyet sukhaprepsuriha loke paratra ca
āmacchede'tyayo hyatra kuvedhādvopajāyate
abhiṣak tatra mandātmā kiṃ kariṣyatyaśāstravit
kadā vedhyaṃ kathaṃ vedhyaṃ kutra vedhyaṃ kathaṃ vyadhaḥ
hito'hito'tyayaḥ kaśca tatrājñaḥ kiṃ prapatsyate
tasmādbhiṣak sukuśalaḥ karṇaṃ vidhyedvicakṣaṇaḥ
śiśorharṣapramattasya dharmakāmārthasiddhaye
iti ha smāha bhagavān kaśyapaḥ
iti cūḍākaraṇīyo'dhyāya ekaviṃśatitamaḥ 21
dvāviṃśatitamo'dhyāyaḥ
athātaḥ snehādhyāyaṃ vyākhyāsyāmaḥ 1
iti ha smāha bhagavān kaśyapaḥ 2
sneho dviyoniruktaścaturvikalpaścarācarasamutthaḥ
sarpirmajjavasākhyaṃ khagamṛgajalajaprabhavamāhuḥ 3
tailāni codbhidebhyastilacūtasarṣapabibhītabilvebhyaḥ
eraṇḍātasiśigrumadhūkamūlakakarañjebhyaḥ 4
ghṛtatailavasāmajjāṃ pūrvaḥ pūrvo varo'nyebhyaḥ
mukhyaṃ ghṛteṣu gavyaṃ saṃskārāt sarvasātmyācca 5
vinihanti pittamanilaṃ pītaṃ sarpiḥ kaphaṃ na ca cinoti
janayati balāgnimedhāḥ śodhayati śukraṃ ca yoniṃ ca 6
uṣṇaṃ kaphānilaghnaṃ svaravarṇakaraṃ tanusthirīkaraṇam
bhagnacyutasandhānaṃ dhātuvraṇaśodhanaṃ tailam 7
majjāvase viśeṣādvātaghne vṛṣyasaṃmate caiva
balināṃ tatsātmyānāṃ prajābalāyuḥ sthirīkaraṇe 8
nityānityātmavidhau tilatailaghṛte budhaḥ prayuñjīta
eraṇḍaśaṅkhinībhyāṃ sraṃsanamanyadrasāyanaṃ nāsti 9
majjāvase vasante prāvṛṣi tailaṃ pibeccharadi sarpiḥ
sarpirvā sarveṣāṃ sarvasmiñcharayate pātum 10
anupānamuṣṇamudakaṃ ghṛtasya tailasya yūṣamicchanti
majjadhasayostu maṇḍaṃ sarveṣāṃ kaśyapaḥ pūrvam 11
śūlakaphānilatṛṣṇāhikkārocakavibandhagulmaghnam
vraṇadhātumṛdūkaraṇaṃ dīpanamuṣṇodakamuśanti 12
pādāvaśeṣasiddhaṃ taddoṣaghnaiḥ śṛtaṃ jalaṃ mukhyam
peyaṃ kavalagrāhyaṃ snehaṃ hi tathā vilāyayati 13
payasi dadhani madhumaye tūkte noṣṇodakaṃ bhavet pathyam
pitte raktasrāve garbhacyavane ca garbhadāhe ca 14
odanavilepikābhyāṃ rasamāṃsakṣīradadhiyavāgūbhiḥ
kāmbalikasūpayūṣaiḥ peyāśanabhakṣyavikṛtībhiḥ 15
snehaprayoga iṣṭaḥ sordhvādhaḥ karmabhiḥ khalābhyaṅgaiḥ
cakṣurvadanaśrotrairdhāraṇayogaśca sātmyajñaiḥ 16
pittānilaprakṛtayaḥ snehaṃ rātrau pibeyuruṣṇe ca
śleṣmādhiko divoṣṇe nirmalasūrye laghutve ca 17
tṛṇmūrcchonmādādīn pūrvo labhate viparyayeṇa piban
ānāhāruciparvaṇyucchūlaṃ ca samṛcchate śeṣaḥ 18
snehācchapāne trividhā tu mātrā
hrasvā'tha madhyā mahatī tṛtīyā |
hrasvā dinārdhena dinena madhyā
jīryatyahorātravaśāt pradhānā 19
dīptāgnayo balinaḥ snehanityā
unmādino dhṛtiviṇmūtrasaktāḥ |
gulmārditāścāhidaṣṭā virūkṣā
vaisarpiṇaḥ pravarāṃ te pibeyuḥ 20
pramehakuṣṭhānilaśoṇitāruci
vicarcikāsphoṭaviṣeṣu kaṇḍau |
mṛdau tathā'gnau pravadanti madhyāṃ
bale ca madhyā aśane ca ye syuḥ 21
bāleṣu vṛddheṣu sukhociteṣu
jīrṇe'tisāre jvarakāsayośca |
yeṣāṃ hi koṣṭho na guṇāya rikto
mandāgnikārśye ca kanīyasī syāt 22
doṣānukarṣiṇyanusāriṇī ca
yatnopacaryā balavardhanī ca |
jyeṣṭhā'tha madhyā na balaṃ nihanti
tvanyonyathoḥ snehayate sukhācca 23
hrasvā parīhārasukhā'vikārā
vṛṣyā'tha balyā'pyanuvartanī ca |
deśaṃ vayaḥkālabalāgnisātmyā
nyālakṣyamātrāṃ matimān vidadhyāt 24
pittānilātmā'nilapittarogī
kṣāmaḥ śiśurvarṇabalāyuraktaḥ |
medhendriyārthī viṣaśastradāhai
rārtāḥ piveyurghṛtameva kāle 25
pravṛddhamedaḥkaphamāṃsavātā
nāḍīkṛmivyādhyanilārtadehāḥ |
krūrānukoṣṭhāstanuvīryakāmā
stailaṃ pibeyurna tu tīvrakuṣṭhe 26
saṃśuṣkamedaḥkapharaktaśukrā
vātātapādhvaśramaraukṣyanityāḥ |
bhṛśāgnayo vātanipīḍitāṅgā
vasāṃ pibeyurdhṛtidhātukāmāḥ 27
dīptāgnayo ghasmarāḥ snehanityāḥ
kleśakṣamāḥ krūrakoṣṭhānilārtāḥ |
majjānameteṣu bhiṣagvidadhyāt
sneho bhavetsātmyato yasya yo vā 28
vyāyāmamadyacintāmaithunanityāḥ śramādhvakṛśadehāḥ
snehyāstathāvidhāḥ syurbalakālavayognisātmyajñaiḥ 29
na snehayedgarbhiṇīṃ na prasūtāṃ
na kṣīrapaṃ naiva dagdhātivṛddhau |
na śleṣmapittopahatāntarāgniṃ
mūrcchāruciglānibhṛśāmatṛṭsu 30
bastau na nastaśca vidhikriyāyāṃ
chardyāṃ jvare viṭprakope kaphe ca |
bṛhattvajāḍyeṣu galāmayeṣu
nasnehayet snehamadātyayeṣu 31
teṣāṃ snehācchapānānte vardhante vyādhayo bhṛśam
asādhyanāṃ vā gacchanti snehapānābhivardhitāḥ 32
vāyorapraguṇatvaṃ raukṣyaṃ kharatā'ghṛtirjvalanahāniḥ
śuṣkaprathitapurīṣaṃ lakṣaṇamasnigdhagātrasya 33
dhṛtirmṛdupurīṣatvaṃ medhāpuṣṭyagnitejasāṃ vṛddhiḥ
kāle śarīravṛttiḥ snigdhasya vadanti liṅgāni 34
gauravajāḍyotkleśādhmānāni purīṣamavipakvam
arucirapi pāṇḍutandre vadantyatisnigdhaliṅgāni 35
dravamitalaghūṣṇapannaṃ kāle sātmyaṃ balāgnirugyuktam
svaḥ snehapānamicchan bhuñjīta śayīta guptaśca 36
uṣṇodakopacārī jitendriyaḥ syānnivātaśayanasthaḥ
vyāyāmavegaroṣatyāgī snehācchapo'svapnaḥ 37
saṃsnihyati mṛdukoṣṭho narastrirātreṇa saptarātreṇa
snehācchapānayogājjīvaka yaḥ krūrakoṣṭhastu 38
drākṣāpīlutriphalāgorasataptāmbutaruṇamadyāni
bhuktvā'tha pāyasaṃ yo mṛdukoṣṭhaḥsraṃsyate nānyaḥ 39
pittabahuletarālpā grahaṇī bhavati mṛdukoṣṭhināṃ tasmāt
suvirecyā mṛdukoṣṭhāḥ prāyaḥ pittaṃ hyadhobhāgi 40
tṛṇmūrchāmukhaśoṣaiḥ śabdadveṣāṅgamardajṛmbhābhiḥ
tandrīvāgdehasādaiḥ snehajñājīryatītyāha 41
jīrṇājīrṇaviśaṅkī kevalamuṣṇodakaṃ pibet taddhi
udgārasya viśuddhiṃ janayati bhaktābhilāṣaṃ ca 42
taile'dhiko vidāhaḥ sarpiṣi mūrcchā vasāsu hṛllāsaḥ
majjani gauravameṣāṃ doṣairalpā pravṛttistu 43
snehājīrṇe tṛṣṇā śūlaṃ parikartikā ca yasya syāt
samatītajaraṇakāle tasya pracchardanaṃ śreyaḥ 44
udgārasya viśuddhiḥ kāṃkṣā sthiratā laghutvamaviṣādaḥ
balavāgindriyasaṃpajjīrṇe snehe balasukhe ca 45
karṇākṣipraṇabalaṃ smṛtikeśaujasāṃ vṛddhidhṛtipuṣṭiḥ
śāntistadvyādhīnāṃ bhuktvā'nu snehapītasya 46
pittānilāmayaghnaṃ bastyūrukaṭīdṛḍhīkaraṃ vṛṣyam
ūrjaskaraṃ śramaghnaṃ vidyāt snehāvapīḍaṃ tu 47
varṇasvaramedhaujaḥśukrāyurdhṛtibalāgnisaṃvṛddhiḥ
viṇmūtrānilavṛttiḥ sukhena saṃbhojanasnehāt 48
jvarapāṇḍukuṣṭhaśothāstṛṇmūrchācchardyarocakotkledāḥ
grahaṇīndriyopaghātastaimityānāhaśūlādyāḥ 49
snehāpacārajāste rogāḥ svedopapāditā yeṣu
vamanavirecanayogā rūkṣāśanatakramūtrādyāḥ 50
mātrākālaviyuktaḥ snehaḥ sātmyopacāraguṇahīnaḥ
yukto vyāpadamṛcchati tasmin saṃśodhanaṃ pathyam 51
snehadveṣī kṣāmo mṛdukoṣṭhaḥ snehamadyanityaśca
adhvaprajāgarastrīśrāntā nācchaṃ pibeyuste 52
teṣāmannairvividhaiḥ snehasya vicāraṇā sātmyam
nirdiṣṭvā māsādyaiḥ kālāgnivayaḥprakarṣācca 53
gurupānabhojyamāṃsairguḍadadhitilaśākadugdhaniryūhaiḥ
na snehayet pramehe na kuṣṭhakaphaśoṣarogārtān 54
taddoṣaghnairdravyaiḥ snehaiḥ siddhairyathāsvamavikāraiḥ
snehyāstathātridhāḥ syustriphalāsavyoṣalavaṇādyaiḥ 55
snehitadehasyādau svedamanantaramatha prayuñjīta
samyaksnigdhasvinnairviśodhanamanantaraṃ kāryam 56
iti snehādhyāyo dvāviṃśatitamaḥ 22
trayoviṃśatitamo'dhyāyaḥ
athātaḥ svedādhyāyaṃ vyākhyāsyāmaḥ 1
iti ha smāha bhagavān kaśyapaḥ 2
samyaksnigdhasya bhagavan kathaṃ svedaṃ prayojayet
anatyayaṃ bhiṣagvāle dravyaṃ svedopagaṃ ca kim 3
mandātisamyaksvinnānāṃ bālānāṃ lakṣaṇaṃ ca kim
kaḥ svedyo na ca kaḥ svedya ityuktaḥ prāha kaśyapaḥ 4
śṛṇu svedavidhiṃ kṛtsnaṃ vṛddhajīvaka tattvataḥ
yathā bāle prayoktavyaḥ prayuktaśca yathā hitaḥ 5
staimityaśūlakāṭhinyavibandhānāhavāggrahaiḥ
hṛllāsārucyalasakaśītāsahanavepanaiḥ 6
vātaśleṣmodbhavaṃ dṛṣṭvā pṛthagvā sveda iṣyate
vāte snigdhaḥ kaphe rūkṣo dvayoḥ sādhāraṇo mataḥ 7
bālānāṃ kṛśamadhyānāṃ sveda āvasthiko hitaḥ
śītavyādhiśarīrāṇāṃ bālānāṃ ca viśeṣataḥ 8
vṛṣaṇau hṛdayaṃ cakṣurmṛdu vā svedayenna vā
śepovaṅkṣaṇasandhīṃstu madhyamaṃ śeṣamiṣṭataḥ 9
kumudotpalapadmānāṃ patrairācchādya locane
vāsasā vā'tha ślakṣṇena bāle svedaṃ prayojayet 10
muktāvalīcandrakāntaśītāmbukarabhājanaiḥ
spṛśedabhīkṣṇaṃ hṛdayaṃ bālasya svedakarmaṇi 11
karpūracūrṇamāsyena dhārayet svidyataḥ sukham
phalāmlayuktaṃ khaṇḍaṃ vā mṛdvīkāṃ vā saśarkarām 12
śītagauravaviṣṭambhaśūlādīnāṃ nivartane
tadviparyayabhāve ca svedaṃ prājño nivartayet 13
viṣādamūrcchātṛḍdāhapittakopāratibhramāḥ
svarāṅgahānirvaihvalyamatisvinnasya lakṣaṇam 14
taccikitsāṃ prayuñjīta yathā vaisarpiṇāṃ tathā
rāgavraṇavisaṃjñābhiḥ kṛcchrasādhyaṃ tamādiśet 15
vātasyāpraguṇatvaṃ ca gurutvaṃ stabdhagātratā
mandasvinne na ca glānistṛṣṇādīnāṃ ca vibhramaḥ 16
tatra svedaṃ prayuñjīta bhiṣagbhūyo vicārayan
balakālavayodoṣān pathyaceṣṭāśanasthitīḥ 17
svedābhinanditā saukhyaṃ mṛdutā rogadehayoḥ
kāle visṛṣṭiḥ kṣuttṛṣṇā samyak svinnasya lakṣaṇam 18
pittātmā pittarogī ca garbhiṇī madhumehinaḥ
kṣuttṛṣṇāśoṣaroṣārtāḥ kāmalyudarivikṣatāḥ 19
kārśyamadyaviṣārtāśca bhṛśāgnitimirasrutāḥ
bhraṣṭabhagnavidagdhāṅgā na svedyāste kathaṃcana 20
svarabhedapratiśyāyagalagrahaśiroruji
manyākarṇaśiraḥśūle gaurave śvāsakāsayoḥ 21
kukṣipārśvakaṭīpṛṣṭhaviḍgrahe mūtrayakṣmaṇi
śukrāghāte pakṣavadhe koṣṭhānāhavibandhayoḥ 22
vināmārditajṛmbhāsu hanamanyāśirograhe
aṅgamarde mahattve ca vepathau vātakaṇṭake 23
śītaśothāmakhalvīṣu pāṇipādāṅgamārute
āyāmākṣepaśūlādau svedaḥ pathyatamo nṛṇām 24
janmaprabhṛti bālānāṃ svedamaṣṭavidhaṃ bhiṣak
prayuñjīta yathākālaṃ rogadehavyapekṣayā 25
hastasvedaḥ pradehaśca nāḍīprastarasaṃkarāḥ
upanāho'vagāhaśca pariṣekastathā'ṣṭamaḥ 26
jātasya caturo māsān hastasvedaṃ prayojayet
apramādī nivātastho vidhūmāgnyūṣmaṇā śanaiḥ 27
nivartamāne bālasya saukumārye yathākramam
pravartamāne kāṭhinye teṣāṃ svedaṃ pravardhayet 28
santi cāpyapare bālāḥ sukumārāḥ sadāsukhāḥ
ghṛtakṣīrāśinaḥ kalyā īśvarāṇāṃ mahātmanām 29
madhyamā madhyamānāṃ ca daridrāṇāṃ ca duḥkhinām
niṣekadeśasātmye ca tān vidyāt paṇḍito bhiṣak 30
aviśeṣeṇa bādhante sarve sarvānnarān gadāḥ
viśeṣastu mahān dṛṣṭo dakṣiṇāhārabheṣaje 31
deśakālavayomātrāsarvaruggurulāghavaiḥ
svedo'tirikto hīno vā hanyādbālaṃ yathā viṣam 32
tasmādavekṣya deśādīn kāṭhinyaṃ sukumāratām
śiśoḥ svedaṃ prayuñjīta yaśodhanyārthasiddhaye 33
galakarṇaśiromanyākarṇākṣicibukorasi
abhiṣyandāt samucchūne pradehasveda iṣyate 34
eraṇḍavṛṣaśigrūṇāṃ tvakpatraiḥ kalkasādhitaiḥ
samūtrabukkalavaṇaiḥ pradehaḥ syāt sukhoṣmabhiḥ 35
śītībhūtaṃ tu nirmṛjya lepayedaparāparam
anekaśastu vijñāya svinnaṃ svedaṃ nivartayet 36
dravyairvātakaphaghnaiśca pradehaḥ śigruvaddhitaḥ
anyairapi karīṣaiśca gokharāśvāvibastajaiḥ 37
vaṃśamuñjanalādyaiśca yathāyogaṃ yathāsukham
nāḍīsvedaṃ prayuñjīta nivāte vastrasaṃvṛtam 38
uṣṇān pulākānāstīrya pāyasaṃ kṛsarādi vā
vāsasāntarite bālamabhyaktaṃ śāyayet sukham 39
pañcāṅguloruvūkārkapatrairvā snehitoṣṇitaiḥ
prastarasvedamityāhurabhīkṣṇaparivartinaḥ 40
pāyasaiḥ kṛśarairmāṃsairodanaistrikaṭhorakaiḥ
uṣṇaiḥ salavaṇasnehairambarāstaritaiḥ sukhaiḥ 41
kiṇvātasīdadhikṣīrasaṃyuktaiḥ piṇḍakaiḥ kṛtaiḥ
sthānasvedanamicchanti saṅkarasveda ucyate 42
kiṇvātasīdadhikṣīralavaṇaiḥ sāmlacikkaṇaiḥ
kuṣṭhādibhiśca sasnehairupanāhaḥ praśasyate 43
kharājāvibiḍālenadvīpisiṃhatarakṣujaiḥ |
--- 44
caturviṃśatitamo'dhyāyaḥ
--- proktaṃ cikitsitam |
ataḥ pañcajanāt kañcitsaṃmyakśuddhaṃ prakāṅkṣitam
laghuṃ viśadasarvāṅgaṃ prasannendriyamicchukam
sukhāmbusiktasarvāṅgamanuliptaṃ vibhūṣitam
kṛtapūjānamaskāraṃ manojñāsanaveśmagam
purāṇaraktaśālīnāṃ maṇḍapūrvāṃ susādhitām
yavāgūṃ triḥsrutāmuṣṇāṃ dīpanīyopasaṃskṛtām
bhojayedyuktalavaṇāṃ rūkṣāṃ yuktāśito bhavet
bhojaneṣu suhṛdyeṣu sudhauteṣvaparāhṇike
śirolalāṭahṛdgrīvāvṛṣaṇe sākṣaśaṅkhake
svedaścet pītamaṇḍasya samyakśuddhaṃ tamādiśet
udgāravātakarmabhyāṃ viśuddhābhyāṃ dine dine
nirupadravapuṣṭibhyāṃ samyakśuddhaṃ vinirdiśet
sukhoṣitaṃ jīrṇabhaktaṃ dvitīye'hani bhojayet
yavāgūṃ tu tṛtīye'hni dadyādasmai vilepikām
dīpanodakasaṃsiddhāṃ rūkṣāmuṣṇāṃ sasaindhavām
caturthe mudgamaṇḍaḥ syādodanaśca susādhitaḥ
purāṇaraktaśālīnāṃ bhṛṣṭānāṃ vā kṛśātmanaḥ
nistuṣāṇāṃ ca mudgānāṃ maṇḍaḥ syāduktaveṣaṇaḥ
īṣatphalāmlaḥ kartavyo mudgamaṇḍo'hni pañcame
īṣatsnehaḥ kṛtaḥ ṣaṣṭhe saptame ca vidhīyate
jāṅgalānāṃ rasaṃ siddhaṃ tanukaṃ māṃsavarjitam
dine'ṣṭame'tha navame dadyāt snehālpasaṃskṛtam
daśamaikādaśe cāhni lavaṇasnehasaṃskṛtaḥ
phalāmlasiddho yuktoṣṇaḥ śasyate rasakaudanaḥ
uṣṇodakānupānau tu syātāṃ vātakaphātmakau
tata uttarakālaṃ tu bhojyasaṃsarga iṣyate
eṣāṃ maṇḍādisaṃsargo sarvavyādhikriyopagaḥ
enaṃ vyabhicaranmohāddāruṇām̐llabhate gadān
jvarāmakāmalāpāṇḍukarṇakuṣṭhagalāmayāḥ
hikkātisāraśvayathukāsādyā vyabhicārajāḥ
śūlātisarau śuddhasya śītapānānnasevanāt
śothodarajvarā amlabhṛśasnehadivāśayāt
kāṃkṣā bubhukṣā vaiśadyaṃ laghutā sthiratā sukham
svasthavṛttānuvṛttiśca samyagjīrṇānnalakṣaṇam
viṣādo gauravaṃ tandrī śleṣmasekāratibhramāḥ
svasthavṛttoparodhaśca tadajīrṇasya lakṣaṇam
āmaṃ vidagdhaṃ saśleṣma rasaśeṣaṃ tathaiva ca
caturvidhamajīrṇaṃ tu tasya vakṣyāmi lakṣaṇam
yathābhuktaṃ bhavedāme dhūmodgārau vidāhini
saśleṣmaṇi gurutvaṃ tu rasaśeṣe tu hṛddravaḥ
tandrīśūlāratiglānitṛḍvidāhārucibhramāḥ
aṅgamardajvarānāhāḥ sarveṣvapyalpaśo gadāḥ
sarvairasādhyatotkṛṣṭaiḥ kramaśo yāpyasādhyate
sādhyānāṃ sādhanaṃ yattu tanme pravadataḥ śṛṇu
āmasyoddharaṇaṃ pathyaṃ vidagdhe prāvṛtaḥ svapet
saśleṣmaṇi bhavet svedaḥ pariśoṣyo rasādhike
yaduktaṃ pathyamaśanaṃ tadevaiteṣu śasyate
dīrghakālauṣadhānāṃ tu mudgamaṇḍaḥ sadāḍimaḥ
sasnehalavaṇavyoṣaḥ peyo māṃsaraso'pi vā
bālamūlakayūṣo vā hitaḥ śālyodanastathā
cikitsitaṃ pañcajanān rājño rājopamasya vā
dhanināṃ nirdhanānāṃ vā yathārthamupakalpayet
balaghnaṃ doṣaśamanaṃ balavarṇasukhāvaham
samyak saṃśodhanaṃ kṛtvā dīrghamāyuravāpnute
iti ha smāha bhagavān kaśyapaḥ
ityupakalpanīyo'dhyāyaścaturviṃśatitamaḥ 24
pañcaviṃśatitamo'dhyāyaḥ
athāto vedanādhyāyaṃ vyākhyāsyāmaḥ 1
iti ha smāha bhagavān kaśyapaḥ 2
upāsyamānamṛṣibhiḥ kaśyapaṃ vṛddhajīvakaḥ
codito dāruvāhena vedanārthe'bhyacodayat 3
bālakānāmavacasāṃ vividhā dehavedanāḥ
prādurbhūtāḥ kathaṃ vaidyo jānīyāllakṣaṇārthataḥ 4
iti pṛṣṭo mahābhāgaḥ kaśyapo lokavṛddhapaḥ
provāca vedanāstasmai kāraṇairbāladehajāḥ 5
bhṛśaṃ śiraḥ spandayati nimīlayati cakṣuṣī
avakūjatyaratimānasvapnaśca śiroruji 6
karṇau spṛśati hastābhyāṃ śiro bhramayate bhṛśam
aratyarocakāsvapnairjānīyāt karṇavedanām 7
lālāsravaṇamatyarthaṃ stanadveṣārativyathāḥ
pītamudgirati kṣīraṃ nāsāśvāsī mukhāmaye 8
pītamudgirati stanyaṃ viṣṭambhiśleṣmasevanam
īṣajjvaro'rucirglāniḥ kaṇṭhavedanayā'rdite 9
lālāsrāvo'rucirglāniḥ kapole śvayathurvyathā
mukhasya vivṛtatvaṃ ca jānīyādadhijihvikām 10
jvarārucimukhasrāvā niṣṭanecca galagrahe
kaṇḍūke śvayathuḥ kaṇṭhe jvarāruciśirorujaḥ 11
muhurnamayate'ṅgāni jṛmbhate kāsate muhuḥ
dhātrīmālīyate'kasmāt stanaṃ nātyabhinandati 12
prasrāvoṣṇatvavaivarṇye lalāṭasyātitaptatā
aruciḥ pādayoḥ śaityaṃ jvare syuḥ pūrvavedanāḥ 13
dehavaivarṇyamaratirmukhaglāniranidratā
vātakarmanivṛttiścetyatīsārāgravedanāḥ 14
stanaṃ vyudasyate rauti cottānaścāvabhajyate
udarastabdhatā śaityaṃ mukhasvedaśca śūlinaḥ 15
animittamabhīkṣṇaṃ yasyodgāraḥ pravartate
nidrājṛmbhāparītasya chadistasyopajāyate 16
niṣṭanatyurasā'tyuṣṇaṃ śvāsastasyopajāyate
akasmānmārutodgāraḥ kṛśe hikkā pravartate 17
stanaṃ pibati cātyarthaṃ na ca tṛṣyati roditi
śuṣkauṣṭhatālustoyepsurdurbalastṛṣṇayā'rditaḥ 18
viśālastabdhanayanaḥ parvabhedāratiklamī
saṃruddhamūtrānilaviṭ śiśurānāhavedanī 19
akasmādaṭṭahasanamapasmārāya kalpate
pralāpārativaicittyairunmādaṃ copalakṣayet 20
romaharṣo'ṅgaharṣaśca mūtrakāle ca vedanā
mūtrakṛcchre daśatyoṣṭhau bastiṃ spṛśati pāṇinā 21
gauravaṃ baddhatā jāḍyamakasmānmūtranirgamaḥ
pramehe makṣikākāntaṃ mūtraṃ śvetaṃ ghanaṃ tathā 22
baddhapakvapurīṣatvaṃ saraktaṃ vā kṛśātmanaḥ
gudaniṣpīḍanaṃ kaṇḍūṃ todaṃ cārśasi lakṣayet 23
saśarkarātimūtratvaṃ mūtrakāle ca vedanā
pratataṃ roditi kṣāmastaṃ brūyādaśmarīgadam 24
raktamaṇḍalakotpattistṛṣṇā dāho jvaro'ratiḥ
svāduśītopaśāyitvaṃ visarpasyāgravedanāḥ 25
dahyante'ṅgāni sūcyante bhajyante niṣṭanatyati
visūcikāyāṃ bālānāṃ hṛdi śūlaṃ ca vardhate 26
śiro na dhārayati yo bhidyate jṛmbhate muhuḥ
stanaṃ pibati nātyarthaṃ grathitaṃ chardayatyapi 27
viṣādādhmānārucibhirvidyādalasakaṃ śiśoḥ
visūcikālasakayordurjñāne lakṣaṇauṣadhe 28
dṛṣṭivyākulatā todaśothaśūlāśraraktatāḥ
suptasya copalipyante cakṣuṣī cakṣurāmaye 29
gharṣatyaṅgāni śayane roditīcchati mardanam
śuṣkakaṇḍva'rditaṃ vidyāttataścārdrā pravartate 30
sukhāyate mṛdyamānaṃ mṛdyamānaṃ ca śūyate
śūnaṃ sravati sasyoḍhā mārdrāyāṃ śūladāhavat 31
staimityamarucirnidrā gātrapāṇḍukatā'ratiḥ
ramaṇāśanaśayyādīn dhātrīṃ ca dveṣṭi nityaśaḥ 32
asnātaḥ snātarūpaśca snātaścāsnātadarśanaḥ
āmasyaitāni rūpāṇi vidyādvaidyo bhaviṣyataḥ 33
nābhyāṃ samantataḥ śothaḥ śvetākṣinakhavakratā
pāṇḍuroge'gnisādaśca śvayathuścākṣikūṭayoḥ 34
pītacakṣurnakhamukhaviṇmūtraḥ kāmalārditaḥ
ubhayatra nirutsāho naṣṭāgnirudhiraspṛhaḥ 35
mūrcchāprajāgaracchardidhātrīdveṣāratibhramaiḥ
vitrāsodvegatṛṣṇābhirvidyādbāle madātyayam 36
muhurmukhenocchvasiti pītvā pītvā stanaṃ tu yaḥ
sravato nāsike cāsya lalāṭaṃ cābhitapyate 37
srotāṃsyabhīkṣṇaṃ spṛśati pīnase kṣauti kāsate
uroghāte tathaiva syānniṣṭanatyurasā'dhikam 38
svasthavṛttaparo bālo na śete tu yadā niśi
raktabinducitāṅgaśca vidyāttaṃ jantukāditam 39
yadā tu lalitā dhātrī sukhinī sarvabhoginī
paśyatyabhīkṣṇaṃ duḥsvapnaṃ svayaṃ kṣīraṃ pravartate 40
bālo vismarate cāsyāḥ sahasāṅkāt patatyapi
asajjanena saṃsargaṃ yāti saṃbhojanaṃ tathā 41
mṛtāpatyāvakīrṇābhiḥ paravṛddhyasahiṣṇubhiḥ
amaṅgalāni ghorāṇi paśyatyācarate'pi ca 42
sevate viparītāni mṛtyuṃ codayate śiśoḥ
supte śiśau nilīyante pakṣiṇo dāruṇodayāḥ 43
viḍālo laṅghayatyenaṃ paradhūmaṃ ca jighrati
parāvatāraṇabaliṃ prekṣate laṅghayatyapi 44
durgandhadehavakratvaṃ nāsikāgre malodbhavaḥ
ahṛdyaraktamālyānāṃ mātāputraniṣevaṇam 45
bhasmāṅgāratuṣādīnāmadhirohaṇasevanam
rodityakasmāttrasati chāyāśīlaviparyayaḥ 46
alpāśito'tiviṇmūtrastvaviṇmūtro viparyaye
bhaviṣyatāṃ nimittāni grahāṇāṃ vedanāśca tāḥ 47
na yaḥ śiro dhārayati kṣipantyaṅgāni durbalaḥ
śvāsādhmānaparītābhyāmantavaccopalakṣyate 48
vinodyamāno bahudhā vinodaṃ nābhinandati
tṛṭpramīlakanidrārtaḥ kūjatyapi kapotavat 49
pīḍyamānasya rūpāṇi jvaracchardyatisāriṣu
vaidyo dṛṣṭvaiva jānīyāt kṛcchraṃ sarvaṃ na sidhyati 50
ityetā vividhāḥ proktā vedanā bāladehajāḥ
prāyodbhavānāṃ rogāṇāṃ kaśyapena maharṣiṇā 51
teṣāṃ cikitsitaṃ svaṃ svamaviruddhaṃ yathākramam
dṛṣṭvā cikitsitasthāne doṣataścābhyupakramet 52
iti ha smāha bhagavān kaśyapaḥ
iti vedanādhyāyaḥ pañcaviṃśatitamaḥ 25
ṣaḍviṃśatitamo'dhyāyaḥ
athātaścikitsāsaṃpadīyamadhyāyaṃ vyākhyāsyāmaḥ 1
iti ha smāha bhagavān kaśyapaḥ 2
cikitsāsaṃpadyathopapadyate tamupāyamanuvyākhyāsyāmaḥ | catvāraḥ khalu pādāścikitsitasyopapadyante | te yadā guṇavanta upapadyante tadā sādhyo vyādhirnātivartate | tadyathābhiṣak bheṣajamāturaḥ paricāraka iti 3
tatra bhiṣak sutīrtho nyāyenārṣajñānaprāpto vijñānavānanekaśo dṛṣṭakarmā viditasiddhayogo dakṣo dakṣiṇaḥ śuciranuddhataveṣaḥ sarvabhūteṣu bandhubhūtaḥ siddhimān dharmārthadarśī satyadayādānārjavanirato devadvijagurusiddhānāṃ pūjayitā cābhigantā cottarottarapratipattikuśalo guruvṛddhasevī nyāyābhiniveśī vyapagatabhayalobhamohakrodhānṛto'paiśunyo'madyalaulyaḥ sumu
khaścāvyasanī ceti 4
tatra bheṣajasaṃpat -- subhūmau jātaṃ kāle coddhṛtaṃ kāle cotpannam avikāri agnitoyajantuviṇmūtrajarādibhiranupahataṃ tattadrogayogyaṃ krameṇa
vidhivadupapāditamiti 5
tatrāturasaṃpat -- sādhyarogatā sattvabalabuddhiśarīrendriyadhṛtitejasāṃ dārḍhyaṃ nidānapūrvarūpātaṅkopadravayātropaśayānupaśayānāṃ yathāvadākhyānaṃ dhātryā vā śraddadhānatā devadvijagurubhiṣagbheṣajasuhṛdāmabhi
nandanamāstikyaṃ vinayapradhānatā yathoktakāritvaṃ vaśitvaṃ ceti 6
tatra paricārakasaṃpat -- vipakvakaṣāyatā ārogyaṃ śaktiḥ bhartṛbhaktiḥ upacārajñatā dākṣyaṃ śaucam āśukāritvaṃ sarvakarmasu kauśalam aghṛṇitvam akṣudraputratvamadvaividhyaṃ damo jitakrodhāditā sahiṣṇutā
ceti 7
tatra ślokāḥ
asya pādacatuṣkasya manyante śreṣṭhamāturam
tadarthaṃ guṇavanto hi trayaḥ pādā ihepsitāḥ 8
neti prajāpatiḥ prāha bhiṣaṅmūlaṃ cikitsitam
bhiṣagvaśe trivargo hi siddhiśca bhiṣaji sthitā 9
sa yunakti prayuṅkte ca śāsti ca jñānacakṣuṣā
tasmājjñāne savijñāne yuktaḥ śreṣṭhatamo bhiṣak 10
yadā caturṇāṃ pādānāṃ saṃpadbhavati jīvaka
tadā dharmārthayaśasāṃ vaidyo bhavati bhājanam 11
iti ha smāha bhagavān kaśyapaḥ
iti cikitsāsaṃpadīyo'dhyāyaḥ ṣaḍviṃśatitamaḥ 26
saptaviṃśatitamo'dhyāyaḥ
athāto rogādhyāyaṃ vyākhyāsyāmaḥ 1
iti ha smāha bhagavān kaśyapaḥ 2
eko rogo rujākaraṇasāmānyāditi bhārgavaḥ pramatiḥ dvau rogau nijaścāgantuśceti vāryovidaḥ trayo rogāḥ sādhyayāpyāsādhyā iti kāṅkāyanaḥ catvāro rogā āgantuvātapittakaphajā iti kṛṣṇo bhāradvājaḥ pañca rogā āgantuvātapittakaphatridoṣajā iti dāruvāho rājarṣiḥ ṣaḍrogāḥ ṣaḍrasatvādannapānasyetyṛṣiṣadbhyaḥ sapta rogā vātādyekaikadvitridoṣajā iti hiraṇyākṣaḥ aṣṭau rogā vātādyekaikadvitridoṣā
gantunimittā iti vaideho nimiḥ aparisaṅkhyeyāḥ samahīnādhikadoṣabhedāditi vṛddhajīvakaḥ evamanavasthānamupalabhyāha bhagavān ka
śyapo dvāveva khalu rogau nijaścāgantuśca tāvanekavistarāviti 3
hetuprakṛtyadhiṣṭhānavikalpāyatanārthataḥ
jñeyā rogā asaṅkhyeyāścikitsānāṃ ca vistarāt 4
adhiṣṭhānadvayaṃ teṣāṃ śarīraṃ mana eva ca
mānasānāṃ ca rogāṇāṃ kuryācchārīravat kriyām 5
dhātusthūṇātmavaiṣamyaṃ tadduḥkhaṃ vyādhisaṃjñakam
dhātusthūṇātmasāmyaṃ tu tatsukhaṃ prakṛtiśca sā 6
avyāhataśarīrāyurabhivardheta vā katham
ityarthaṃ bheṣajaṃ proktaṃ vikārāṇāṃ ca śāntaye 7
nijāgantunimittā ca dvividhā prakṛtī rujām
nakhadantāgnipānīyavadhabandhādhidevatāḥ 8
śāpābhicārādāganturnijā vātādihetavaḥ
vātapittakaphānāṃ tu dehe sthānāni me śṛṇu 9
sarvagānāmapi satāṃ prāyaḥ sthānaṃ ca karma ca
adhonābhyasthimajjānau vātasthānaṃ pracakṣate 10
pittasyāmāśayaḥ svedo raktaṃ saha lasīkayā
medaḥ śira uro grīvā sandhirbāhuḥ kaphāśrayaḥ 11
hṛdayaṃ tu viśeṣeṇa śleṣmaṇaḥ sthānamucyate

āganturbādhate pūrvaṃ paścāddoṣān prapadyate

tasmādāganturogāṇāmiṣyate nijavat kriyā
nijānāṃ pūrvarūpāṇi dṛṣṭvā saṃśodhanaṃ hitam 14

madhurasnigdhaśītāni laghūni ca hitāni ca

āviṣkṛtatamān vyādhīn yathāsthūlān pracakṣmahe 18
aśītirvātikā rogāścatvāriṃśattu paittikāḥ
viṃśatiḥ kaphajāḥ proktā vātarogānnibodha me 19

gṛdhrasīpiṇḍikodveṣṭau jānuviśleṣabhedakau

oṣaḥ ploṣo bhramo dāho vamathurdhūmakāmlakau

tvagdāhaḥ śoṇitakledo māṃsakledo'ṅgaśīryaṇam

raktapittaṃ ca koṭhāśca kakṣyā hāridranīlake

iti pradhānāḥ pittārtyaḥ svaṃ rūpaṃ tasya vakṣyate

śvetairadhanāḥ alekhaiḥ parakarmakarāḥ bahulekhai rogiṇaḥ suvṛttaślakṣṇapārṣṇibhiḥ sarvaguṇopapannā bhavanti hīnapārṣṇibhiranāyuṣaḥ prajāhīnāḥ cipiṭāḥ pāradārikāḥ | aṅgulīnakhapādairdīrghairdīrghāyuṣo hrasvairhrasvāyuṣaḥ | aṅgulībhirghanābhirbhāgyavanto gūḍhaparvābhirbhoginaḥ sthūlaparvābhirācāryāḥ lomaśābhiradhanāḥ | kharaparuṣatanuviṣamasphuṭitamaṃlinā pāṣṇira praśastā | uttarapādamunnatamasiramalomakaṃ praśasyate viṣamaṃ viparīta ca taskarāṇām | gulphau gūḍhāvalpāvalomasirau praśasyete dhananāśāyolvaṇau vipulau parikleśāya | prajaṅghā tanvī praśasyate sthūlā patiputradravyasukhakṣayakarī stenāya ca | jaṅghe cānudbaddhe asire alomike praśasyete śuṣkasthūlasirālomaśe viparīte vaidhavyakaryau tu nārīṇām | jānunī ca gūḍhe dhanye | ūrū māṃsopacitau gūḍhasirau ślakṣṇau praśasyete | sphicau nirvṛttāvalambau nirvraṇābalomaśāvaviṣamau praśasyete śuṣkāvanapatyānāṃ lambau pradhānanāśāya mahāntau pauṃścalyāya alpakau śīlavatām | kukundarau gambhīrāvalomaśau pravibhaktau samau praśasyete lomaśau pravrajyāyai pradakṣiṇāvartau tu dhanyau vipulau dīrghāyuṣāṃ śliṣṭāvanāyuṣām | jaghanamurasā tulyaṃ praśasyata ityeke | kumārāṇāmurastu viśālataraṃ jaghanaṃ tu kumārīṇāṃ na tu madhyāya kalpate | vṛṣaṇau pralambau bṛhatau gaurasya kṛṣṇau kṛṣṇasya gaurau raktasya śyāmau śyāmasya raktau lomaśau madhyau smṛtau pīnau praśasyete viparītau daurbhāgyapustvaprajāhānikarau svalpāvanāyuṣāṃ duḥkhāya caike gokharahayājāvikākṛtī tu subhagānāmāyuṣmatāṃ ca vijñeyau | prajananaṃ mṛdu dīrghamucchritaṃ bṛhattāmranirvṛttamaṇi mahākośaṃ mahāsrotaḥ praśasyate tanu hrasvaṃ lambi vikośaṃ śvetaśyāvavisṛtaṃ vāmāvṛttamapraśastam | mūtramanāviddhamatanukamanalpamṛjuvegaṃ praśasyate tadviparītamatigandhi savedanamatyuṣṇaṃ vivarṇamanimittakālamaśabdamapraśastaṃ kanyakānāṃ ca sphālitamūtratvamubhayorvā'napatyakaram | yoniḥ śakaṭākṛtirapatyalābhāya pīnā saubhāgyāya lambā'patyavadhāya maṇḍalā vyabhicaraṇāya utkṣiptā'napatyatvāya sūcīmukhī daurbhāgyāya bhṛśavivṛtasaṃvṛtaśuṣkā lambā viṣamā viliṅgā kleśalābhāya madhyanibiḍā kanyāprajananāya unnatā ramaṇīyā māṃsalā putrajanmane vyañjanavatī ca dhanyā atilomaśā vaidhavyakarī vyañjanahīnā tvayaśase piplumadvasāvatī vyabhicārapravrajyāyai | tathaiva lomarājyubhayato madhyamāgatā nātighanā praśasyate vaidhavyāyātisthūlā atisthūlaghanalomā pauṃścalyāya adhojātā daurbhāgyāya nābhimativṛttā madhyatvāya | kukṣī samunnatau praśasyete lomaśau pravrajyāyai sirālau kubhojanāya nimnau dāridryāya samau madhyatvāya dakṣiṇonnatau putrajanmane vāmonnatau viparītau | īṣadunnatamudaramaśithilamakaṭhinamavipulaṃ praśasyate dāridryāya śuṣkam unnataṃ bhogāya viśālaviṣamaṃ viṣamaśīlabhogāya kalpate bhṛśaśuṣkamanapatyaṃ striyāścādhastādupacitamasiramativipulamavalikamanāyuṣe madhyaṃ nābherupariṣṭādanāyuṣe ekavalikaṃ dhanyaṃ dvivalikaṃ buddhilābhāya trivalikaṃ saubhāgyāya caturvalikaṃ prajāyuṣe bahuvalikamadhanyamanāyuṣe bhavatyudaram | nābhiḥ gambhīrā pradakṣiṇā vṛttotsaṅginī lomasirāvartavarjitā praśasyate gartākṛtiranunnatā sukhaduḥkhakarī viṣamennatā'nāyuṣyā svalpākṛtiranapatyā videśasthā pravrājayati bṛhatī gambhīronnatā''dhipatyāya | nābhyā pāyurvyākhyātaḥ | pārśve vṛtte māṃsale snigdhe alomasire praśasyete lomasire pravrājayete | pṛṣṭhaṃ samamupariviśālamasiramalomakamanāvartakaṃ praśasyate madhye nimnamāyuṣmatāṃ nirbhugnaṃ duḥkhabhāgināṃ saṃkṣiptamanāyuṣāṃ lomaśamamaitrāṇāmalpāpatyānāṃ ca | lomaskandho vaṇigbhārajīvī kitavo raṅgajīvī vā śuṣkāṃso daridraḥ tāvubhau dīrghāyuṣau kadācit pravrajetāmapi snigdhāṃsaḥ karṣakaḥ pīnāṃsaḥ āḍhyaḥ kaṭhināṃsaḥ śūraḥ śithilāṃso'saktaḥ unnatāṃsaḥ pumān praśasyate bhraṣṭāṃsā kanyā viparīte tadguṇahāniḥ | kakṣāvunnatau pṛthulau pīnau suvyañjanau praśasyete viparītāvadhanyau bhṛśalomaśau ca nārīṇām | tathā bāhū ānupūrvyopacitau gūḍhāratnī dīrghau jānuspṛśau praśasyete sirātatāvāyuṣmatāṃ pakṣmavantau prajāvatām asirāvaprajānāṃ tiryaksirau kṛcchrajīvināṃ tilavantau | pravrājayataḥ maśakalakṣaṇavantau kalahāya | maṇibandhane sthūle puṃsaḥ praśasyete tanū striyaḥ | ubhayoreva tisro yavapaṅktayo'cchinnāḥ praśasyante prathamā dhanyā dvitīyā mukhyā tṛtīyā prajāyuṣe sarvāścedavicchinnāḥ snigdhā vyaktagambhīralikhitā ādhipatyāya catasro rājarṣeḥ pañca ṣaṭ śataputrasya sapta devanikāyānāmekā'pi
cedavicchinnā vyaktā sukhāyopapadyate | --
strīṇāṃ cātidīrghāścātihrasvāśca ninditāḥ | keśabhūmiḥ snigdhā lohitā nirmalā nirvraṇā ca praśasyate || mattagajavṛṣabhasiṃhaśārdūlahaṃsagatayo'dhipatayaḥ stimitagatayo dhanyāḥ capalagatayaścapalasukhaduḥkhalābhinaḥ tiryaggatayastvadhanyāḥ skhalanāścāṅgavisphoṭinaścāpraśastāḥ | tathā atigauramatikṛṣṇamatidīrghamatihrasvamatikṛśamatisthūlamatilomaśamalomaśamatimṛdvatikaṭhinaṃ ca śarīreṣvapraśastamucyate | tathā bālānāṃ ruṣitaruditasvapnaprajāgarakrodhaharṣavisargādā
napaṅktisthairyagāmbhīryāṇi yuktāni guṇādhikāni praśasyanta iti 6
atra ślokāḥ --
yathā vaktraṃ tathā vṛttaṃ yathā cakṣustathā manaḥ
yathā svarastathā sāro yathā rūpaṃ tathā guṇāḥ
trividhaṃ sattvamuddiṣṭa kalyāṇakrodhamohajam
śreṣṭhamadhyādhamatvaṃ ca teṣāṃ proktaṃ yathākramam
aṣṭa sapta tridhā caiṣāṃ kramādbhedaḥ pravakṣyate
sattvānāṃ sattvavijñānaṃ hitamauṣadhakalpane
tapaḥsatyadayāśaucadānaśīlarataṃ samam
jñānavijñānasaṃpannaṃ brāhmaṃ vidyājjitendriyam
prajāvantaṃ kriyāvantaṃ dharmaśīlaṃ jagatpriyam
anīrṣyamaśaṭhaṃ prājñaḥ prājāpatyaṃ vadecchucim
śaucavratejyādhyayanabrahmacaryadayāparam
jitamānamadakrodhaṃ vaktāraṃ cāṣamādiśet
trivarganityaṃ vidvāṃsaṃ śūramakliṣṭakāriṇam
prāhuraindraṃ mahābhāgamadhiṣṭhātāramīśvaram
tyaktadambhabhayakrodhaṃ prāptakāriṇamīśvaram
samaṃ mitre ca śatrau ca yāmyaṃ vidyāt suniścitam
aśuciviśuciḥ śūraḥ śīghrakrodhaprasādavān
puṇyaśīlo mahābhāgo vāruṇo varuṇapriyaḥ
sthānamānaparīcāradharmakāmārthalobhinam
krodhaprasādaphaladaṃ kauberaṃ prāhurūjitam
ślokākhyānetihāsajñaṃ gandhamālyāmbarapriyam
nṛttagītopahāsajña gāndharvaṃ subhagaṃ viduḥ
ye cānye'pi śubhā bhāvāḥ śuddhāste cāpi sāttvikāḥ
etat kalyāṇabhūyiṣṭhaṃ śuddhaṃ sattvamihāṣṭadhā
ārogyaṃ praśamo rūpaṃ jñānavijñānamāryatā
dīrghamāyuḥ sukhātyaktaṃ sāmānyaṃ śuddhalakṣaṇam
īśvaro'sūyakaścaṇḍa ātmapūjopadhipriyaḥ
sānukrośabhayo raudro hantā śūrastathā''suraḥ
krūracchidraprahārī ca roṣerṣyāmarṣasantataḥ
vairamāṃsāśanāyāsaḥ kalahārthī ca rākṣasaḥ
śucidviḍaśuciḥ krūro'bhīrurbhīṣayitā''vilaḥ
madyamāṃsapriyaḥ śaṅkī paiśāco bahubhojanaḥ
tīkṣṇamāyāsabahulaṃ nidrāluṃ bahuvairiṇam
akruddhabhīruṃ straiṇaṃ ca sārpaṃ nityauṣṭhalehinam
dānaśayyātyalaṅkārapānabhojanamaithunaiḥ
nityopetaṃ pramuditaṃ yākṣaṃ vidyāt prabhakṣaṇam
ahaṅkṛtā mahāhārā vairiṇo vikṛtānanāḥ
virūpā vikṛtātmāno bhūtasattvā niśāpriyāḥ
amaṣikutsitāhāravāgdyūnaṃ nityaśaṅkitam
calaṃ durmedhasaṃ bhīruṃ śākunaṃ viddhyanokasam
ityetadrājasaṃ sattvaṃ saptadhā krodhakāritam
vyāmiśraguṇadoṣaṃ ca raja evopalakṣayet
āhāramaithunaparaṃ svapnaśīlamamedhasam
athaivaṃ pāśavaṃ vidyānmṛjā'laṅkāravarjitam
bhīrumaprajñamādyūnaṃ kāmakrodhavaśaṃ gatam
hiṃsramātmaparaṃ vidyānmātsyaṃ suprajasaṃ śaṭham
vadhabandhaparikleśaśītavātātapakṣamam
buddhyaṅgahīnamalasaṃ vānaspatyaṃ vadedṛjum
ityetattrividhaṃ sattvaṃ tāmasaṃ mohasaṃbhavam
yaccāmedhyamakalyāṇaṃ sarvaṃ taccāpi tāmasam
sattvaṃ prakāśakaṃ viddhi rajaścāpi pravartakam
tamo niyāmakaṃ proktamanyonyamithunapriyam
yadā yaccādhikaṃ yasya sa dehī tena bhāvitaḥ
śubhāśubhānyācarati phalaṃ bhuṅkte tathāvidham
samānasattvā bālānāṃ tasmāddhātrī praśasyate
udvegavitrāsakarī viparītā na śasyate
na jīvantyatha jīvanti kṛcchrā dhātrīviparyaye
samāna ttvā bālānāṃ puṣṭirāyurbalaṃ sukham
tvagasṛṅmāṃsamedo'sthimajjaśukrāṇi dhātavaḥ
ojaḥ sattvaṃ ca sarvaṃ ca tatsāraṃ tu nibodha me
tvagrogarahito bhogī prasannavyañjanacchaviḥ
sadyaḥkṣataprarohaśca tvaksāraḥ sutanūruhaḥ
raktasāro'ruṇābhāsaḥ ---
sūtrasthānasyaitāvāneva bhāga upalabdhaḥ
<2>
tṛtīyaṃ vimānasthānam
---pṛthak pajā hitāśanam |
tithinakṣatradevārcā ghnantyavekṣitajān gadān
iti ha smāha bhagavān kaśyapaḥ
iti karṇāyajayāvaṣṭhīvanaṃ vimānam
śiṣyopakramaṇīyavimānādhyāyaḥ
athātaḥ śiṣyopakramaṇīyaṃ vimānamadhyāyaṃ vyākhyāsyāmaḥ 1
iti ha smāha bhagavān kaśyapaḥ 2
atha khalu guruḥ śiṣyamabhigataṃ vidyāthinaṃ śiṣyaguṇānvitaṃ vidhinopanayedudagayane puṇyāhe nakṣatre'śvayuji rohiṇyāmuttarāsvanyasmin vā | puṇye prāgudakpravaṇadeśe gomayenādbhiśca gocarmamātraṃ sthaṇḍilamupalipya yathoktaṃ tatra lakṣaṇollekhanāgnipraṇayanaparisamūhanaparyukṣaṇabrahmapraṇītāstaraṇājyotpavanāghārājyabhāgāgnihomān kṛtvā pālāśīḥ samidho ghṛtāktā juhoti--agnaye svāhā somāya svāhā prajāpataye svāhā kaśyapāya svāhā aśvibhyāṃ svāhā indrāya svāhā dhanvantaraye svāhā sarasvatyai svāhā pūrṇabhagāya svāhā agnaye sviṣṭakṛte svāhā iti hutvā brāhmaṇaṃ haviṣyaudanena dakṣiṇāvatā tarpayitvā devāṃśca balibhiḥ gurave pūrṇakumbhaṃ dakṣiṇāṃ dattvā dadhikrāvṇa iti prāṅmukho dadhi prāśya upaspṛśyādbhiḥ parikramya pradakṣiṇaṃ gurorbāhuṃ saṃspṛsya brūyāt asāvahaṃ
putra iti pādau saṃspṛśya brūyāt--asāvahaṃ śiṣya iti 3
atha śiṣyaguṇāḥ -- kṣāntirdākṣyaṃ dākṣiṇyamānukūlyaṃśaucaṃ kule
janma dharmasatyāhiṃsāsāmakalyāṇajñānavijñānasthitiviniveśaḥ pāṭavaṃ yathoktakāritvaṃ brahmacaryamanutseko lobherṣyāvivarjanamiti ato'anyathā doṣaiḥ savarjyaḥ 4
atha guruḥ--dharmajñānavijñānohāpohapratipattikuśaloguṇasaṃpannaḥ saumyadarśanaḥ śuciḥ śiṣyahitadarśī copadeṣṭā ca bhiṣakaśāstravyākhyānakuśalastīrthāgatajñānavijñānaḥ kalyo'nanyakarmā'vyāvṛttaḥ śiṣya guṇānvitaśca | ato'nyathā dauṣairvarjyaḥ 5
atha śiṣyānuśāsanaṃ--bhoḥ saumyenānukūlena dhārmikeṇa jitendriyeṇāhūtādhyāyinā ca bhavitavyaṃ sarvanivedinā samānaduḥkhena deśakālajñena dhṛtimatā ca bhavitavyaṃ lobhakrodhamoherṣyāprahāsavairamadyamāṃsastrībhyo nivarttayitavyaṃ guruśuśrūṣā'vaśeṣeṇādhyetavyaṃ na
cānanujñātena na cānabhyarcya vā gurumasamāptavidyena vā pracaritavyam 6
athādhyayanavidhiḥ -- guruḥ śuciruddhatahastaḥ śucau deśe tadvacchiṣyāyāvahitāyāthaśabdamoṅkāraṃ vā pūrva prayujya mahāvyāhṛtīranūcya sāvitrīṃ ca trirabhyasyā'dhīṣva bho ityukte rūpamekaṃ nigadet taṃ cānupaṭhet tacchiṣyo rūpahataṃ saṃsthāhataṃ ca kuryāt grahaṇaśaktyavekṣaḥ khaṇḍanasaṃdarśanāpūrvagrahaṇāni soḍhuṃ yathoktaśravaṇaṃ tasyābhyāso dhanyaḥ dhāraṇādhyāpanenārthatattvādhigamanaṃ tu mokṣāya | nānadhyāyeṣvadhīyīta na guruvyalīkeṣu na parvasu na sandhyāyāṃ na vidyadulkānabhravarṣā'sūryadarśaneṣu na mahotsave na bhuktavān nādbhutadarśane na gobrāhmaṇaguruparātmapīḍāyāṃ na pakṣiṇīṣu nāpyaṣṭakāsu nātyuccanācaplataklībasvaraiḥ nāmukhādguroḥ nālakṣitaṃ na saṃdigdhaṃ na ca kṣutpipāsāvyādhivaimanasyādiyukto'bhyaset 7
adhītyānujñātaḥ pracarecchuklavāsāḥ saṃhatakeśo'nudbhrānto yugamātrāvalokī pūrvābhibhāṣī sumukhaḥ | na cāturakulamanāhūtaḥ praviśet praviśaṃśca nimittāni lakṣayet | na ca sarvato'valokayedanyatrāturāt | na cāturakuleṣu strībhiḥ preṣyābhirapi mahopahāsaṃ gacchet na cāsāmapūjāpuraskṛtaṃ nāma gṛhṇīyāt mānyasthānenaiva tu brūyāt na ca tābhiḥ saṃvyavahāramatipraṇayaṃ vā kuryāt na ca bharturaviditaṃ strībhyaḥ kiñcidādadyāt na cāviditaḥ pradiśet na ca rahasi striyā saha brūyādāsīta vā na caināṃ vivṛtāṃ prekṣeta vihasedvā praṇayantīṃ copekṣeta na ca prakāśayet | na cāturakulaguhyaṃ bahiḥ prakāśayet nāturakuladoṣān prathayet | dṛṣṭāriṣṭamapi cāturaṃ na tattvaṃ brūyāt nityamāśvāsayet | na mṛtyuparigataśarīramasādhyarogamanupakaraṇaṃ copagacchet nauṣadhamakrameṇopadiśet na parādhīnaṃ kuryāt | na svayaṃ kṛtakamauṣadhaṃ prayuñjīta śarīrauṣadhavyādhivayasāṃ cāvasthāntarajñaḥ syāt | nityasaṃbhṛtadhūpāñjanauṣadhaḥ syāt | na cānyabhiṣagbhirvirodhaṃ gacchet | sayuktaśca tairauṣadhaṃ prakalpayet | pragalbho niḥśaṅka upasthitapade vispaṣṭaṃ vicitraṃ mṛdūpanayavadgrāhakamaviruddhaṃ dharmyaṃ sadā brūyāt | prajānāṃ hi svastikāmo
bhiṣagiha cāmutra ca nandata iti 8
athānyo bhiṣagabhiṣadettasmai kṣameta sāmnā cānunayet | punaḥ punaḥ kutsayantaṃ tu vigṛhyādito granthenā'vakiret na cāsya vākyāvakāśaṃ dadyāt | bruvato'pi proktaṃ ca brūyāt naitadevamiti | parihaset apaśabdāṃścāsya vigṛhṇīyāt arthe kṛcchre cainamavatārayet na cainamavaśaḥ
paruṣayet stotragarbhairevainaṃ dharṣayediti 9
bho bhiṣak āyuḥ kiṃ kimāyurvedasyāyurvedatvaṃ kiṃ cāyurityucyate katyaṅgaścāyurvedaḥ kathaṃ cādhyeyaḥ kimarthaṃ cādhyeya kiñcāsyādyaṃ tantraṃ kaścaiṣāṃ dhuryaḥ katamaṃ ca vedaṃ śrayati kiṃ nityo'nityaḥ kimāśrayaścāyurvedaḥ kāni caiṣāṃ su lakṣaṇāni tatprakṛtīnāṃ tisṛṇāṃ ca vedanānāmatītavarttamānānāgatānāṃ katamāṃ bhiṣak cikitsati kiṃ cāsyāyurvedasādhanaṃ kiṃ puṇyo'puṇyaḥ iti pṛṣṭo vā pratibrūyāt -- bhoḥ tatrāyurjīvitamityucyate || vida jñāne dhātuḥ vidḷ lābhe ca āyuranena jñānena vidyate jñāyate vindate labhyate na riṣyatītyāyurvedaḥ katyaṅgaścāyurveda iti aṣṭāṅgaḥ tasya kaumārabhṛtyaṃ kāyacikitsā śalyāhartṛkaṃ śālākyaṃ viṣatantraṃ bhūtatantramagadatantraṃ rasāyanatantramiti || atrāha--aṅgānyetāni śarīramasya katamat yadāśrayantyaṅgāni aṅgāni hi śarīrāśrayāṇi bhavanti atrāha tasya śarīraṃ dharmaḥ dharmāśrayaṃ hyasmin karma sidhyatīti || kathaṃ cotpanna iti āhaatharvavedopaniṣatsu prāgutpannaḥ svayaṃbhūrbrahmā prajāḥ sisṛkṣuḥ prajānāṃ paripālanārthamāyurvedamevāgre'sṛjat sarvaṃvit tato viśvāni bhūtāni | tatastaṃ puṇyamāyurvedamanantamāyuṣo vardhanamādhāramāpyāyanamamṛtamaśvibhyāṃ kaḥ pradadau tāvindrāya indra ṛṣibhyaścaturbhyaḥ kaśyapavaśiṣṭhātribhṛgubhyaḥ te putrebhyaḥ śiṣyebhyaśca pradadurhitārthaṃ dharmārthakāmamokṣaśaktiparipālanārthaṃ ceti evamutpannaḥ || kathaṃ cādhyeya iti guroranumateneti || kena cādhyeya iti brāhmaṇakṣatriyavaiśyaśūdrairāyurvedo'dhyeyaḥ || tatrārthaparijñānārthaṃ puṇyārthaṃ cātmanaḥ prajānugrahārthaṃ brāhmaṇaiḥ prajāsaṃrakṣaṇārthaṃ kṣatriyaiḥ vṛttyarthaṃ vaiśyaiḥ śuśrūṣārthamitaraiḥ dharmārthaṃ ca sarvaiḥ | sukhajīvitadānaṃ hi sarvadharmasyādhikaṃ bruvate tataśca puṇya evamāyurvedaḥ | sukhajīvitadānatuṣṭāśca dehinaḥ kṛtajñāya saṃvibhajanti puraḥstuvanti ca tadasya dharmārthakāmanirvartakaṃ bhavatīti kimarthaṃ cādhyeya ityatroktam || kiṃcāsyādyaṃ
tantramiti
kaumārabhṛtyamaṣṭānāṃ tantrāṇāmādyamucyate
āyurvedasya mahato devānāmiva havyapaḥ
anena hi saṃvardhitamitare cikitsanti | bālasya hṛdyamauṣadhamanyat pramāṇamanya upakramo'nye ca viśeṣāḥ || kaṃ ca vedaṃ śrayati atharvavedamityāha tatra hi rakṣābalihomaśānti --- pratikarmavidhānamuddiṣṭaṃ viśeṣeṇa tadvadāyurvede tasmādatharvavedaṃ śrayati | sarvān vedānityeke padyagadyakathyageyavidyāśrayāditi na caitadevamāyurvedamevāśrayante vedāḥ | tadyathā--dakṣiṇe pāṇau catasṛṇāmaṅgulīnāmaṅguṣṭha ādhipatyaṃ kurute na ca nāma tābhiḥ saha samatāṃ gacchati ekasmiṃśca pāṇau bhavati evamevāyamṛgvedayajurvedasāmavedātharvavedebhyaḥ pañcamo bhavatyāyurveda iti | kiṃ kāraṇaṃ yathāhi vedeṣu satata brahmajñaistrivargasaṃyuktaṃ puruṣaniśreyasaṃ cintyate evamevāsminnapi vede nidānotpattiliṅgāriṣṭacikitsitaiḥ satatameva hitasukhakaraṃ trivargasārabhūtaṃ puruṣani śreyasaṃ cintyate tadyathā ca vividhavijñānajñānopapannā bhāṣyavacanavido'ṣṭāṅgayā buddhyopapannā laṅghanaplavanasthānāsanagamanāgamanasamarthā api ca nāma manuṣyā adeśajñānavanto nityameva deśajñaṃ daiśikamanvayurevameva khalu vedanāsu śikṣākalpasūtraniruktavṛttacchandoyajñasaṃstarajñānasamuccayaviśeṣajñā āyurvedamevānudhāvanti tasmādbrūmaḥ ṛgvedayajurvedasāmavedātharvavedebhyaḥ pañcamo'yamāyurvedaḥ | yataśca vyādhitasyārogyamarogasya ca śeṣāḥ kriyā dharmārthakāmamokṣeṣu nirvartante || kiṃ nityo'nitya iti kutaḥ ārṣavacanaprāmāṇyādavināśitvāt sādhyāsiddherdeśakālasāmānyāditi || kimāśraya iti vātapittakaphāśrayaḥ | te ca dve dve devate śritāḥ mārutamākāśa ca vātaḥ śritaḥ agnimādityaṃ ca pittaṃ somaṃ varuṇaṃ ca kaphaḥ tāsteṣāṃ devatāḥ | dharmārthakāmānityeke sattvarajastamāṃsītyeke sādhyayāpyāsādhyatvamityeke || kāni caiṣāṃ
svalakṣaṇāni tatprakṛtīnāmityatrocyate |
tatra śleṣmā snigdha ---
vimānasthānasyaitāvāneva bhāga upalabdhaḥ
<3>
caturthaṃ śārīrasthānam
--- tasmāt pañcaiva khalu ṛtavo'pi tadanupapatternāsti ṣaṭtvamiti atrocyate -- rasārthameṣāṃ ṣaṭtvaṃ rasavimāne proktam || --- sa kaḥ kalāsamūhaṃ kālaṃ dvividhamakalpayat--śubhaṃ cāśubhaṃ ca tau tulyapramāṇau bhūtavartamānānāgatavibhāgāt | tatra śubha utsarpiṇī aśubho'vasarpiṇī te punarubhe trividhe yugabhedena--ādiyugaṃ devayugaṃ kṛtayugamityutsarpiṇī tretādvāparakaliyugānyavasarpiṇī tayorānantyāt parimāṇaṃ nocyate | tatrādiyugadevayuge'cintyapramāṇodbhave karmabhojanapānagativīryāyuṣi anirdeśye | kṛtayuge tu nārāyaṇaṃ nāma dehināṃ saṃhananaṃ śarīramutpadyate tasmāttadāhuḥ -- tasya ghanaṃ niṣkapālaṃ śiraḥ asthīni ca sattvāspadānyākṛtayo vajragarīyasyaḥ hṛdi cāsya mahāsirā daśaiva tvagasya śiraścābhedyamacchedyaṃ sarvato'sya śukraṃ yojanaṃ cāsyotsedhaḥ saptarātraṃ cāsya garbhavāsaḥ sadyojātasya cāsya sarvakarmāṇi śakyāni bhavanti na cainaṃ kṣutpipāsāśramaglāniśokabhayerṣyā'dharmacintādhivyādhijarā bādhante na ca stanyavṛttirbhavati dharmatapojñānavijñānasthitiyuktiścāti bhavati | tasya palitopamārdhamāyurutkṛṣṭamāhuriti || atha tretāyāmardhanārāyaṇaṃ nāma dehināṃ saṃhananaṃ śarīramutpadyate | tasyaikāsthiprāyaṃ śarīramākuñcanaprasāraṇavarjyaṃ garbhavāso'syāṣṭamāsikaḥ stanyajīvikā ca dve śiraskapāle pārśvayorekaikaḥ sandhiḥ urasi ca tryasthi pṛṣṭhaṃ koṣṭhasya sirā viṃśatiḥ śukraṃ ca palitopamacaturbhāgamāyurutkṛṣṭaṃ pūrvāccārdhaguṇāvasarpaṇamiti || atha dvāpare kaiśikasaṃhananaṃ śarīramutpadyate keśamātrāṇusuṣirāsthi atikṣiptasandhi mahāhastibalaḥ sirānuveṣṭitagātraḥ gātrasandhiṣu cāsya śukraṃ palitopamāṣṭabhāgamāyurutkṛṣṭaṃ pūrvāccā rdhaguṇāvasarpaṇamiti ||
atha kaliyuge prajñaptipiśitaṃ saṃhananaṃ śarīramutpadyate | tasya ṣaṣṭiśca trīṇi cāsthiśatāni bhṛśasuṣirāṇi majjapūrṇāni nalavadāsannavadhāni catvāri māṃsapeśīśatāni sapta sirāśatāni hṛdayamūlāni nava snāyuśatāni mastuluṅgamūlāni dve dhamanīśate tālumūle saptottaraṃ marmaśataṃ trīṇi mahāmarmāṇi daśa prāṇāyatanāni pañca hṛdayāni trīṇi sandhiśatānyekāśītāni caturdaśa kaṇḍarāḥ kūrcā dvicatvāriṃśat ṣaṭ tvacaḥ sapta dhātavaḥ srotāṃsi dvividhāni jātasya pṛthagdantajanma daśamāsaṃ garbhavāsaḥ saṃvatsarādūrdhvaṃ pratitiṣṭhati vācaṃ ca visṛjati tasya
varṣaśatamāyurutkṛṣṭaṃ sukhaduḥkhādhivyādhijarāmṛtyuparigataḥ---
sarvagātraḥ kṣutpipāsāgauravaśramaśaithilyacitterṣyāroṣānṛtalaulyaparikleśamohaviyogaprāyaḥ saṃsāragocaraḥ ābādhabahula iti dve dve yuge
sattvarajastamonvaye viddhi | iti puruṣasya sṛṣṭikāraṇamuktam ||
samudayakāraṇaṃ tu brūmaḥ -- avyaktān mahān mahato'haṅkāraḥ ahaṅkārāt khādīni tā aṣṭau bhūtaprakṛtayaḥ | cakṣuḥ śrotraṃ ghrāṇaṃ rasanaṃ sparśanamiti pañcendriyāṇi tānyeva buddhīndriyāṇi hastau pādau jihvā guda upastha iti pañca karmendriyāṇi śabdasparśarasarūpagandhāḥ pañcendriyārthāḥ atīndriyaṃ tu manaḥ ityete ṣoḍaśa vikārāḥ mahadādi sarvaṃ kṣetramavyaktamācakṣate kṣetrajñaṃ tu śāśvatamacintyamātmānam | asya liṅgānicetanāhaṅkāraprāṇāpānonmeṣanimeṣasukhaduḥkhecchādveṣasmṛtidhṛtibuddhayaḥ tadabhāve mṛtākhyā | śarīrendriyātmasattvasamudayaṃ puruṣamācakṣate ātmānameke | jñānasyābhāvo bhāvaśca manaso lakṣaṇaṃ tasyaikatvamaṇutvaṃ ca dvau guṇau prayatnajñānāyaugapadyādekaṃ pṛthak | samanaskamindriyamarthagrahaṇasamarthaṃ bhavati | khaṃ vāyusteja āpaḥ pṛthivīti pañca mahābhūtāni śarīraheturucyate | śabdādayasteṣāṃ guṇāḥ | gunavṛddhyā'vasthitāni mahābhūtāni digātmā manaḥ kālaśca dravyāṇi | dravyāśrayā guṇāḥ | khasyāpratiṣedho liṅgaṃ vāyoścalanaṃ tejasa auṣṇyam apāṃ dravatvaṃ pṛthivyāḥ sthairyam | manaḥṣaṣṭhānāmindriyāṇāṃ trīṇi trīṇi viprakṛṣṭasannikṛṣṭavṛttīni | manaścakṣuḥ śrotramiti viprakṛṣṭavṛttīni ghrāṇaṃ rasanaṃ tvagiti sannikṛṣṭavṛttīni | tat sarvaṃ sparśanalakṣaṇamāhuḥ tadyathā--puruṣaḥ sarvatogavākṣaṃ prāsādamabhirūḍhastāṃstānarthān gavākṣairālocayatyevamayamātmā śarīrastha indriyairanupahatairmanaḥ
asamānagotrīyaśārīrādhyāyaḥ
prāṇastu bījadhātuṃ hi vibhajatyasthisaṃkhyayā
praviṣṭamātraṃ bījaṃ hi raktena pariveṣṭyate
śukrādasthyasthito māṃsamubhābhyāṃ snāyavaḥ smṛtāḥ
sarvendriyāṇi garbhasya sarvāṅgāvayavāstathā
tṛtīye māsi yugapannirvartante yathākramam
praspandate cetayati vedanāścāvabuddhyate
sūkṣmapravyaktakaraṇastṛtīye tu manodhikaḥ
caturthe sthiratāṃ yāti garbhaḥ kukṣau nirāmayaḥ
gurugātratvamadhikaṃ garbhiṇyāstatra jāyate
māṃsaśoṇitavṛddhistu pañcame māsi jīvaka
garbhiṇī pañcame māsi tasmāt kārśyena yujyate
balavarṇaujasāṃ vṛddhiḥ ṣaṣṭhe mātuḥ śramo'dhikaḥ
sarvadhātvaṅgasaṃpūrṇo vātapittakaphānvitaḥ
saptame māsi tasmācca nityaklāntā'tra garbhiṇī
aṣṭame garbhiṇīgarbhāvādadāte parasparam
ojo rasavahāyukteḥ pūrṇatvācchalayatyapi
tasmāttatra muhurglānā muhurhṛṣṭā ca garbhiṇī
atyayaṃ cāpnute tasmānna māso gaṇyate'ṣṭamaḥ
navamādiṣu māseṣu janma cāsya yathākramam
pūrvadehakṛtaṃ karma garbhāvāsasukhāsukham
jātaḥ smarati tāvacca yāvannopaiti jīvikām
iti ha smāha bhagavān kaśyapaḥ
iti śārīre'samānagotrīyaṃ nāma
garbhāvakrāntiśārīrādhyāyaḥ
athāto garbhāvakrāntiṃ śārīraṃ vyākhyāsyāmaḥ 1
iti ha smāha bhagavān kaśyapaḥ 2
jīvastu khalu bho sarvagatatvādīśvaraguṇasamanvitaḥ pūrvaśarīrāccāvakrāmati paraśarīraṃ copakrāmati yugapat na kadācidapi bījaśoṇitavāyvākāśādimanobuddhibhirviyuktapūrvaḥ sarvagatatvācca na kasyāṃcidyonau
nopapadyate svakarmaphalānubhavāditi 3
garbhasya punarbhagavan ke śarīrāvayavā ākāśānnirvartante ke vāyoḥ ke tejasaḥ ke'dbhyaḥ ke pṛthivyāḥ ke cāsya mātṛtaḥ saṃbhavataḥ saṃbhavanti ke cāsya pitṛtaḥ kimātmanaḥ kiṃca sātmyataḥ kiṃca rasataḥ kiṃca sattvataḥ kutra caite sarvabhāvā anvāyattā bhavanti kaṃ cārthamavekṣante iti pṛṣṭo
bhagavān kaśyapa uvāca --
garbhasya khalu bho śabdaśca śrotraṃ ca lāghavaṃ ca saukṣmyaṃ ca vivekaśca mukhaṃ ca kaṇṭhaśca koṣṭhaṃ cākāśātmakāni bhavanti sparśaśca sparśanaṃ ca raukṣyaṃ ca preraṇaṃ ca dhātuvyūhanaṃ ca prāṇaścāpānaśca śarīraceṣṭā ca vāyvātmakāni bhavanti rūpaṃ ca cakṣuśca prakāśaśca pittaṃ ca paktiścoṣmā ca śarīravṛddhiśca taijasāni bhavanti rasaśca rasanaṃ ca śaityaṃ ca mārdavaṃ ca dravaśca snehaśca kledaśca śleṣmā ca medaśca raktaṃ ca māṃsaṃ ca śukraṃ cāpyāni bhavanti gandhaśca ghrāṇaṃ ca gauravaṃ ca sthairyaṃ ca mūrtiśca pārthivāni bhavanti tasmāt puruṣo lokasaṃmitaḥ procyate | lohitaṃ ca māṃsaṃ ca nābhiśca hṛdayaṃ ca kloma ca yakṛcca plīhā ca vṛkkau ca bastiśca purīṣadhāraṇaṃ cāmāśayaścottaragudaśca kṣudrāntraṃ ca sthūlāntraṃ ceti mātṛjāni keśāśca romāṇi ca śmaśrūṇi ca nakhāśca dantāścāsthīni ca sirāśca snāyavaśca dhamanyaśca śukraṃ ceti pitṛjāni āyuścātmajñānaṃ ca manaścendriyāṇi ca prāṇāpānau ca dhāraṇaṃ ca preraṇaṃ ca cākṛtiśca svaravarṇopacayaviśeṣāśca sukhaduḥkhe icchādveṣau ca smṛtiścāhaṅkāraśca prayatnaścāvasthāntaragamanaṃ ca sattvaṃ ca nānāyoniṣūpapattiścetyātmajāni ārogyaṃ cotthānaṃ ca saṃtoṣaścendriyaprasādaśca svaravarṇabījasaṃpacca medhā ca praharṣabhūyiṣṭhatā ceti sātmyajāni śarīrābhinirvṛttiśca śarīrābhivṛddhiśca prāṇāśca bandhaśca vṛttiśca puṣṭiścotsāhaśceti rasajāni | kalyāṇaroṣamohātmakaṃ tu sattvaṃ trividhamuktamagre tatraupapādikaṃ sattvaṃ manaśca layi nityaṃ śubhāśubhamiśrabhāvānāṃ sparśa ityucyate | te sarvabhāvāḥ svakarmaṇyāyattāḥ kālaṃ cāvekṣante | vāyurhi kālasahitaḥ śarīraṃ vibhajati saṃdadhāti ceti 4
tatra ślokāḥ --
śoṇitāddhṛdayaṃ tasya jāyate hṛdayādyakṛt
yakṛto jāyate plīhā plīhnaḥ phupphusamucyate 5
parasparanibandhāni sarvāṇyetāni bhārgava
teṣāmadhastādvipulaṃ srotaḥ kuṇḍalasaṃsthitam 6
jarāyuṇā parivītaṃ sa garbhāśaya ucyate
āmapakvāśayau tasminnannapānāśrayau gudaḥ 7
tasmāt saṃjāyate bastiḥ pariṣyandācca pūryate
dhamanīmukhasaṃsthāne srotasī cāpyadhaḥ smṛte 8
viṇmūtrakṛmipakvāmakaphapittāśayāḥ pṛthak
santyete dehināṃ koṣṭhe striyā garbhāśayo'ṣṭamaḥ 9
śukramajjāsthi pitṛto mātṛto māṃsaśoṇitam
ṣaṭkośaṃ pravadantyeke deha --- 10
śarīravicayaśārīrādhyāyaḥ
dvātriṃśattu matā dantāstāvantyū khalikāni ca
pāṇipādāṅgulāsthīni ṣaṣṭiḥ syurviṃśatirnakhāḥ
pāṇipādaśalākāstu viṃśatiḥ parikīrtitāḥ
pāṇipādaśalākānāmadhiṣṭhānacatuṣṭayam
dve pārṇyorasthinī kūrcāścatvāraḥ pādayoḥ smṛtāḥ
dvāveva hastamaṇikau catvāryāhuraratniṣu
jānvasthinī dve saṃkhyāte catvāryasthīni jaṅghayoḥ
dvāvūrunalakau dve ca khyāte jānukapālike
dvāvaṃsāvaṃsaphalakāvapi dvāveva cākṣakau
dve bāhunalake dve dve śroṇitālūṣake tathā
ekaṃ jatru bhagāsthyekaṃ grīvā pañcadaśāsthikī
bhārgavā'sthīni pṛṣṭhayāni catvāriṃśacca pañca ca
caturdaśāsthīnyurasi hanvasthyekaṃ tu nirdiśet
śirasastu kapālāni catvāryāhurmanīṣiṇaḥ
caturviṃśatiḥ pārśve ca tāvanti sthālakāni ca
caturviṃśatirevāhuḥ sthālakārbudakāni ca
dvau śaṅkhau parisaṃkhyātau dve hanumūlabandhane
lalāṭanāsikāgaṇḍakūṭāsthyekaṃ vinirdiśet
ityasthisaṃkhyā sāmānyād vṛddhihrāsau nimittajau
daśaivāyatanānyāhuḥ prāṇānāṃ tāni me śṛṇu
mūrdhā'tha hṛdayaṃ bastiḥ kaṇṭhaujaḥ śukraśoṇitam
śaṅkhau gudaṃ tatastrīṇi mahāmarmāṇi cāditaḥ
nābhiḥ plīhā yakṛt kloma hṛdvṛkkau gudabastayaḥ
kṣudrāntramatha ca sthūlamāmapakvāśayau vapā
koṣṭhāṅgāni vadanti jñāḥ pratyaṅgāni nibodha me
akṣiṇī nāsike karṇau stanāvoṣṭhau kukundarau
hastau pādau bhruvau kūṭau bāhujaṅghorupiṇḍikāḥ
sṛkkiṇī karṇaśaṣkulyau karṇaputrākṣitārake
vṛṣaṇau dantaveṣṭau ca śaṅkhakāvupajihvike
dantalohādhimūlāni dve dve sarvāṇi nirdiśet
bastirbastiśiraḥ śephaḥ pṛṣṭhaṃ sacibukodaram
lalāṭamāsyaṃ gojihvā śiro hṛdayamekaśaḥ
pāṇipādataleṣveva catvāri hṛdayāni tu
śākhāhṛdayasaṃjñāni pañcamaṃ cetanāśrayam
akṣibandhāni catvāri vidyādviṃśatiraṅgulīḥ
srotāṃsi dvividhānyāhuḥ sūkṣmāṇi ca mahānti ca
mahānti nava jānīyād dve cādhaḥ sapta copari
nābhiśca romakūpāśca sūkṣmasrotāṃsi nirdiśet
hṛdayāt saṃpratāyante sirāṇāṃ daśa mātaraḥ
ūrdhvaṃ catasro dve tiryakcatasro'dhovahāḥ sirāḥ
vyāpnuvanti śarīraṃ tā bhidyamānāḥ punaḥ punaḥ
parṇānāmiva sīvanyaḥ saraṇācca sirāḥ smṛtāḥ
yathā kāṣṭhamayaṃ rūpaṃ tṛṇarajjvopaveṣṭitam
bhavelliptaṃ mṛdā bāhyaṃ tathedaṃ dehasaṃjñakam
asthīni snāyubaddhāni snāyavo māṃsalepanāḥ
sirābhiḥ puṣyate nityaṃ tasya sarvaṃ tvacā tatam
santataṃ karṇamūlābhyāṃ dhamanīnāṃ śataṃ śatam
tāsu nityo'nilastiṣṭhannagnīṣomau bibhartyadhi
sāgre śatasahasre dve bahirantaśca kūpakāḥ
romakūpāni tāvanti jātānyekaikaśo yadi
vṛddhihrāsau niṣekācca svabhāvādviśvakarmaṇaḥ
caturbhāgavihīnāni strīṇāṃ viddhi svabhāvataḥ
kūpake kūpake cāpi vidyāt sūkṣmaṃ sirāmukham
prasvidyamānastaiḥ svedaṃ vimuñcati sirāmukhaiḥ
jātasya vardhamānasya yūno vṛddhasya dehinaḥ
svenāñjalipramāṇena dravāṇi pramimīmahe
majjamedovasāmūtrapittaśleṣmāṇi viṭ tathā
ekadvitricatuṣpañcaṣaṭsaptāñjalikāḥ smṛtāḥ
śoṇitāñjalayo'ṣṭau tu nava paktirasasya tu
daśaivāñjalayaḥ proktā udakasya tvagāśrayāḥ
tenodakena puṣyanti dhātavo lohitādayaḥ
atīsāre puruṣaṃ ca tato mūtraṃ pravartate
vraṇe lasīkā pūyaṃ ca picchā cātaḥ pravartate
bhavanti tasmin duṣṭe ca dadrukaṇḍūvicarcikāḥ
tvagāmayāḥ kilāsāni pāmā keśavadhastathā
tadagnimārutodviddhaṃ pakaiḥ sveda ucyate
śleṣmaṇastu pramāṇena pramāṇaṃ tulyamojasaḥ
śukrasyārdhāñjalirdehe mastiṣkasya tathaiva ca
etat pramāṇamuddiṣṭamutkṛṣṭaṃ sarvameva tu
prajñāptapiśitīyasya tato madhyaṃ tato'dhamam
śukraṃ tu ṣoḍaśe varṣe saṃpūrṇaṃ saṃpravartate
anyonyasaṃśrayāṇyāhuranyonyaguṇavanti ca
mahābhūtāni dṛśyāni dārvagnitilatailavat
śarīrasaṃkhyā nirdiṣṭā yathāsthūlaṃ prakārataḥ
dehāvayavasūkṣmaṃ tu bhedānantyaṃ sudurvacam
iti ha smāha bhagavān kaśyapaḥ
iti śārīrasthāne śarīravicayaṃ nāma śārīram
jātisūtrīyaśārīrādhyāyaḥ
athāto jātisūtrīyaṃ vyākhyāsyāmaḥ 1
iti ha smāha bhagavān kaśyapaḥ 2
jātau jātau khalu svabhāva evākṛtibhedanirvartayitā bhavati | svabhāvato hyasya vāyuparamāṇavaḥ saṃyogavibhāgaceṣṭādhikārā ākuñcanaprasāraṇakoṣṭhāṅgapratyaṅgadhātucetanāsrotāṃsi vibhajānta | samatyake dhāturiva niṣiktaḥ puruṣaḥ puruṣamabhinirvartayati gaurgāmaśvā'śvamevamādi | nṛṇāmapi tu madhye garbhanirvṛttiḥ | tatra dvayordampatyoḥ svabhāvāt svakarmapariṇāmādvā prajābhivṛddhirbhavati tau dhanyau ato'nyathā bhiṣajitavyau | snehasvedavamanavirecanāsthāpanānuvāsanaiḥ kramaśa upacarenmadhurauṣadhasiddhābhyāṃ kṣīraghṛtapuṣṭaṃ puruṣaṃ striyaṃ tu tailamāṃsābhyāmityeke sātmyaireveti prajāpatiḥ 3
yathā ca puṣpamadhye phalamanirvṛttaṃ susūkṣmamasti na copalabhyate yathā cāgnirdāruṣu sarvagataḥ pratnābhāvānnopalabhyate tathā strīpuṃsayoḥ śoṇitaśukre kālāvekṣe svakarmāvakṣe ca bhavataḥ | ṣoḍaśavarṣayorhi śoṇitaśukrayormadhye prabhavataḥ arvāgapi yadāhāraviśeṣādārogyāśca pūrṇe bhavata
iti pariṣat 4
rajasvalāyāścet prathame'hani garbha āpadyeta taṃ vātagarbhamācakṣate viphalaṃ vātapuṣpamivodbhidānāṃ dvitīye'hani cet sraṃsate cyavate vā tṛtīye'hani sūtikāsane mriyate na vā dīrghāyurbhavati hīnāṅgaśca jāyate ata ūrdhvamṛturdvādaśāhaṃ brāhmaṇīnāmekādaśāhaṃ kṣatriyāṇāṃ daśāhaṃ vaiśyānāṃ navarātramitarāsām | ṛturbījakālamavekṣata ityāhurmaharṣayaḥ | ata ūrdhvamakālajamāhuḥ | akālajaṃ hīnaṃ durbalamasthiramadṛḍhamapīnabhaṅguraṃ
dhānyamiva bhavati 5
yugmeṣvahaḥsu putrakāmo'nyatra kanyārthī harṣitastṛpto'nuruddhaḥ striyamupeyāditi siddham 6
atha śuddhasnātāṃ striyaṃ caturthe'hani snānagṛhe śvetena evā'nyena vāsasā'vaguṇṭhyānavalokayantī śucirdevagṛhaṃ praviśyodghaṭāgniṃ prajvalantaṃ ghṛtākṣatenābhyarcya brāhmaṇamīśvaraṃ viṣṇuṃ skandaṃ ca saṃprekṣyābhivādya niṣkramya sūryācandramasāviti na tu pretapiśācarakṣāṃsi śuddhasnātamātrā hi strī yaṃ vā paśyati manasā vā'bhidhyāyati tādṛśācāravapuṣaṃ prāyeṇa janayati tasmāddevagobrāhmaṇaguruvṛddhācāryān sataḥ paśyet kalyāṇamanāśca syāt | na tu sandhyayoḥ snānaṃ maithunaṃ vopeyānnānyamanā iti

tata ṛtvik putrīyāmiṣṭiṃ nirvapet | siddhamāṃsaudanā vātaghnau vājyabhāgau yavamayaḥ puroḍāśo'ṣṭākapālo vrīhimayaścaruḥ ubhau vāgāyuryutau prajāyete na tvade --- kṣe ābrahmanbrāhmaṇa iti yajamānabhāgamabhimantrya śeṣaṃ dampatī prāśnīyātām | śveta ṛṣabho'śvo vā hiraṇyaṃ vā bhiṣaje saiva dakṣiṇā saivamanāhitāgneḥ śālāgnau nityaṃ homaṃ hutvā tenaiva mantreṇa hutaśeṣaṃ tau prā | śayanīye mṛdusvāstīrṇopahite'syai bhartā ---tra lakṣmaṇāmadbhirāloḍya somaḥ pavata ityetena śatajaptena sāvitryā vyāhṛtibhiḥ apo devīrupasṛja iti mantreṇa nasyaṃ dattvā vāmadevyaṃ japitvā dakṣiṇena pārśvena striyaṃ śāyayīta vāmapārśvena pumānūrdhvottareṇopaśayīta | śanaiḥ prajārthaṃ cācaret | bīje'vasikte vidhāryāvasarpet | śītodakena ca śaucaṃ kuryāt | tata ūrdhvamagnikarmapratāpāyāsavyāyāmaśokādivarjanamiti 8
sā cedicched gauramojasvinaṃ śucimāyuṣmantaṃ putraṃ janayeyamiti tasyā evaṃ śuddhasnānāt prabhṛti śuklayavasaktūnāṃ madhughṛtābhyāṃ śvetāyāḥ śvetapuṃvatsāyā goḥ kṣīreṇa saṃsṛjya manthaṃ rājate pātre kāṃsye vā sadā pāyayet śāligaurayavakṣīradadhighṛtaprāyaṃ ca kāle mātrayā aśnīyāt puṣpābharaṇavāsāṃsi ca śuklāni bibhṛyāt sāyaṃ prātaśca śvetamaśvaṃ vṛṣabhaṃ vā paśyet saumyahitapriyakathābhirāsīta anukūlaparivārā ca syādiṣṭamapatyaṃ janayati | yā tu śyāmaṃ lohitākṣaṃ vyūḍhoraskaṃ putramicchet kṛṣṇaṃ vā tatra tādṛgupacāro bhojanavasanakusumālaṅkārāṇāṃ tādṛgdeśānucintanaṃ ceti | yavāgūṃ tu kanyāthinībhyodadyāt kṣīrodakatilasiddhāstu varṇyāḥ | gauraśyāmakṛṣṇebhyo'nye varṇā ninditāḥ 9
āhāraścaturvidhaḥ ṣaḍrasāśrayo viṃśativikalpogurulaghuśītoṣṇasnigdharūkṣamandatīkṣṇasthirasaramṛdukaṭhinaviśadapicchilaślakṣṇakharasūkṣmasthūlasāndradravavikalpāt tena tvagādayaḥ śukrāntā dhātava āpyāyante | teṣāṃ samānaṃ vardhanamaviruddhāśanam | vātādīnāṃ tu dhātūnāmanye dhātava āpyāyitā bhavanti bhujyamānaṃ māṃsaṃ māṃsasya śoṇitaṃ śoṇitasyeti tadadharmabhayādaniṣṭaṃ tadguṇaistu śucibhirāhāraiḥ kṣīṇadhātūnāpyāyayet | śukrakṣaye kṣīraghṛtopayogo madhurasnigdhajīvanānāṃ cānyeṣāmapi dravyāṇāmavidāhināṃ praśasyate mūtrakṣaye punarikṣurasavāruṇīmaṇḍadravamadhurāmlalavaṇatakraguḍatrapusopakledināṃ purīṣakṣaye yavānnavikṛtikulmāṣamāṣaṣaṣṭikayāvakagorasāmlalavaṇasnigdhaśākopayogaḥ vātakṣaye kaṭutiktakaṣāyalaghurūkṣaśītayavānnopayogaḥ pittakṣaye kaṭulavaṇāmlatīkṣṇoṣṇakṣārāṇāṃ kaphakṣaye snigdhamadhuragurusāndrādīnām 10
yāni dravyāṇi puṇyāni maṅgalyāni śucīni ca navānyabhagnakhaṇḍāni punnāmāni priyāṇi ca
garbhiṇyai tānyupaharedvāsāṃsyābharaṇāni ca
na strīnapuṃsakākhyāni dhārayedvā labheta vā
dhūpitārcitasaṃmṛṣṭaṃ maśakādyapavarjitam
brahmaghoṣaiḥ savāditrairvāditaṃ veśma śasyate
prātarutthāya śaucānte gurudevārcane ratā
arcedādityamudyantaṃ gandhadhūpārghyavārjapaiḥ
kṣīyamāṇaṃ ca śaśinamastaṃ yāntaṃ ca bhāskaram
na paśyedgarbhiṇī nityaṃ nāpyubhau rāhudarśane
somārkau sagrahau śrutvā garbhiṇī garbhaveśmani
śāntihomaparā''sīta muktayogaṃ tu yācayet
na dviṣyādatithiṃ bhikṣāṃ dadyānna prativārayet
svayaṃ prajvalite cāgnau śāntyarthaṃ juhuyādghṛtam
pūrṇakumbhaṃ ghṛtaṃ mālyaṃ pūrṇapātraṃ ghṛtaṃ dadhi
na kiñcit pratirudhnīyānna na badhnīta garbhiṇī
sūtreṇa tanunā rajjvā stambhanaṃ bandhanāni ca
varjayedgarbhiṇī nityaṃ kāmaṃ bandhāni mokṣayet
atha hīmāni rūpāṇi garbhiṇyā upalakṣayet
yānidṛṣṭvā jivānīyādbālajanmānyupakramet
mukhaglāniḥ klamo'ṅgānāmakṣibandhanamuktatā
kukṣeśca syādavasraṃsastvadhobhāgasya gauravam
pṛṣṭhapārśvakaṭībastivaṃkṣaṇaṃ cātitudyati
yoniprasravaṇaudāryabhaktadveṣāratiklamāḥ
etāni dṛṣṭvā rūpāṇi kṛtvā brāhmaṇavācanam
praviśeyuḥ striyo vṛddhāḥ kuśalāḥ śastadhāvitāḥ
garbhiṇīṃ sāntvayeyustā harṣayeyuḥ priyaṃvadāḥ
āśvāsayeyurdharmārthau codayantaṃ prajāpatim
lokān putravatīnāṃ ca sukhāni vividhāni ca
kīrtayeyuraputrāṇāṃ duḥkhāni nirayādiṣu
aditiṃ kaśyapaṃ devamindrāṇīmindramaśvinau
āyuṣmatāṃ putravatāṃ maṅgalyānāṃ ca kīrtanam
tantrīvarṇo'lpaśaḥ srāvaḥ picchilaḥ putrajanmani
kiṃśukodakasaṃkāśaḥ putrikājanma śaṃsati
sūterūrdhvaṃ tu ye srāvā ninditāñśasmayettu tān
tasyā asyāmavasthāyāmupayāceta devatāḥ
avyāvṛte striyā garbhe vivṛte cāparāmukhe
grāhīṣu vartamānāsu sā vivarteta garbhiṇī
na tīkṣṇaṃ grāhiśūleṣu kṣipraṃ nārī prajāyate
vilambitābhirāvībhirgarbhaḥ kleśayate striyam
kecidasyāmavasthāyāṃ vyāyāmaṃ musalādikam
jṛmbhācaṅkramaṇādyaṃ ca bhiṣajo bruvate hitam
varjanīyaṃ tu tat sarvaṃ bhagavānāha kaśyapaḥ
nāryāḥ prasavakāle hi śarīramupamṛdyate
trayo doṣāḥ prakupyanti vicālyante ca dhātavaḥ
garbhiṇī tadavasthā hi yatnadhāryā viśeṣataḥ
adhikaṃ saukumāryaṃ hi garbhiṇyāḥ kledyameva ca
srāvakāle viśeṣeṇa viṣādabhayasaṃśrayaḥ
ekapādo yamakule pāda eka iha sthitaḥ
dṛṣṭvā duḥkhaṃ striyastasyā ityevaṃ bruvate mithaḥ
tasyāstvasyāmavasthāyāṃ vyāyāmo na praśasyate
vyāyāmaḥ sevyamāno hi garbhiṇīmāśu nāśayet
aticaṅkramaṇenāpi hanyādgarbhamupasthitam
atyayaṃ prāpnuyāddhoraṃ dehāntakaraṇaṃ mahat
upaviṣṭā'sakṛttasmādanirviṇṇātrapānvitā
vṛddhastrīdravyasaṃpannā prajāyeta prajārthinī
vacā lāṅgalikī kuṣṭhaṃ cirabilvailacitrakāḥ
cūrṇitaṃ mukharājighrettathā śīghraṃ prajāyate
ājighredbhūrjadhūpaṃ vā namerorguggulostathā
atha prapadyate garbhastathā kṣipraṃ vimucyate
pārśvasandhikaṭīpṛṣṭhaṃ tailenoṣṇena mrakṣitam
mṛdgīyuravakarṣeyuḥ śanaiḥ prājñyaḥ striyaḥ sukhāḥ
durbalāṃ pāyayenmadyamityeke neti kaśyapaḥ
pūrvakliṣṭā tathaivāsyāyavāgūṃ tṛṣitā pibet
yadā garbhodakaṃ yonau saśūlaṃ saṃpravartate
kālena codito garbho vimucya hṛdayodaram
bastiśīrṣamadhobhāgamavagṛhṇāti janmani
glāniśca jāyate'tyarthaṃ yonyutpīḍanabhedanam
ityetaiḥ kāraṇairvidyādgarbhasya parivartanam
athāsyāḥ prasavaśceti tataḥ paryaṅkamāruhet
prāvāramupadhānaṃ vā ---
śārīrasthānasyaitāvāneva bhāga upalabdhaḥ
