indriyasthānam
pañcamamindriyasthānam oṣadhabheṣajendriyādhyāyaḥ
athāta oṣadhabheṣajīyamindriyaṃ vyākhyāsyāmaḥ 1
iti ha smāha bhagavān kaśyapaḥ 2
oṣadhaṃ bheṣajaṃ proktaṃ dviprakāraṃ cikitsitam
tayorviśeṣaṃ vakṣyāmi bheṣajauṣadhayordvayoḥ 3
oṣadhaṃ dravyasaṃyogaṃ bruvate dīpanādikam
hutavratatapodānaṃ śāntikarma ca bheṣajam 4
ubhayaṃ tadyadā jantoḥ kṛtaṃ na kurute guṇam
kṣīṇāyuriti saṃjñātvā na cikitsedvicakṣaṇaḥ 5
yasya gomayacūrṇābhaṃ cūrṇaṃ mūrdhani jāyate
sasnehaṃ bhraśyate caiva māsāntaṃ tasya jīvitam 6
kukṣiḥ snātānuliptasya pūrvaṃ yasya viśuṣyati
ārdreṣu sarvagātreṣu māsārdhaṃ tasya jīvitam 7
svapnādhipānago nāśo jyotiṣāṃ patanāni ca
agnidāhopaśāntiśca patanaṃ gṛhavṛkṣayoḥ 8
guhāṭavīpraveśaśca svapnaṃ svapne vigarhitam
kṛṣṇāṃ daṇḍadharāṃ nagnāṃ muṇḍāṃ lohitalocanām 9
svapne dṛṣṭvaiva jānīyādyamadūtānupasthitān
dīrghakeśastananakhīṃ virāgakusumāmbarām 10
svapne dṛṣṭvā striyaṃ kṛṣṇāṃ kālarātrīṃ nivedayet
gandhān puṣpāṇi vāsāṃsi yā raktāni niṣevate 11
yadā svapne śiśurvā'pi tadā skandagrahādbhayam
mayūraṃ kukkuṭaṃ bastaṃ meṣaṃ vā yo'dhirohati 12
raktarcitaḥ sahaitairvā tatrāpi skandato bhayam
ghaṇṭāṃ patākāṃ yaḥ svapne vidhvastāṃ bhuvi paśyati 13
śayanaṃ śoṇitāktaṃ vā tatrāpi skandato bhayam
raktapuṣpāmbaradharā raktacanadanarūṣitā 14
nṛtyate saha bhūtairvā skandāpasmārato bhayam
raktapadmavanaṃ prāpya dhātryātmānaṃ yadā'rcati 15
bālaṃ vā padmamālābhistadā skandapiturbhayam
raktapuṣpavanaṃ dhātrī svapne'gniṃ vā yadā viśet 16
dahyate vā'gninā bālaḥ pauṇḍarīkādbhayaṃ tadā
samudrādiṣu toyeṣu nimagne revatībhayam 17
śuṣkakūpanadīdarśaṃ nihanyācchuṣkarevatī
māṃsādān pakṣiṇo dṛṣṭvā śakunyā badhyate śiśuḥ 18
avaḍīnābhidaṣṭastu sadyo maraṇamṛcchati
haritālādibhī raṅgairmaṇḍitaḥ pītakāmbaraḥ 19
māṃsalo'laṅkṛtaḥ svapne taṃ hanti mukhamaṇḍikā
nakṣatragrahacandrārkatārakā'kṣikanīnikāḥ 20
dṛṣṭvā prapatitāḥ svapne pūtanābhyo bhayaṃ bhavet
sarvāṇyetāni rūpāṇi naigameṣyāṃ prapaśyati 21
kīṭavṛścikasarpairvā daṣṭaḥ syādviṣamṛtyuḥka
śvabhirduṣṭaiḥ kharairvā'pi dakṣiṇāṃ yāti muṇḍitaḥ 22
kṛṣyate mṛdyate tairvā jvarasyāntastaducyate
prārthitaṃ kalpitaṃ dṛṣṭamanubhūtaṃ śrutaṃ ca yat 23
bhāvitaḥ paśyati svapne hrasvaṃ dīrghaṃ divā ca yat
aphalāḥ sarva evaite nidānoktāstu doṣajāḥ 24
yathā tu phalavān svapno vṛddhaśīvaka tacchṛṇu
adṛṣṭamaśrutānuktamakalpitamabhāṣitam 25
kāryamātraṃ ca yaḥ svapno jīrṇānte phalavāṃstu saḥ
etāṃścānyāṃśca duḥsvapnān dṛṣṭvā rogī vinaśyati 26
svasthastu saṃśayaṃ gatvā dharmaśīlo vimucyate
yadyadeva dvijādīnāṃ svapne śītakṛśātmanām 27
malināmbarapuṣpāṇāṃ darśanaṃ na praśasyate
teṣāmeva tu hṛṣṭānāṃ śuddhapuṣpāmbarātmanām 28
darśanaṃ śasyate svapne taiśca saṃbhāṣaṇaṃ śubham
prāsādavṛkṣaśailāṃśca hastigovṛṣapūruṣān 29
adhirohanti ye svapne teṣāṃ svastyayanaṃ kṛtam
sūryasomāgniviprāṇāṃ nṛṇāṃ puṇyakṛtāṃ gavām 30
matsyāmiṣasya cāṣasya darśanaṃ puṇyamucyate
śuklapuṣpādarśacchatragrahaṇaṃ toyalaṅghanam 31
svaraktadarśanaṃ caiva surāpānaṃ ca śasyate
gavāśvarathayānaṃ ca yānaṃ pūrvottareṇa ca 32
rodanaṃ patitotthānaṃ ripūṇāṃ nigrahastathā
paṅkakūpaguhābhyaśca samuttāro'dhvanastathā 33
evaṃvidhāni cānyāni siddhaye munayo'bruvan
adāruṇatvaṃ rogāṇāṃ vaidyabhaiṣajyasaṃbhavam 34
dhṛtirjanmānukūlyaṃ ca sattvaṃ dharmaśca bhūtaye
dṛṣṭvā svapnān dāruṇānvetarān vā
pūtaḥ snātaḥ sarṣapānagnivarṇān
hutvā sāvitryā sarpiṣāktāṃstilāṃśca
pūtaḥ pāpairmucyate vyādhibhiśca 35
kaumārabhṛtyamativardhanamuktametajjñātvā hi dehagatamindriyamādirūpaiḥ śaścikitsitaparāṃstu vivarjayadhvaṃ śāstraṃ ca dharmamatayaḥ paripālayadhvam 36
iti ha smāha bhagavān kaśyapaḥ
iti vṛddhajīvakīye kaumārabhṛtye vāstyapratisaṃskṛte indriyasthāne
auṣadhabheṣajīyaṃ nāmendriyam
samāptāni cendriyāni
indriyasthānasyāyamantimo'dhyāya evopalabdhaḥ
<5>
cikitsitasthānam
ṣaṣṭhaṃ cikitsitasthānam jvaracikitsitādhyāyaḥ
athāto jvaracikitsitaṃ vyākhyāsyāmaḥ 1
iti ha smāha bhagavān kaśyapaḥ 2
prajāpatiṃ samāsīnamṛṣibhiḥ puṇyakarmabhiḥ
papraccha vinayādvidvān kaśyapaṃ vṛddhajīvakaḥ 3
sūtrasthāne bhagavatā nirdiṣṭo dvividho jvaraḥ
punaraṣṭavidhaḥ prokto nidāne tattvadarśinā 4
teṣāṃ jvarāṇāṃ katamo jātamātrasya jāyate
pūrvarūpaṃ ca rūpaṃ ca kiñca tasya cikitsitam 5
itareṣāṃ jvarāṇāṃ ca pūrvarūpaṃ salakṣaṇam
cikitsitaṃ ca kiṃ teṣāmāmajīrṇajvareṣu ca 6
kṣīrapasya ca kiṃ pathyaṃ pathyaṃ kiṃcānnabhojinaḥ
kṣīrānnabhojinaḥ kiṃca jvaritasya śiśorhitam 7
kāṃ ca vṛttiṃ ---
garbhiṇīcikitsitādhyāyaḥ
saṃyojya madhunā śītaṃ kṣīraṃ madhurasādhikam
śarkarā madhu tailaṃ ca yaṣṭīmadhukaphāṇitam
ete hi lohitā ghnanti tathaiva parikartikām
phāṇitaṃ tilakalkaṃ ca śarkarā madhukaṃ tathā
taṇḍulodakasaṃyuktaṃ sadyo hanti pravāhikām
kāśmaryavṛkṣatvakkalkaṃ śyāmāmūlaṃ tathaiva ca
yavāgūṃ dadhimaṇḍena siddhāmalpaghṛtāṃ pibet
kirātatiktakaṃ lodhraṃ yaṣṭīmadhukameva ca
pātavyaṃ madhusaṃyuktaṃ sadyo hanti pravāhikām
varṣābhūmūlaniṣkvāthaṃ yojayeddevadāruṇā
tat pibenmadhusaṃyuktaṃ śūnā strī mūrvayā saha
pippalyaṅkoṭhamūlāni vājileṇḍarasaṃ tathā
māhiṣeṇa pibeddadhnā kāmalāyāṃ cikitsitam
mātuluṅgarasaḥ peyaḥ saindhavena suyojitaḥ
hṛdi śūlasya bhaiṣajyaṃ śreṣṭhamityāha kaśyapaḥ
pippalīmūlakalkastu patraṃ gandhapriyaṅgavaḥ
mātuluṅgarasaścaiva hṛdi śūlasya bheṣajam
priyaṅgavo'tha pippalyo bhadramustaṃ hareṇavaḥ
kṣaudraṃ badaracūrṇaṃ ca ṣaḍaṅgaṃ hṛdayauṣadham
snigdho māṃsarasaḥ pathyaḥ saindhattrāvenāvacūrṇitaḥ
māhiṣe ṣaṣṭikā vā'pi syādamle tvaci mārute
bhadradāruharītakyau saindhavaṃ kuṣṭhameva ca
saphāṇitaṃ ghṛtaṃ caiva leha ūrdhvānilāpahaḥ
pippalyo gairikaṃ bhārgī hiṅgu karkaṭakī tathā
samākṣiko bhavelleho hikkāśvāsanibarhaṇaḥ
pippalī pippalīmūlaṃ mustā nāgarameva ca
dīpanīyaṃ pibedetaṃ payasā śarkarā'nvitam
nityaṃ snātā ca hṛṣṭā ca śuklavastradharā śuciḥ
devavipraparā saumyā garbhiṇī putrabhāginī
naivonnatā na praṇatā na guruṃ dhārayecciram
udvejanaṃ tathā hāsyaṃ saṃghātaṃ cāpi varjayet
iti ha smāha bhagavān kaśyapaḥ
iti garbhiṇīcikitsitam
duṣprajātācikitsitādhyāyaḥ
athāto duṣprajātācikitsitaṃ vyākhyāsyāmaḥ 1
iti ha smāha bhagavān kaśyapaḥ 2
ye strīṇāṃ duṣprajātānāṃ vyādhayaḥ saṃbhavanti hi
nāmatastān pravakṣyāmi teṣāṃ caiva cikitsitam 3
yāḥ kṛcchreṇa prajāyante prasūtāścāmayanti yāḥ
snehasvedaistatastāsāṃ kṣipraṃ vāyuḥ praśāmyati 4
yavāgūṃ dīpanīyāṃ tu smṛtimān dātumarhati
yathā śete sukhaṃ nārī nīrujā śayane sukhe 5
rātrau nirgamanāttrāsāt sahasotpanādapi
īrṣyāśokabhayakrodhānnānāvegavidhāraṇāt 6
etaiścānyaiśca nārīṇāṃ vyādhayaḥ saṃbhavanti hi
sūtikānāṃ divāsvapnādajīrṇāddhyaśanādapi 7
yonipṛṣṭhakaṭībhedaśākhāvāyurasṛgdaraḥ
vātāṣṭhīlā ca gulmaśca hṛdi śūlaṃ pravāhikā 8
purīṣamūtrasaṃrodha ādhmānaṃ śūlameva ca
śūyate duṣyate yoniryoniśūlaṃ ca dāruṇam 9
vepathuśchardanaṃ moho manyāstambho hanugrahaḥ
jvarātisāro vaisarpo dadrupāmāvicarcikāḥ 10
kiṭibhānyatha visphoṭā gātre cārdhaśirorujā
hṛdrogāścākṣirogāśca plīhā śvayathukāmale 11
ete cānye ca bahavo duṣprajātāśarīrajāḥ
vyādhayaḥ saṃprakupyanti cikitsitamataḥ param 12
dve pañcamūle bhārgī ca madhuśigruḥ śatāvarī
uśīraṃ candanaṃ caiva śvadaṃṣṭrā madayantikā 13
dve bale vasukaḥ pāṭhā payasyā hyamṛtā tathā
vṛṣādanī sugandhā ca tathā kāryā punarnavā 14
mūrvā gṛdhranakhī mustā moraṭastilvakastathā
ityetāsāṃ tu mūlāni yathālābhaṃ samānayet 15
yavakolakulatthānāṃ trayaḥ prasthāḥ samāstataḥ
etānyaṣṭaguṇe toye pācayedbhiṣaguttamaḥ 16
aṣṭabhāgasthitaṃ taṃ tu paripūtaṃ nidhāpayet
tatrāvāpamidaṃ dadyānmuṣṭikānyauṣadhāni tu 17
pippalī pippalīmūlaṃ citrakaṃ hastipippalī
cavyaṃ dve rajanī caiva śṛṅgaveraṃ vacā'bhayā 18
kuṣṭhaṃ rāsnā'jamodaśca viḍaṅgaṃ maricāni ca
bhadradārurathailā ca bhārgīkuṭajataṇḍulāḥ 19
eteṣāṃ kārṣikā bhāgā lavaṇānāṃ palaṃ bhavet
tailaprasthaṃ vasāprasthaṃ niṣkvātho dviguṇo bhavet 20
kṣīraprastho dadhiprastho jalaprasthastathaiva ca
mātuluṅgāmrapeśīnāṃ rasaprasthārdhayojitam 21
śanairmṛdvagninā siddhamathainamavatārayet
abhyañjaneṣu pāneṣu bastikarmaṇi cottamam 22
ye tu vātasamutthānāḥ sūtikānāmupadravāḥ
sarveṣāṃ śamanaṃ śreṣṭhametattraivṛtamuttamam 23
eteṣāmeva sarveṣāṃ kalkaṃ niṣkvāthya pāyayet
yaḥ kaścit sūtikāvyādhistaṃ trirātreṇa sādhayet 24
dve pañcamūlyau bhārgī ca rāsnā dve ca punarnave
śigrurhaṃsapadī ---
bālagrahacikitsitādhyāyaḥ
--- ścābhiyācanam
brahmaṇyabhāvāt kruddhā'pi prāha sānugrahaṃ vakkkpkṣaīcaḥ
eṣāṃ vyatikramāṇāṃ tvaṃ phalamāpnuhi revati
sarvagrahāṇāmekā tvaṃ tulyavīryabaladyutiḥ
bhaviṣyasi durādharṣā devānāmapi pūjitā
nābhirbahubhiścaiva tvāṃ vakṣyanti janā bhuvi
vāruṇī revatī brāhmī kumārī bahuputrikā
śuṣkā ṣaṣṭhī ca yamikā dharaṇī mukhamaṇḍikā
mātā śītavatī kaṇḍūḥ pūtanā'tha niruñcikā
rodanī bhūtamātā ca lokamātāmahīti ca
śaraṇyā puṇyakīrtiśca nāmāni tava viṃśatiḥ
ye ca tvāṃ pūjayiṣyanti śraddadhānā janā bhuvi
naiteṣāṃ sarvabhūtebhyo bhaviṣyati bhayaṃ kvacit
sāyaṃ prātaśca nāmāni yo japettava viṃśatim
śucirnaraḥ prajāstasya vardhiṣyanti vipāpmanaḥ
tata ugreṇa tapasā skandamārādhayan punaḥ
tasyā manīṣitaṃ jñātvā revatīmabravīd guhaḥ
bhrātṝṇāṃ ca caturṇāṃ vai pañcamo nandikeśvaraḥ
bhrātā tvaṃ bhaginī ṣaṣṭhī loke khyātā bhaviṣyasi
yathā māṃ pūjayiṣyanti tathā tvāṃ sarvadehinaḥ
asmattulyaprabhāvā tvāṃ bhrātṛmadhyagatā sadā
ṣaṇmukhī nityalalitā varadā kāmarūpiṇī
ṣaṣṭhī ca te tithiḥ pūjyā puṇyā loke bhaviṣyati
ityevaṃ bhaginī jajñe ṣaṣṭhī skandasya dhīmataḥ
tasmāt sā satataṃ pūjyā sā hi mūlaṃ sukhāyuṣoḥ
tasmācca sūtikāṣaṣṭhīṃ pakṣaṣaṣṭhīṃ ca pūjayet
uddiśya ṣaṇmukhīṃ ṣaṣṭhīṃ tathā lokeṣu nandati
ityevaṃ revatī jajñe surāsuranamaskṛtā
vṛddhajīvaka karmāṇi śṛṇu tasyāḥ pradhānataḥ
jvarātisāro vaisarpaḥ pīḍanendriyadūṣaṇam
ānāhaḥ śūlamarucirmi---naṃ śvāsakāsatṛṭ
nidrānāśo'tinidrā ca mukhapāko vraṇodbhavaḥ
ekāṅgakaḥ pakṣavadhaḥ kṣīrālasavisūcikāḥ
hikkā mūrcchā mado moho rodanaṃ stabdhanetratā
svaravarṇāgnibhedaśca pāṇḍutvaṃ kāmalā'ratiḥ
kṣīradūṣaṇanāśau ca śirorugghṛdayadravaḥ
nāsākṣikarṇarogāśca trāsakuñcanarodanam
ye cānye caiva vividhā ye rogā nānukīrtitāḥ
revatīroṣasaṃbhūtā bhūyiṣṭhaṃ ta udāhṛtāḥ
tasmāt sādhāraṇīṃ tasyāḥ kriyāṃ kuryādvicakṣaṇaḥ
ataśca revatīmeke grahameke vadantyapi
aśvagandhā'jaśṛṅgī ca sārive dve punarnave
kṣudrā sahā vidārī ca kaṣāyaḥ pariṣecane
palaṅkaṣā sarjarasaḥ kuṣṭhaṃ girikadambakaḥ
devadāru samañjiṣṭhaṃ surā tailaṃ suvarcikā
naladaṃ tumbaru tvak ca samabhāgāni kārayet
etena gātramabhyajya tataḥ saṃpadyate sukhī
aśvakarṇasya puṣpāṇi dhātakyāstindukasya ca
kakubhasya ca puṣpāṇi dāḍimasya dhavasya ca
tvakkṣīrī madhukaṃ caiva kṣīreṇa saha pācayet
tato mātrāṃ pibedbālastataḥ saṃpadyate sukhī
eteṣveva ghṛtaṃ pakvamatīsāramarocakam
hanti tṛṣṇā'rucicchardīḥ śarkarāmadhusaṃyutam
ulūkagṛdhraromāṇi kaṭvalābūstathā'jaṭī
yavāḥ śvetā ghṛtaṃ gavyaṃ peyo'yaṃ revatīnude
varuṇāriṣṭakau cobhau putrañjīvakacitrakau
eteṣāṃ tu tvacaṃ bālo mātā dhātrī ca dhārayet
upadravām̐śca śamayeddṛṣṭvā svaiḥ svaiścikitsitaiḥ
nakṣatre cāsya revatyāṃ puṣṭikarmāṇi kārayet
ataścordhvaṃ pravakṣyāmi pūtanāyāścikitsitam
yaduktaṃ pūrvabhaiṣajyaṃ tacca sarvaṃ pracārayet
asuro dundubhirnāma surāsurabhayaṅkaraḥ
skandamāyodhayanmohāt kruddhaṃ dṛṣṭvā ca ṣaṇmukham
viveśa krauñcasya guhāṃ mātulasya mahāgireḥ
skandastaṃ ca mahāśailaṃ mātulaṃ taṃ ca dānavam
śaktyā jaghāna yugapattato guhavadhādguhaḥ
āsīdvarṇaparibhraṣṭastaṃ sametya surāsurāḥ
diśaḥ samudrāḥ sarito mahābhūtāni toyadāḥ
pūtyarthaṃ dhūpayāmāsustataḥ pūto'bhavadguhaḥ
yasmin deśe tu bhagavān pūtaḥ skando mahābalaḥ
saṃjajñe pūtanā tasmātsarvalokabhayaṅkarī
tāmabravīdguhaḥ puṇyāṃ pūtanāmagrataḥ sthitām
yāhi tvaṃ bhinnamaryādā cetyuktā''ha tathā'stviti
malajā pūtanā kauñjī vaiśvadevī ca pāvanī
pañcanāmeti cāpyuktā śṛṇu tasyāścikitsitam
karañjaśobhāñjanakāvāsphoṭā hyāṭarūṣakaḥ
saptaparṇaśca nimbaśca bhārgī ca pariṣecanam
surāsauvīrakābhyāṃ ca haritālaṃ manaḥśilā
kuṣṭhaṃ sarjarasaṃ caiva tailamabhyañjanaṃ pacet
pippalī pippalīmūlaṃ bṛhatī kaṇṭakārikā
śālaparṇī pṛśniparṇī madhūkaṃ madhukastathā
etaṃ saṃbhṛtya saṃbhāraṃ kṣīre sarpirvipācayet
chardiṃ hikkāṃ ca śamayedetat sarpirneṣevataḥ
kukkuṭasya purīṣaṃ ca keśāścarma purāṇakam
jīrṇāṃ ca bhikṣusaṅghāṭīṃ sarpanirmocanaṃ ghṛtam
dhūpametaṃ prayuñjīta sandhyākāle sukhaṅkaram
anantāṃ kukkuṭīṃ bimbīmariṣṭāmatha karkaṭīm
sūtreṇa grathitā etā dhārayet pāṇipādayoḥ
akṣirogacikitsābhiḥ śamayedandhapūtanām
śītaṅkāracikitsābhiḥ śamayecchītapūtanām
pippalīpippalīmūlacavyacitrakanāgaraiḥ
paced ghṛtaṃ pañcamūlyā saindhavena ca paṇḍitaḥ
dīpanīyamiti proktaṃ sarpiretanmahātmanā
sakṣaudraśarkaraṃ lehyaṃ śamayecchītapūtanām
rāsnā punarnavā kuṣṭhaṃ tagaraṃ devadāru ca
patrāguruhareṇvaśca guḍūcī triphalā sitā
daśamūlaṃ ca taiḥ sarpiḥ pacet kṣīre caturguṇe
viśuddhaṃ lehayedbālaṃ śāmyate kaṭapūtanā
bilvāṅkoṭhau kapitthārkau kārpāsamaṭarūṣakam
uruvūkasya patrāṇi vaṃśasyāśmantakasya ca
prapauṇḍarīkaṃ madhukaṃ śatapuṣpā punarnavā
etaistailaṃ ghṛtaṃ vā'tha payasā yojitaṃ pacet
etena gātramabhyajya sakṣāraṃ pāyayedidam
mṛdvīkāṃ ca payasyāṃ ca śrīparṇīṃ sārivā tathā
madhūkaṃ nāgapuṣpaṃ ca śītapākīyutaṃ pibet
śarkarāmadhusaṃyuktaṃ tadā saṃpadyate sukhī
athāsya dhūpanaṃ dadyāt sāyaṃ prātaratandritaḥ
golomīsarpanimokaṃ vacāṃ siddhārthakām̐stathā
saṃsṛjya sarpiṣā tena dhūpayet sandhyayorbhiṣak
ityandhapūtanāyāstu bilvāṅkoṭhādi bheṣajam
jātamātraṃ purā skandamumāśaṅkarasannidhau
gandhālaṅkārapuṣpādyairmaṇḍayāmāsa ṣaṇmukham
iṣukaṃ citrakaṃ cāsya lalāṭacakṣuṣi vyadhāt
nāsikāgaṇḍacibukavaktre citraviśeṣakān
gandharvāpasarasaścainaṃ ramayanti gaṇāstathā
tato'bravīdbālabhāvānmātaraṃ bhagavān guhaḥ
etameva mahādevajaṭābhāraṃ vibhūṣaṇam
dehīti na ca lebhe taṃ punaḥ punarapi dhruvam
guhastvalabhamānastaṃ ruṣito lalitaḥ sadā
apaviddhya kṣitau sarvaṃ nirmṛjya mukhamaṇḍalam
tataḥ kṣubdhāstrayo lokā naṣṭajñānā vicetasaḥ
dampatī cāpi saṃvignau dadatuścāmṛtodbhavam
tataḥ prabhṛti saptānāṃ candraḥ śirasi dṛśyate
rudraskandādinandīnāṃ revatyāśca mahātmanaḥ
apaviddhaṃ tu yat krodhāt skandena mukhamaṇḍalam
tato grahaḥ sā babhūva dāruṇā mukhamaṇḍikā
nirdahiṣyanniva krodhāttatastāmabravīdguhaḥ
annaṃ kuru mahābhāge saṅkīrṇākārakarmaṇām
tathā'stviti ca sā prāha skandasya paricārikā
evaṃ mukhāci jajñe śṛṇu tasyāścikitsitam
kapitthabilvatarkārībimbīgandharvahastakāḥ
tailametaistu saṃyuktaṃ hitamabhyañjanaṃ śiśoḥ
khuḍḍākaṃ pañcamūlaṃ ca śyonāko'tha madhūlikā
madhūkāni tvacaḥ kṣīrī pippalyastairghṛtaṃ pacet
gavyaṃ kṣīraṃ gavāṃ pakvaṃ śarkarāmadhusaṃyutam
pibet kolamitaṃ bālastataḥ saṃpadyate sukhī
kuṣṭhaṃ sarjarasaṃ caiva yavāḥ sarpiśca dhūpanam
sarpavīrallacāṣāṇāṃ jihvānāṃ dhāraṇaṃ maṇeḥ
jīrṇe bhojanamapyasya tataḥ śastaṃ pradāpayet
ataścordhvaṃ pravakṣyāmi śītapūtanayā'rdite
nādeyī surasā bimbī kapitthaṃ jīvakastathā
nadībhallātakaṃ śyāmā bilvaṃ śītaśivaṃ tathā
etaṃ kaṣāyaṃ niṣkvāthya pariṣiñcet sukhāmbunā
etena pariṣiktasya tailamabhyañjanaṃ śṛṇu
gomūtraṃ bastamūtraṃ ca mustakaṃ devadāru ca
kuṣṭhaṃ ca sarvagandhāśca tailamabhyañjanaṃ pacet
khadiraṃ rohiṇīsāraṃ palāśaṃ kakubhatvacam
etaṃ saṃbhṛtya saṃbhāraṃ kṣīre sarpirvipācayet
tat siddhaṃ lehayet kāle śarkarākṣaudramātrayā
śītapūtanayā grasto mucyate pathyabhojanaḥ
gṛdhrolūkatarakṣūṇāṃ purīṣāṇi samānayet
agniko bastalomāni picumandaśca dhūpanam
śītapūtanayā graste taccedaṃ ca cikitsitam
ata ūrdhvaṃ tu sarveṣāṃ śṛṇu sāmānyabheṣajam
agnimanthaḥ kurabako varuṇaḥ pāribhadrakaḥ
niśā'nalaḥ poṭagalaḥ pūtikā rohiṣastathā
etena pariṣiktasya tailamabhyañjanaṃ śṛṇu
priyaṅgū rocanā caiva śatapuṣpā kuṭannaṭam
tālīsapatraṃ naladaṃ tathā candanasārive
madhūkāṅkoṭhamañjiṣṭhāpṛthvīkābhūtikāni ca
etaistailaṃ samaṃ siddhaṃ mudgāmlodakasaṃyutam
etena bālamabhyaktaṃ muñcatyāśu pitṛgrahaḥ
bimbīkāśmaryamadhukaṃ kulatthā badarā yavāḥ
khuḍḍākapañcamūlasya niṣkvāthaṃ cātra dāpayet
kharjūraṃ mustakaṃ caiva nārikelaphalāni ca
nālikāṅkuramṛdvīkā madhūkaṃ madhukaṃ tathā
etāni śuṣkacūrṇāni ---
plīhahalīmakacikitsitādhyāyaḥ
---do'gnibalasaṃkṣayaḥ
mūrcchā tṛṣṇā bhramastandrī viṣādārucigauravam
tasya pratikriyāṃ kuryādvātapittaharīṃ budhaḥ
guḍūcīsvarase siddhaṃ sakṣīraṃ māhiṣaṃ ghṛtam
upasnigdhaṃ tatastaṃ tu sraṃsayedbalakālavit
rasenāmalakānāṃ tu trivṛdyuktena yuktitaḥ
madhurāṇyavidāhīni viriktaṃ nityamāśayet
durbalasya prayojyā tu nityaṃ guḍaharītakī
raktapittauṣadhaṃ yacca taccāpyatra praśasyate
dhātrīphalānāṃ pakvānāṃ svarasasyāḍhakaṃ bhavet
pippalyo madhukaṃ drākṣā candanośīrabālakam
ghṛtaprasthaṃ pacedetaiḥ pakve dadyācca śarkarām
tallihenmadhunā prātaḥ pathyāśī nīrujo bhavet
etat pittottare kāryaṃ śṛṇu vātottare'pi tu
kalyāṇakaṃ balātailaṃ kaumāraṃ vā prayojayet
kāmalāpāṇḍuśothānāṃ tulyaṃ kuryācca bheṣajam
pathyāśinā ca satataṃ sevyā'gastyaharītakī
iti ha smāha bhagavān kaśyapaḥ
iti plīhāhalīmakacikitsatam
udāvartacikitsitādhyāyaḥ
athāta udāvartacikitsitamadhyāyaṃ vyākhyāsyāmaḥ 1
iti ha smāha bhagavān kaśyapaḥ 2
kaṣāyakaṭutiktarūkṣaśītapūtiśuṣkaśākavallūrapiṇyākasuniṣaṇṇakadugdhikākodravaśyāmākanīvārayavakakaṭṭa vetrāgrakarkandhūkapitthabilvakarīragāṅgerukīlikucapārāvatabhavyakāñjikaśuktāranālatuṣodakamudnakalāyātasīprabhṛtiniṣevaṇādrukṣātmano vātabhūyiṣṭhaprakṛtervegavidhāraṇādanilaḥ prakupito dehamūrdhvamudāmutya vāyunodānena pratyāhato gudamāsādyāśayaṃ kṛtvā'dhovahāni srotāṃsi dūṣayitvā viṇmūtrakaphapittānilaśukramārgānuparuṇaddhi tata ānāhamupajanayati prāṇaharaṃ tallakṣaṇaṃ vā | kṣīramupasevamānasya śiśoraciraṃ vā kaṭīdhāraṇādbastigudasaṃrodhanādatirodanāt prajāgarādasnehātkṣīrānupasevanādvegavidhāriṇyā upavāsapramitāśanaviṣamāśanaprajāgaracitterṣyāvyāyāmanityāyāḥ kṣīramāmodyate vāyunā tat pīyamānamudāvartāya saṃpadyate | tatra ṣaḍudāvartāḥ | vātaviṇmūtraśukracchardikṣavathūnāṃ saṃdhāraṇādapravṛtteśca ṣaṇṇāṃ ṣaḍucyante | tadudāvartasāmānyāttu tamekamevāhureke | teṣāṃ navegāndhāraṇīye'dhyāye lakṣaṇānyauṣadhāni coktāni | tānīhāpi tu dāruṇatvādasya vyādheryatkiñcidupadekṣyāmaḥ | śūlamūrcchādāhānāhādhmānāni pravṛttidveṣo vaivarṇyaṃ saṃjñānāśaskhalanapatanavilapanatṛṣṇāhikkāśvāsaprasvedā'ṅgāraparikartikābhirabhīkṣṇaṃ bādhyate bastigudahṛdayapārśvavaṃkṣaṇodaraśūlamūrusādo vyathā cetsudāvartalakṣaṇāni | pañcādau
pūrvarūpāṇi bhavanti 3
pañcajanamādāvuṣṇalavaṇatailābhyaktaṃ yathāyogaṃ svinnaśarīraṃ phalavartibhirupakramet | visraṃsitaṃ ca saṃtamuṣṇasnigdhamadhuralavaṇaprāyamaśanaṃ yavagodhūmaṣaṣṭikaśākaghṛtaprāyamānūpaudakamāṃsaprāyaṃ vā''hāramanupadravāya baline upakalpayet | kośātakīkaṣṭvalābubījapippalīsaindhavahiṅguvacāharitālamanaḥśilāmāṣacūrṇairgomūtrapiṣṭaiḥ phalavartya upavātā ghṛtākte kaṭutailākte vā gude śalākayā praṇayet | pūrvavadeva copacāraḥ | mrakṣaṇācchādanapariṣekāśanāni tāni cāsya snigdhoṣṇāni vidadhyāt | saktuvāṭyakulmāṣāpūpavāstukayavaśākasudhāpatratrivṛcchākapañcāṅgulaśrīvārikāśrīphalāsuvarcalākākamācīkalāyapālaṅkyādibhiśca śākairghṛtasiddhairbhojayedyavānnam | trivṛtpīluyavotkārān vā gomūtreṇa pāyayet | trivṛddharītakīśyāmāsudhāḥ kṣīreṇa yuktā mūtrairvānāhabhedanam | triphalādantīśyāmātrivṛtkampillakapīlusvarṇakṣīrīvacāsaptalānīlikāgrahaghnīcūrṇāni sudhākṣīreṇa guṭikā āmalakamātrīḥ kṛtvā tata ekāṃ bhakṣayitvoṣṇodakamūtrānupānādānāhairmucyate | etānyeva triphalādīni kṣīramūtravarjitāni pañcakaṭukapañcalavaṇahiṅgukuṣṭhakṣāradvayaśatapuṣpāpāṭhāśrīphalayutāni cūrṇāni kṛtvā biḍālapadakaṃ kṣīramadyoṣṇavārigomūtrānyatamapītamānāhaśūlagulmabhaganda rārśasāṃ nirṇāśanaṃ cūrṇaṃ nārācakamityucyate tat 4
tatra ślokāḥ--
yadi vyatikramedetānudāvarta upakramān
yuktoṣṇalavaṇaṃ tasya nirūhamupakalpayet 5
āsthāpanaṃ ca ---
rājayakṣmacikitsitādhyāyaḥ
--- rogānīkavināśanam |
pippalyo viṃśatiḥ śreṣṭhā udakārdhāḍhake śṛtāḥ
caturbhāgāvaśeṣaṃ taṃ chāgakṣīreṇa tāvatā
śṛtaṃ nityaṃ pibecchoṣī tenaivāśnīta nityadā
vinā vā'nnodakaṃ śaktyā tatpradhāno vimucyate
dvādaśābdānatīto vā snigdhasvinno vaśodhitaḥ
pibetkṣīreṇa pippalyaḥ pañca pañca ca vardhayet
śataṃ tathaiva hrasayedbhojanodakavarjitaḥ
pippalīvardhamānaṃ tu sarvarogavināśanam
pippalīnāṃ śataṃ vā'pi śṛtaṃ toyāḍhake sadā
pādaśiṣṭaṃ samakṣīraṃ śrapayetpunareva tat
kaphādhike tu sakṣaudraṃ saghṛtaṃ pavanādhike
pittottare śarkarayā sevamānaḥ sukhī bhavet
pippalīvardhamānaṃ tu vātaśleṣmottare hitam
sarvatra pippalīkṣīraṃ hitaṃ kālādidarśanāt
śaranmukhe nāgabalāmūlānyuddhṛtya śoṣayet
sannidhāya nave bhāṇḍe vrahmacārī jitendriyaḥ
strīśūdravarjī vijane cūrṇaṃ kṣīreṇa pāyayet
prathame divase karṣaṃ tataścordhvaṃ vivardhayet
tataḥ palaṃ palaṃ nityaṃ pāyayet payasā śuciḥ
jīrṇe tasmin pibet kṣīraṃ bhaktodakavivarjitaḥ
māsātsopadravaṃ śoṣaṃ hanti nāgabalā nṛṇām
prajāmāyurbalaṃ medhāṃ prayatāya dadātyapi
ṣaṇmāsena śrutadharaḥ sarvarogavivarjitaḥ
aśītiko'pi ca yuvā bhavet saṃvatsarānnaraḥ
maṇḍūkaparṇyāḥ śuṇṭhyāśca brāhmyāśca madhukasya ca
tadguṇaḥ sarvarogaghno vidhirnāgabalāsamaḥ
udvartitastvajāleṇḍairajāmūtrābhiṣecitaḥ
ajākṣīraṃ pibennānyadajābhiśca vaset saha
adhastādavaṭe'jānāṃ vasañchoṣī vimucyate
ājaṃ rasāyanaṃ hyetatkṣayaghnaṃ balavardhanam
harītakīnāṃ śreṣṭhānāṃ dve śate jarjarīkṛte
daśamūlasudhādantīkarañjādhoguḍāsanāḥ
mayūrakaṃ devadāru niculaṃ kuṭajāṭajī
kaṭaṅkaṭerī bṛhatī rāsnā śyonākacitrakau
varuṇaṃ ceti saṃkuṭya pañcaviṃśatikaiḥ palaiḥ
ṣaḍdroṇe'pāṃ pacedetadyāvatpañcāḍhakaṃ sthitam
tasmin pūte guḍatulāṃ dattvā bhūyaśca sādhayet
parivṛttaṃ samālakṣya ghṛtabhāṇḍe nidhāpayet
maricāni viḍaṅgāni bhārgīṃ śakrayavāṃstathā
āvapetkuṭabījāni pippalīprasthameva ca
madhuprasthaṃ ca saṃsṛjya māsādūrdhvaṃ prayojayet
pathyāśī mātrayā kāle mucyate kaphajairgadaiḥ
mahābhayāriṣṭa iti kaśyapena prakalpitaḥ
apāmārgo'śvagandhā ca nākulī gaurasarṣapāḥ
tilā bilvaṃ ca kalkaḥ syātkṣayeṣūdvartanaṃ hitam
laśunānāṃ palaśataṃ nistuṣaṃ jarjarīkṛtam
jaladroṇeṣu daśasu śrapayetpādaśeṣitam
ghṛtāḍhakadvayaṃ tatra vipacejjīvanaiḥ saha
ājasya payaso droṇaṃ kvāthaṃ ca daśamūlikam
āvapettadghṛtaṃ gopyaṃ prayojyaṃ māsataḥ param
indrāṇīghṛtamityetadrājayakṣmavināśanam
vandhyāṣaṇḍhakavṛddhānāṃ kāmadaṃ pathyabhojinām
laśunaṃ vā'pi kalpena yathoktenopacārayet
ghṛtasyārdhāḍhake gavye jarjaraṃ laśunāḍhakam
ghṛtabhāṇḍe samāvāpya varṣaṃ dhānyeṣu gopayet
ṣaṇmāsamaṣṭamāsaṃ vā caturmāsamatho tataḥ
peyaṃ nāgabalāvacca sarvarogairvimucyate
indrāṇīghṛtakalpena drākṣāsarpirvipācayet
tathā pīlughṛtaṃ caiva sarvarogairvimucyate
iṣṭayo rohiṇīsnānaṃ tayormaṅgalasevanam
rudrapūjā dhṛtiḥ śaucaṃ brahmacaryaṃ ca śāntaye
ṣaḍekādaśa coktāni yāni rūpāṇi yakṣmiṇaḥ
ta evopadravāsteṣāmaśāntau svaṃ cikitsitam
iti ha smāha bhagavān kaśyapaḥ
iti cikitsiteṣu rājayakṣmacikitsitam
gulmacikitsitādhyāyaḥ
athāto gulmināṃ cikitsitaṃ vyākhyāsyāmaḥ 1
iti ha smāha bhagavān kaśyapaḥ 2
vātādisarvaraktotthāḥ kukṣihṛtpārśvabastijāḥ
pañca pañcāpadāmagryā gulmāḥ sūtre nidarśitāḥ 3
ceṣṭānnapānasāmānyā doṣāḥ prakupitā nṛṇām
āmāśayamadhiṣṭhāya tato gulmān prakurvate 4
vātaleṣvannapāneṣu vātalo yaḥ prasajjati
dhāvati plavate'dhīte bhṛśaṃ gāyati nṛtyati 5
śītavātāmbusevī ca śītarūkṣakaṭupriyaḥ
vyādhikliṣṭaḥ kṛśo rūkṣaḥ sevate tīkṣṇamauṣadham 6
udīrayati cchardiṃ ca balācchardayate'pi vā
niruṇaddhi ca vātādīn tṛptaḥ pibati vā bahu 7
śīghrayānena vā yati vyavāyaṃ vā'tisevate
vyāyāmādhyayanastrīṣu bālo rūkṣaśca yo rataḥ 8
etaiścānyaiśca kupito māruto doṣasaṃcayam
karoti yatra tatrāsya sthāne gulmo nicīyate 9
agnināśo'ruciḥ śūlaṃ cchardyudgārāntrakūjanam
purīṣavartanaṃ kārśyaṃ gulmānāṃ pūrvalakṣaṇam 10
śūlaṃ mūrcchā jvarastodaḥ kārśyaṃ tṛṣṇā'ruṇātmatā
pārśvāṃsākṣakaśūlaṃ ca vātagulmasya lakṣaṇam 11
ūṣāyaṇaṃ jvaro dāhastṛṣṇāviḍbhedepītatāḥ
pittagulme vijānīyāt pittalānnoṣṇasevinaḥ 12
romaharṣo jvaraśchardirarucirhṛdayagrahaḥ
mūtrākṣinakhaviṭśauklyaṃ śaityaṃ ca kaphagulminaḥ 13
sarvāṇyetāni rūpāṇi lakṣyante sānnipātike
raktagulmaḥ striyā yonau jāyate na nṛṇāṃ kvacit 14
duṣprajātā garbhā ca garbhasrūrbahumaithunā
anvakṣagarbhakāmā ca bahuśītārtavā ca yā 15
udāvartanaśīlā ca vātalānnaniṣeviṇī
yā strī tasyāḥ prakupito vāto yoniṃ prapadyate 16
niruṇaddhyārtavaṃ tatra māsikaṃ saṃcinoti ca
rakte ca saṃsthite nārī garbhiṇyasmīti manyate 17
stanamaṇḍalakṛṣṇatvaṃ romarājiḥ sadohadā
garbhiṇīrūpamavyaktaṃ bhajate sarvameva tu 18
vipākapāṇḍukārśyāni bhavantyabhyadhikāni tu
ityevaṃ lakṣaṇaṃ strīṇāṃ raktagulmaṃ pracakṣate 19
anekadoṣasaṃghāto gulmavadgulma ucyate
tridoṣajādṛte gulmāḥ siddhyanti na cirotthitāḥ 20
gulminaṃ prathamaṃ vaidyaḥ snehasvedopapāditam
yathāsvadoṣaśamanairauṣadhaiḥ samupakramet 21
bṛṃhaṇaṃ cātigulmeṣu bhṛśaṃ cātivirūkṣaṇam
atisaṃśodhanaṃ caiva gulmināṃ na praśasyate 22
abhayā pippalī vyoṣaṃ yāvaśūko'tha citrakaḥ
sauvarcalaṃ viḍaṅgāni vacā cetyakṣasaṃmitāḥ 23
samyakpakvaṃ ghṛtaprasthaṃ tatpibecca yathābalam
ghṛtaṃ daśāṅgamityetadvātagulmanivāraṇam 24
saindhavaṃ yāvaśūkaśca pippalī hastipippalī
śuṇṭhī citraka ityeṣāṃ ṣaḍbhāgāḥ palikā pṛthak 25
tulyakṣīraṃ ghṛtaṃ prasthaṃ pakvaṃ ṣaṭpalamucyate
ṣaṭpalaṃ sarvagulmeṣu vaidyāḥ prāhuryathā'mṛtam 26
abhayā pippalī drākṣā guḍūcī sapunarnavā
lavaṇakṣāragandharvabhārgīrāsnārasāñjanam 27
tulyakṣīraṃ pacedetairghṛtamakṣasamairbhiṣak
śaiśukaṃ nāma tat sarpirvātagulmanivāraṇam 28
snigdhasvinnasamāśvastaṃ gulminaṃ sraṃsayettataḥ
virecanena mṛdunā tailenairaṇḍajena vā 29
viriktaṃ ca yathākālaṃ nātirūkṣāṇi bhojayet
yuktāmlalavaṇoṣṇāni yuktasneharasāni ca 30
bhojayedgulminaṃ nityaṃ nidānaguruvarjakam
na cātibhojanaṃ nityaṃ śasyate sarvagulminām 31
pippalīṃ pippalīmūlaṃ cavyaṃ citrakanāgaram
bilvaṃ kapitthaṃ badaraṃ vṛṣakaṃ gaṇikārikām 32
hiṅgudāḍimajīvantīvṛkṣāmlaṃ sāmlavetasam
pauṣkaraṃ śaṭidantyau ca lavaṇāni ca sarvaśaḥ 33
dvau kṣārāvajamodaṃ ca tulyaṃ śuṣkāṇi cūrṇayet
mātuluṅgarasenaite vaṭakā badaropamāḥ 34
kṛtāḥ sukhāmbunā peyā madyairamlena vā bhiṣak
vātagulmamudāvartaṃ plīhaśūlaṃ ca nāśayet 35
mayūrāṃstittirīn krauñcān kapotān vanakukkaṭān
yavagodhūmaśālīṃśca vātagulmī sadā'śniyāt 36
yadi tu snihyamānasya vātagulmo na śāmyati
hitamāsthāpanaṃ tasya tathaivāpyanuvāsanam
kṣīrānupānāmabhayāṃ saguḍāṃ saṃprayojayet 37
gulmināṃ baddhavarcānāṃ ---
kuṣṭhacikitsitādhyāyaḥ
svedo vā'tikharatvamaṅgānāmatiślakṣṇatā vā vaivarṇyaṃ
raukṣyaṃ lomaharṣaḥ pipāsā gauravaṃ rāgo daurbalyaṃ vepathuḥ
piḍakāruṣāṃ saṃbhavaścātivedanā ca kṣatavisarpaṇamiti
tata ūrdhvamakriyāvatāṃ kuṣṭhāni jāyante | tatra śyāvāruṇaśūlakaṇḍūcimicimakharatvapāruṣyasaṃstambhāyāmairvātottarāṇi vidyāt dāhavedanājvaraviḍbhedoṣāyanapākasrāvakoṭhānikarṇā kṣiprotthānaiḥ śītamadhurakaṣāyasarpiranuśayaiśca pittottarāṇi vidyāt śvetapāṇḍughanotsedhagurustaimityastambhamahāparigrahāgnisādaiḥ śītāditarānuśayaiḥ kaphottarāṇi vidyāt vyāviddharūpabahusphuṭitaparisrāvakṛmidāharujopetaśarīrāvayavapātanamaśucivigandhiśothabahulamanekopadravaṃ sānnipātikaṃ raktatvāt kākaṇamityucyate | dvidoṣajānītarāṇi tānyanuvyākhyāmaḥ -- vātottare kapālakuṣṭhaṃ pittottare tvaudumbaraṃ kaphottare maṇḍalakuṣṭhaṃ vātapaittikamṛṣyajihvaṃ pittaślaiṣmikaṃ pauṇḍarīkaṃ sidhmaṃ ca iti samāsalakṣaṇam | vistaratastvaṣṭādaśa kuṣṭhāni tānyanuvyākhyāsyāmaḥ--sidhmaṃ ca vicarcikā ca pāmā ca dadruśca kiṭibhaṃ ca kapālaṃ ca sthūlāruṣkaṃ ca maṇḍalakuṣṭhaṃ ca viṣajaṃ ceti nava sādhyāni pauṇḍarīkaṃ ca śvitraṃ ca ṛṣyajihvaṃ ca śatāruṣkaṃ caudumbaraṃ ca kākaṇaṃ ca carmadalaṃ caikakuṣṭhaṃ ca vipādikā ceti navāsādhyāni | sarvaṃ tu kuṣṭhaṃ tvaṅmāṃsarudhiralasīkāśrayaṃ sparśaghnaṃ ceti vardhamāna ca vairūpyakaraṃ bhavati | tattu rajodhvastamalābuvāraṇapuṣpīpuṣpasadṛśaṃ sidhmaṃ śyāmalohitavraṇavedanāsrāvapākavatī vicarcikā kaṇḍūtodapākasrāvāruṣmatī pāmā raukṣyakaṇḍūdāhasrāvavanti maṇḍalāni vṛddhimanti dadruḥ kṛṣṇaśyāvāruṇakharaparuṣasrāvavṛddhimanti gurūṇi praśāntāni ca punaḥ punarutpadyante kiṭibhāni kṛṣṇakharaparuṣamalinamanekasaṃsthānamaṇḍalaṃ maṇḍūlamṛtusandhiṣūṣṇe cātibādhate kapālākṛti kapālaṃ picchāsrāvavedanādāhakaṇḍūtodajvaravaisarpamahābraṇaparigrahaṃ mṛdukharanibhaṃ mahāruṣkaṃ maṇḍalairbandhujīvakusumopamairdāhakaṇḍūvedanāsrāvavadbhirmaṇḍalakuṣṭhaṃ lūtākīṭapataṅgasarpadaśanadaṣṭamupekṣitaṃ vyabhicāreṇa kharībhavati kṛcchrasādhyaṃ viṣajaṃ mahāśayasamutsedhaṃ jātaṃ cirādbhedi puṇḍarīkapalāśavarṇaṃ pauṇḍarīkaṃ śvetabhāvācchvitraṃ pañcavidhamuttaratropadekṣyāmaḥ ṛṣyajihvopamaṃ pāruṣyavaivarṇyagauravarṇavilkedaiṛrṣyajihvaṃ nīlalohitapītāsitairanekairarudbhiḥ kharaiḥ srāvibhirupadrutaṃ śatāruṣkaṃ pakvodumbaraphalasadṛśamasrāvi jaḍamanekamaudumbaraṃ vyākhyātaṃ kākaṇaṃ hasticarmasadṛśaṃ kharaṃ vṛddhimaccarmadalaṃ vaisarpodbhavaṃ nityavisarpi srāvavedanākrimimadekakuṣṭhaṃ pāṇipādāṅguṣṭhauṣṭhajaṅghādaṇḍadeśeṣusphuṭitasrāvivedanāvatīmavipākinīṃ vipādikāṃ vidyāt | sarvarogāścaiva mohādupekṣyamāṇā asādhyatāṃ yānti asādhyāstvāśu nṛṇāṃ ghnanti tasmādātmahitāyāśu prayateta | kuṣṭheṣvādau vātottareṣu ghṛtācchapānamanekaśo maṇḍāntaritaṃ praśasyate tiktasarpiṣa
itarottarayoḥ vamanavirecanāsthāpana ---
mūtrakṛcchracikitsitādhyāyaḥ
---
kaṭīskandhātidharaṇāt pittaṃ kruddhaṃ kaphānilau
anusṛtya yadā bastiṃ dūṣayanti tadāśrayāḥ
mūtrakṛcchraṃ tadā jantordāruṇaṃ saṃpravartate
saphenamalpamaruṇaṃ kālaṃ vā śūlasaṃtatam
mūtramānaddhavarcastvaṃ vātāghātasya lakṣaṇam
sadāhavedanaṃ pītamatyuṣṇaṃ bāṣpasaṃhitam
svidyamānamukho mūtraṃ kurute paittike śiśuḥ
bahulaṃ kurute mūtramalpabādhaṃ sitaṃ ghanam
bastigauravaśothau ca mūtrāghāte kaphātmake
dvandvajaṃ dvandvarūpebhyaḥ sarvebhyaḥ sānnipātikam
raktajaṃ pittavajjñeyaṃ saraktasya ca mūtraṇāt
viśeṣāḥ sannipātotthe mūrcchābhramavilāpakāḥ
sarveṣu kārśyamaratiraruciḥ sānavasthitiḥ
tṛṣṇāśūlaviṣādātista eva syurupadravāḥ
cirāt pramehāḥ kupyanti sadyaḥ kṛcchrāṇi dehinām
viśeṣaḥ kṛcchramehānāṃ kṛcchre dāho'ti cendriye
kucchrāṇyāśu nivartante pramehāstu prasaṅginaḥ
pittaprāyāṇi kṛcchrāṇi vātasthānāśrayāṇi ca
tasmātsāmānyayogena cikitsā hyupadekṣyate
śaramūlāni niṣkvāthya śītaṃ pūtaṃ ca tajjalam
śarkarāmadhusaṃyuktaṃ pibet kṛcchropaśāntaye
madhukaṃ śaramūlaṃ ca triphalā sitavārikā
śṛtaṃ saśarkarākṣaudraṃ mūtrakṛcchranivāraṇam
tārṇasya pañcamūlasya rasaṃ niṣkvāthya pāyayet
śarkarākṣaudrasaṃyuktaṃ sarvakṛcchranivāraṇam
śatāvarī pṛthakparṇī kulatthabadarāṇi ca
śarkarāmadhusaṃyukto leho mūtragrahāpahaḥ
viparītaṃ pramehebhyo mūtrakṛcchreṣu kalpayet
auṣadhaṃ pānamannaṃ ca susnigdhaṃ mṛdu śodhayet
madhurāṇīkṣuvikṛtīstrapusāni ghṛtaṃ payaḥ
seveta varjayennityaṃ yat saṃgrāhi vidāhi ca
ūṣako'tha bṛhatyau dve śvadaṃṣṭrā vasukāvubhau
śṛṅgaveraṃ yavāścaiva darbho vṛkṣādanī balā
pippalī taiḥ śṛtaṃ kṣīraṃ ghṛtamātrādimūrcchitam
sarakte pāyayetkṛcchre kṣiprametena sidhyati
kanīyasīṃ pañcamūlīṃ kulatthaṃ badarāṇi ca
śarkarāmadhusaṃyukto leho mūtragrahe hitaḥ
sasaindhavo rasaḥ kāryo mūtrāghāte ghṛtāyutaḥ
satārṇapañcamūlo vā rāsnāgokṣurakeṇa vā
dvau karañjau nigarbhā ca kārpāso madhuśigrukaḥ
śvadaṃṣṭrā vasukau dvau ca mṛṇālaṃ cotpalāni ca
pippalyaḥ saindhavaṃ caiva sūkṣmailā maricāni ca
etaiḥ siddhāṃ pibedbālo yavāgūṃ sasuvarcalām
etairevauṣadhairlehaṃ śarkarāmadhusaṃyutam
prayuñjīta ghṛtaṃ caiva pakvaṃ kṛcchranivāraṇam
kanīyasī pañcamūlī pañcakolayavaiḥ saha
kulatthamadhuśigrūṇi kāryaśca satilo bhavet
mandasneho rasastveṣa sauvarcalayuto bhavet
mūtrāghāte prayoktavyaḥ śarkarāsu viśeṣataḥ
ekatridoṣajaiḥ kṛcchraiḥ śarkarāstulyalakṣaṇāḥ
suvarṇacūrṇasadṛśāstathā sarṣapasannibhāḥ
rocaneva gavāṃ pitte saṃbhavantyanilātmanām
vātenonmathitaṃ mūtraṃ khajitaṃ pāpakarmaṇām
śarkarāḥ syurvivṛddhāstā aśmaryaḥ saṃbhavantyatha
tadetallakṣaṇaṃ tāsāṃ nityameva tu vedanā
śarkarā sahamūtreṇa nirdhāvatyapi kasyacit
śalyavatyaśmarī bastau vardhamānā'vatiṣṭhate
kṣīyate kṣīyamāṇasya puṣyamāṇasya puṣyati
tasmānna nityaṃ rujati tasyoddharaṇamiṣyate
aśmaryuddharaṇaṃ tīkṣṇamauṣadhaṃ srota īraṇam
sāhasādatibāleṣu sarvaṃ necchati kaśyapaḥ
iti ha smāha bhagavān kaśyapaḥ
iti cikitsāsthāne mūtrakṛcchracikitsitādhyāyaḥ
atha dvivraṇīyacikitsitādhyāyaḥ
athāto dvivraṇīyaṃ nāmādhyāyaṃ vyākhyāsyāmaḥ 1
iti ha smāha bhagavān kaśyapaḥ 2
sūtrasthāne bhagavatā dvau vraṇau parikīrtitau
tayorvistaramicchāmi śrotuṃ lakṣaṇameva ca 3
anugrahāya bālānāṃ ceṣṭāhārauṣadhāni ca
iti pṛṣṭaḥ sa śiṣyeṇa saṃpūjyāha prajāpatiḥ 4
taratantrasya samayaṃ prabruvanna ca vistaram
na śobhate satāṃ madhye lubdhaḥ kāka ivācitaḥ 5
avaśyaṃ bhiṣajā tvetajjñātavyamanasūyayā
tasmātsamayamātraṃ bho śṛṇu bālahitepsayā 6
atha khalu dvau vraṇau nijaścāgantuśca | nijo vātādyekaikasarvadvandvajaḥ | kṣatabhaṅgaviddhapāṭitadagdhacchinnaniṣpiṣṭābhirūnaśastratṛṇakāṣṭhāgniviṣadantanakhaśāpamantramūlakarmādija āgantuḥ | tasya nijavadeva
lakṣaṇamauṣadhaṃ ca svatarkeṇānuvidadhyāt 7
stambhakāṭhinyālpasrāvaśūlatodasphuraṇakaṣāyāsyatvairvātikaṃ vidyāt jvaradāhamohatṛṣṇāśupākalauhityāvadāraṇārucidaurgandhyaiḥ paittikaṃ vidyāt staimityaśaityamārdavamandavedanāsnehapāṇḍavacirakāritvātisrāvaiḥ
kaphajaṃ vidyāt sarvarūpaṃ sānnipātikaṃ dvidoṣaṃ saṃsṛṣṭaṃ vidyāt 8
ityatra ślokaḥ--
sarvavraṇānāṃ prakṛtiniruktā doṣadarśanāt
na hi doṣānanāśritya vraṇaḥ kaściccharīriṇaḥ 9
teṣāmupakramaṃ dhātrībālanigrahau saṃśamanaṃ bandhanam utklinnaprakṣālanaṃ kalkapraṇidhānaṃ śodhanaṃ ropaṇaṃ savarṇīkaraṇam ityetaiḥ snehapānasaṃbhojanopanāhasvedoṣṇapariṣekamadhurāmlalavaṇairvātavraṇaṃ śītodakadugdhapariṣekaśītapralepanamadhurakaṣāyatiktakalkaghṛtapānamudgaśālijāṅgalopacārairuṣṇāmlakaṭulavaṇabandhanasaṃpūraṇavarjanaiśca paittikavraṇam uṣṇatīkṣṇatiktakaṭukaṣāyakṣārasaṃśodhanopanāhasvedanoṣṇavāripariṣekalaṅghanabandhanasrāvaṇaiḥ kaphavraṇaṃ śamayet | ato yuktyetarān |
srāvaṇapāṭanadahanasīvanaiṣaṇasāhasādīnyatibāleṣu na kuryāditi 10
atra ślokāḥ--
jīrṇaiḥ prakṣālitairvastraistathā baddhaṃ nidhāpayet
yathauṣadhaṃ na patati bālakaṃ ca na pīḍayet 11
vaisarpaḥ śvayathurdāho jvarastṛḍatibandhanāt
śithilādanavasthānaṃ madhyamastu praśasyate 12
vātārkatṛṇakāṣṭhāmbumakṣikādibhayādvraṇam
bandho rakṣati śīghraṃ ca dahyate na ca khādati 13
jvaravaisarpadāhārtaṃ raktapittolvaṇaṃ vraṇam
na badhnīyāddvirahnastu sarvaṃ prakṣālayedvraṇam 14
dve haridre tilāḥ sarpiḥ saindhavaṃ madhukaṃ trivṛt
ariṣṭapatramityeṣa kalkaḥ śodhanaropaṇaḥ 15
śodhane ropaṇe caiva yuktyā kṣaudrarasakriyā
tatra nirvāpaṇe coktā ghṛtenodakasaktavaḥ 16
samaṅgādhātakīpuṣpamagrasthāmalakītvacam
ghṛtaṃ kṛṣṇāstilā māṃsī kalko'yaṃ vraṇaropaṇaḥ 17
etairevauṣadhaiḥ sarvaiḥ sarpistailamatho pacet
vraṇaropaṇamityāhuḥ kaṭphalaṃ vā'vacūrṇitam 18
jvaradāhapipāsārtyā pacyamānaṃ vraṇaṃ vadet
teṣāṃ nivṛttau jānīyāt pakvaṃ pīnonnatākṛtim 19
marmasthaścedupekṣyaḥ syādbālaṃ dhātrīṃ ca pūrayet
godadhnā mrakṣitaṃ cainaṃ badhnīyāllavaṇānvitam 20
amarmajaṃ pāṭayedvā netyeke pūrvadarśanāt
raktakṣayādalpabhāvāddhanyādbālaṃ kupaṇḍitaḥ 21
pāṭitaṃ vā pra---
---mañjiṣṭhātha manaḥśilā |
pralepaḥ saghṛtakṣaudraḥ savarṇakaraṇaḥ param
triphalā jātipuṣpāṇi kāsīsaṃ lohapatrikā
lepaḥ sagomayarasaḥ savarṇakaraṇaḥ param
catuṣpadānāṃ tvagromakhuraśṛṅgāsthibhasmanā
tailāktā cūrṇitā bhūmirbhavellomaruhā punaḥ
atha khalu bālānāmaṣṭau piḍakāḥ svaśarīradoṣāt svadoṣāccotpadyante | tāsāṃ nidānalakṣaṇe prokte nāmarūpacikitsāṃ ca brūmaḥ -- śarāvikā ca kacchapikā ca jālinī ca tāḥ kaphaprāyāḥ sarṣapikā cā'lajī ca vidradhiśca tāḥ pittādhikāḥ vinatā vātādhikā sarvadoṣajā tvaruṃṣikā | tāsāṃ lakṣaṇānimadhye nimnā śarāvikā ślakṣṇonnatā kacchapikā sirājālatanucchidravatī jālinī sarṣapābhā'lpā'śupākinī bahulā vā sarṣapikā bahūpadravā''śupākavaisarpā'lajī vinatā tūdare pṛṣṭhe vā'vagāḍhanīlā rujāvatī māṃsapākastu pittaprakopo vā utpadyate sa eva sandhiṣu marmasu vā vidradhirityucyate vidahyāśu aṅgaṃ vidīryata iti vidradhiḥ sā bahirantaścotpadyate te cobhe bālānāṃ kṛcchrasādhye | tridoṣajā tvaruṃṣikā caturvidhā doṣabhedādekaikādhikasamadoṣatvāt śūlatodāṭopasphuraṇānāhapāmā vātaliṅgāni jvaratṛṣṇādāhamohamadapralāpāḥ pittaliṅgāni śaityapaicchilyabahukledārucistaimityāni kaphaliṅgāni sarvaiḥ samadoṣatvam anyatrāpi ca vraṇe pūrvoktāni ca
lakṣaṇāni
tatra ślokāḥ--
pūrvaṃ sarāvikādyāsu susnigdhasya virecanam
śasyate ca bhiṣak tāsu vraṇakarma yathocitam
nivartanamapakvāsu piḍakāsu prayojayet
pariṣekaiḥ pralepaiśca ghṛtapānaihitāśanaiḥ
aruṃṣikāsu satataṃ śiraso muṇḍanaṃ hitam
snāpanaṃ mrakṣaṇaṃ caiva vraṇatailairanekaśaḥ
dve haridre trikaṭukaṃ saindhavaṃ vā manaḥśilā
suvarṇajo japā jātirvacā kuṣṭhaṃ rasakriyā
aśvaghnamūlodakaṇādaśamūlaṃ phalatrayam
etairgomūtrasaṃyuktaiḥ pramṛdvīyādaruṃṣikām
etaireva pacettailaṃ hanti taccāpyaruṃṣikām
atha cedvedanāṃ dadyāttilairudvartayettataḥ
svādunā vraṇatailena navanītena vā dihet
atho salohitāṃ chinnāṃ kṣureṇāruṃṣikāṃ bhiṣak
tulyābhyāṃ kṣīramūtrābhyāṃ siddhoṣṇābhyāṃ pralepayet
na cedevaṃ nivarteran srāvaṇaṃ tu tataḥ param
yadā pakveṣṭakācūrṇairabhīkṣṇaṃ guṇḍyate śiśuḥ
trapusairvārubījaṃ vā khādato'ṅgeṣu śuṣyati
medo'bhivardhanaṃ cānnaṃ divāsvapnaṃ ca sevate
tasya medaḥ prakupitaṃ vāyunā tvacamāhṛtam
medaḥpūrṇatvacānaddhā janayatyarakīlikāḥ
lavakartanataścaitā dṛśyante ca kvacit kvacit
karkandhugostanaprakhyā vardhamānā bhavanti ca
tāsāṃ dahanamevāge?ra taptaiḥ snehairguḍena vā
ekaikaśo hitaṃ jantośchitvā vā kṣārasāraṇam
bandhanaṃ kṣārasūtrairvā vraṇakarma tataḥ param
viruddhāddhyaśanapūtiparyuṣitātyuṣṇaviṣamāśanāttura ---

---śālipiṣṭakasādhitam |
śītaṃ saśarkarakṣaudranavanītaṃ---
---lehayennavanītaṃ vā lehayedvā tapaścitam |
dīptāgnimavasannāgni stanyenaiva tu dhārayet
capalānāṃ tu bālānāṃ sarpatāṃ vā tathā bhṛśam
tṛṇakāṣṭheṣṭakāśastrairanyairvā'pi kṣataṃ bhavet
āmacchedavade dāruṇastasya cātyayaḥ
saṃdadhyāttaṃ ninirmṛjya siñceduṣṇodakena ca
athātirudhirasrāve saṃstambhyaḥ śītavāriṇā
svedayedvā prasaṅge tu dahanaṃ kṣārameva vā
sahitaṃ madhusarpirbhyāṃ badhnīyāt pathyabhojinaḥ
ubhayorghṛtapānaṃ ca vidadhyādvraṇavat kriyām
śītakāleṣu bhūyiṣṭhaṃ bālānāṃ kukṣiśāyinām
svamūtropahatāṅgānāṃ mūtrasaṃklinnavāsasām
tṛṇeṣu vā śayānānāṃ snānodvartanavarjinām
kṛmimatkuṇayūkānāṃ saṃbhavāttaiśca bhakṣaṇāt
gātraṃ dadrulatāṃ yāti kaṭideśe viśeṣataḥ
mrakṣaṇodvartanasnānaṃ gandhadhūpaniṣevaṇam
bālānāṃ śasyate tatra śayyāyāśca vikalpanam
nityameva tu bālānāṃ niśi snehavimardanam
hitaṃ nidrākaraṃ balyaṃ vardhanaṃ śramanāśanam
tasmācca śasyate nityaṃ bālānāṃ parimardanam
mahāsenasya tuṣṭyarthaṃ sṛṣṭaḥ śakreṇa dhīmatā
kuñjaro dussaho nāma airāvaṇabaladyutiḥ
sa skandenopavāhyaśca kṛtaḥ śākhaviśākhayoḥ
ābhyāṃ paramatuṣṭābhyāṃ grāmapo'styaśmabhiḥ kṛtaḥ
upagrahāṇāṃ sarveṣāmādhipatyaṃ ca lambhitaḥ
sa yadā krudhyate jantoḥ pūjākāleṣvapūjitaḥ
pakṣacchidreṣu saṃdhyāsu samājeṣūtsaveṣu ca
svapne trāsayate bālaṃ caturdaṃṣṭro mahāgajaḥ
subuddhyate trāsyamānaḥ sahasā vitrasan drutam
yatraitamaṅgaṃ spṛśati gaṇḍastatrāsya jāyate
medolasīkāpūrṇāni prasajyante bahūnyapi
pacyante kānicitteṣāṃ nirāyāntyaparāṇyapi
duḥsahaṃ pūjayettatra pañcamyāṃ nāgasattamam
kulocitena nyāyena tathā naśyanti tānyapi
ghṛtakṣīrāśino nityaṃ ślaiṣmikasyātibhojinaḥ
svapato māṃsamedo'sṛgvṛddhaḥ saṃvartate gadaḥ
tasmānmātāsutau cātra vamanenopapādayet
śālyannamudgamaṇḍāṃstu saptāhaṃ copacārayet
aśāmyatsu vivardhatsu śaradāho'pi śasyate
tathaiṣāṃ chidyate mūlaṃ pakveṣu vraṇavat kriyā
iti vividharogabheṣajaṃ muniḥ
śiśujanahitāya kaśyapo'bravīt
tadidamupalakṣya paṇḍito bhiṣa
kchiśujanahitāya dhārayet sadā
iti ha smāha bhagavān kaśyapaḥ
iti cikitsāsthāne dvivraṇīyo'dhyāyaḥ
pratiśyāyacikitsitādhyāyaḥ
athātaḥ pratiśyāyacikitsitaṃ vyākhyāsyāmaḥ 1
iti ha smāha bhagavān kaśyapaḥ 2
gurumadhuraśītarūkṣābhyavahārāt satataṃ dvividhaṃ vā stanyaṃ pītvā pītvā svapato nityaṃ gurutvājīrṇayośca snānāt saśleṣmaṇaśca śītodakapānādavagāhanācca bhukte cātipibato vegavidhāraṇācca satataṃ saṃruddhavegasyābhyavahārācca nityaṃ cānupahitaśāyino'tipārśvaśayanaśāyino'pāvṛtamukhaśāyino'nyaiśca nidānairmandāgnerviṣamāśino vātaḥ prakupita ūrdhvakaphāśayaṃ pradūṣya srotāṃsi pratiśyāyayati sa yadā mukhasrotāṃsi dūṣayati tadā mukharogājāyante yadā śrotraṃ tadā karṇarogāḥ yadā nāsikāmūlaṃ prati kaphaṃ pittamasṛgvā śyāyayati tadā pratiśyāya
ityucyate 3
tasya pratinaddhā iva śiromukhanāsikā bhavantīṣṭāniṣṭāvyaktāśca gandhāḥ --- te tasya vātāt pratibandhaḥ kaphādavaiśadyaṃ raktān parikledaḥ
pittāddaurgandhyaṃ srotasa upajāyate 4
sa etadavastho jāḍyārocakaṃ hṛllāsapratighātārthaṃ bhṛśoṣṇatīkṣṇāmlalavaṇeṣu prasajyate tato'sya pittaṃ prakupyati | balābhivardhanāttasya jvaraṃ tṛṣṇāmantardāhamarati --- nī srotasāṃ vaigandhyaṃ
pākaṃ ca divākarāvartaṃ cotpādayati 5
ataścainaṃ caturvidhamṛṣayo vadanti -- vātikaḥ paittikaḥ ślaiṣmikaḥ sānnipātika iti | tadyathā -- yo rauti na ramate jāgartyabhīkṣṇaṃ kṣauti nāsikā cottānasyāpi tanuśleṣma ---rbhavati snigdhoṣṇalavaṇāmlopaśayi cettaṃ pratiśyāyaṃ vātikaṃ vidyāt jvaradāhapipāsāpralāpitātāluśoṣamukhanāsikākṣipākairāśukaphasaṃpākaiḥ---paittikaṃ vidyāt cirakāritvārocakahṛllāsaśirogauravātisrāvamandakṣavathumanyāgrahahṛdayapralepāvipākairuṣṇakaṭukaṣāyarūkṣaṇopaśayaiḥ pratiśyāyaṃ kaphajaṃ vidyāt sarvarūpaṃ srotovaigandhyakṛmi--- sānnipātikaṃ vidyāt 6
tatra ślokāḥ--
vātaśleṣmottaraḥ prāyaḥ pratiśyāyastridoṣajaḥ

---pratiśyāyādvimucyate

uroghātacikitsitādhyāyaḥ

pittaśleṣmottaro vyādhiruroghātastridoṣajaḥ
tasmāt pittakaphaghnāni dhātrī nityaṃ samācaret
iti cikitsāsthāne uroghātacikitsitam
atha śophacikitsitādhyāyaḥ
athātaḥ śophacikitsitaṃ vyākhyāsyāmaḥ 1
iti ha smāha bhagavān kaśyapaḥ 2
vamanavirecanopavāsavyādhikarśanāpathyājīrṇeṣu yaḥ sadā'tyarthalavaṇāmlakaṭukṣāroṣṇopasevī bhavati --- yanānte vā lavaṇādiṣu prasajyate yatheṣṭaṃ ca śītodakasnānapānaśayanavyavāyavyāyāmādibhirvyabhicarati tathā tadguṇakṣīrā vā bhavati tasyāḥ śvayathurnāma roga utpadyate dāruṇaścaturvidhaḥ | doṣā hyasyāḥ -- pathaḥ kṛṣṇāruṇavarṇo'lpo rūkṣaḥ pipīlikāpūrṇa iva savedanaḥ pīḍitaśca nimno bhavatyanimittaścoṣṇasnehābhyāṃ praśāmyati
taṃ vātikaṃ vidyāt nīlalohitapīta ---
kṛmicikitsitādhyāyaḥ
--- ca lābhataḥ samupānayet
pādaśeṣe jaladroṇe śṛte sarpirvipācayet
prasthaṃ saindhavasaṃyuktaṃ tat paraṃ kṛmināśanam
viḍaṅgaghṛtamityetallehyaṃ śarkarayā saha
sarvakṛmīn praṇudati vajro mukta ivāsurān
tiktoṣṇakaṭurūkṣāṇāṃ mūtrāṇāṃ lavaṇasya ca
snehasvedopasevā ca pathyaṃ ca kṛmināśane
bahiḥkṛmīṇāṃ snānādyaṃ dvivraṇīye prakīrtitam
atibālasya tu śiśordhātrī sarvaṃ samācaret
saṃśodhanairviśuddhaṃ ca pathyānnaiśca laghūkṛtam
bhāvitaṃ cauṣadhaiḥ kṣīramamṛtatvāya kalpate
atyuṣṇaṃ kaṭutailaṃ tu gude dattvā sasaindhavam
svedayed gudamaṅgulyā tathā''śu labhate sukham

madātyayacikitsitādhyāyaḥ
athātaḥ pānātyayacikitsitaṃ vyākhyāsyāmaḥ 1
iti ha smāha bhagavān kaśyapaḥ 2
madyapravṛttau rogastu procyate trividho nṛṇām
pānātyayo vibhramaśca pānāpakrama eva ca 3
tatra yo'dhyuṣite pāne tvajīrṇe pibate naraḥ
tat pānamatyayaṃ yāti tasmāt pānātyayo mataḥ 4
vibhrāntānāṃ tu pānānāṃ sevanātpānavibhramaḥ
sahasā pānavicchedaḥ pānāpakrama ucyate 5
dīpanaṃ rocanaṃ vṛṣyaṃ rativaiśadyakārakam
kārśyacittaśramaharaṃ harṣaṇaṃ balavardhanam 6
yuktopasevitaṃ madyaṃ sāttvikānāṃ viśeṣataḥ
pramodārogyapuṣṭīnāṃ tanmūlaṃ cānnabhojanam 7
stanyakṣaye ca dhātrīṇāmutpanne cāpya---
---vātaśūleṣu śītake viṣamajvare 8

dantajanmani bālānāṃ pīḍitānāṃ pipāsayā
tālukaṇṭhauṣṭhaśoṣe ca rodite'tiprajāgare 10
vātaśleṣmātmake vyādhau madyamāhuryathā'mṛtam
tadevātiprasaṅgena pīyamānaṃ punaḥ punaḥ 11

unmatta iva cābhāti pavanotthe madātyaye 19

upodikāṃ takrasiddhāṃ siddhāṃ vā guḍacukrayoḥ 34
evaṃvidhāṃ tvavakṣīrīṃ pānātyayanipīḍitam

---deva prakīrtitam

vidhānaṃ kīrtitaṃ paṇyaṃ ṣaṇḍhabandhyāprajākaram
yonināsāmukhaśrotradaurgandhye picchileṣu ca
kapitthatailaṃ picubhirdhārayeyuḥ sadā striyaḥ
sahakārarasenāpi tadvattailaṃ praśasyate
vividhānāṃ ca tailānāṃ hṛdyā---
---ndhānāṃ ca niṣevaṇam
ata ūrdhvaṃ pravakṣyāmi dhātūṇāṃ śeṣakarma yat
snehapānāt prasūtānāṃ ṣaṣṭhīmallakabhakṣaṇāt
atimātrāśanāccaiva viruddhājīrṇabhojanāt
ṣaṣṭhigrahaḥ kumārāṇāṃ jāyate dehanāśanaḥ
asādhyaścānuṣaṅgī ca ---
---mitā hārā dharmaśīlā tapasvinī
jīrṇāśinī ca satataṃ ṣaṣṭhīmatitaratyasau
prāptāyāṃ vañcanāyāṃ ca pañcakarmāṇi kārayet
athavā tapasogreṇa śivaṃ skandaṃ ca toṣayet
ajīrṇaṃ cāpi dhātrīṇāṃ nityameva na śasyate
ajīrṇadūṣitā doṣā dhātrīṇāṃ janayanti hi
---'ruciglānimadamohavicarcikāḥ
pāmākuṣṭhālajīgulmahṛdrogaśvāsakāsakāḥ
hikkātandrāśramaśvāsacchardyapasmāravigrahāḥ
raktapittabhramonmādaśūlaśoṣagalagrahāḥ
ūrustambhaḥ sasaṃnyāsastathā'nye ca mahāgadāḥ
---
mitapathyāśanānmātuḥ putre teṣāmasaṃbhavaḥ
sukhodayaśca dhātrīṇāṃ tasmāttadupapādayet
vṛddhajīvaka loke'smistrayo duṣkarakāriṇaḥ
bhiṣagdhātrī ca bālaśca ta eva sukhaduḥkhitāḥ
parijñānaṃ vinā vātādyoṣadhakalpane śiśoḥ
---
śāstrānusāraceṣṭābhiriṅgitairnityadarśanaiḥ
karttavyaṃ bheṣajaṃ bāle sa kathaṃ nā'parādhnuyāt
bhiṣakkaumārabhṛtyastaiḥ kāraṇairnityaduḥkhitaḥ
duṣkaraṃ cāpi kurute yuñjan sādhāraṇāḥ kriyāḥ
garbhiṇyāḥ saha garbheṇa dhātryāḥ saha sutena ca
---jñātumadūṣakam
dhātrī putraśarīrārthaṃ svaśarīropaśoṣaṇam
snehātprāpnoti subahūn kleśāṃścānyān sudāruṇān
āśāsnehakṛpādharmādrakṣaṇārthaṃ ca mātaraḥ
sahante sarvaduḥkhāni māninī cātra kīrtyate
garbhān prabhṛti bālo'pi ---
adhikaṃ pīḍyate duḥkhairakurvannapi tat svayam
tasmācca dhātrī satataṃ śubhaceṣṭā'śanasthitiḥ
mātā bhavati putrāṇāṃ bhuṃkte putraphalāni ca
iti ha smāha bhagavān kaśyapaḥ
iti vṛddhajīvakīye kumārabhṛtye cikitsāsthāne dhātrīcikitsitādhyāyaḥ
samāptāni ca cikitsitāni
<6>
atha saptamaṃ siddhisthānam
rājaputrīyā siddhirnāma prathamo'dhyāyaḥ
athāto rājaputrīyāṃ siddhiṃ vakṣyāmaḥ 1
iti ha smāha bhagavān kaśyapaḥ 2
bhagavan rājaputrāṇāmanyeṣāṃ vā mahātmanām
kativatsara yuktasya bastikarma samīritam 3
snehapramāṇaṃ kiṃ bastau bastayaḥ ke ca taddhitāḥ
keṣu rogeṣu śasyante bastayaḥ keṣu garhitāḥ 4
āsthāpanamavasthāyāṃ kasyāṃ keṣu gadeṣu ca
bastikarmapramāṇaṃ kiṃ --- 5
---
hitāhitaṃ vyāpadaḥ kāḥ kiṃ ca tāsāṃ cikitsitam 6
ṛṣimadhye tathā pṛṣṭaḥ praśnān provāca kaśyapaḥ
nibodha samyagbhavatā mahatkāryaṃ pracoditam 7
bastikarma hi durjñānaṃ bālakeṣu viśeṣataḥ
--- 8
śiśūnāmaśiśūnāṃ ca bastikarmāmṛtaṃ yathā
bhiṣajāmarthayaśasī śiśorāyuḥ prajāṃ pituḥ 9
trayamekapade hanti bheṣajaṃ duranuṣṭhitam
tasmādāpannarogeṣu vātaprāyeṣu dehiṣu 10
---
janmaprabhṛti bālānāṃ bastikarmopakalpayet 11
ityāha gārgyo netyāha bālatvādi dati māṭharaḥ
māsena śasyate māsādbālo hi syādavasthitaḥ 12
alpāntaratvānnetyāha tamātreyaḥ punarvasuḥ
caturmāsyo'nuvāsya stu--- 13
--- prāha stanyakṣīraghṛtāśanāt
ūrdhvādho bṛṃhamāṇastu rogaiḥ saṃtarpaṇodbhavaiḥ
kṛcchrasādhyo bhavedgrastastasmāt saṃvatsarāddhitam
tadā śakyaśca śaktaśca bālo vrajati jalpati
mati ---
---nnabhuk
pārāśaryastu netyāha tadā durlalito hi saḥ
trivarṣasyaiva tu hitaṃ neti bhelastamabravīt
alpāntaratvādvyāghātādvibhramāṇāmasaṃsahāt
ṣaḍvarṣaprabhṛtīnāṃ tu bhela ---|
---ṣu pakṣeṣu sūkṣmeṣvapi punaḥ punaḥ
niścayārthaṃ tataḥ sarve kaśyapaṃ paryacodayan
sa tebhyo niścayaṃ prāha śiśūnāṃ bastikarmaṇi
adhastano'nnabhoktā ca cayadāvā---
---bhiṣak puṇyāhe kanakarajatatāmrakāṃsyatrapusīsalohagajadantataruveṇuśṛṅgāsthinalānāmanyatamasyopapattyāmatrakaṃ kārayecchlakṣṇamavraṇamṛju gulikāmukhaṃ gopuccha---noṣṇodakapariṣekagandhaśuklavasanakusumarocanāpratisarābhyalaṅkaraṇabhojanavinodān krameṇa sukhānāpnuyāt | mrakṣaṇotsādanoṣṇodakapariṣekabhojanānyahanyahani prāganuvāsanādiṣyante | paścānnirūhebhya a---koṣṇodakopacārabrahmacaryopasevanaṃ ca
viriktavaditi pariṣat
tatra ślokāḥ--
etena vidhinā bastīn dadyādekāntaraṃ bhiṣak
ahanyahani bastīnāṃ praṇidhānaṃ vināśanam
sneho guruḥ svabhāvena bahutvādba---
---cchūlaṃ jvaro'ruciḥ
ānāhādhmānakṛmayo viḍbhedaḥ kuṣṭhasaṃbhavaḥ
apasmārajaḍatvādyāstasmādekāntaraṃ hitaḥ
ekāntaramapi prāpte bastau deye śarīriṇām
na deyo dhātuvaiṣamye dhātūneva sa sādayet
--- vicakṣaṇaḥ
nirūhayedarthatastu hrāsavṛddhyā nirūhayan
jaḍībhavanti srotāṃsi snehadānātpunaḥ punaḥ
udghāṭanārthaṃ śuddhyarthaṃ teṣāmāsthāpanaṃ hitam
nirūhakāle saṃprāpte yo bālo na nirūhyate
sa ---
svinnaṃ paryuṣitaṃ jīrṇaṃ nivātaśayanādikam
svabhyaktamakṛtāhāraṃ bhiṣagbālaṃ nirūhayet
vātaṃ mūtraṃ purīṣaṃ ca dehināṃ viṣamasthitam
anulomayate śīghraṃ nirūhaḥ sādhu yojitaḥ
āmaṃ jaḍatvamaraciṃ viṣṭambhaṃ do ---
--- dīpayatyapi
vastrasya dhāvanamiva darpaṇasyeva mārjanam
āsthāpanaṃ nṛṇāṃ tadvat prājñaiḥ kālopapāditam
anuvāsanavaccāsya vidhiḥ sarvaḥ praśasyate
nirūhaḥ punarāvṛttānna tu vegān vidhārayet
nirupadravamāśvastaṃ ---
---ṇāṃ vikāre snānamiṣyate
hṛdroge pārśvaśūleṣu kuṣṭheṣu kṛmikoṣṭhiṣu
pramehodaragulmeṣu vātaśūle sakuṇḍale
saṃsṛṣṭadoṣarogeṣu līnagambhīrageṣu ca
rakte śleṣmaṇi vā duṣṭe nirūhamupakalpayet
---yet
ākeśāgranakhāgrebhyo bastirbṛṃhayate narān
varṇatejobalakaramāyuṣyaṃ śukravardhanam
yoniprasādanaṃ dhanyaṃ vandhyānāmapi putradam
bastikarma kṛtaṃ kāle bālānāmamṛtopamam
vātikān vātasaṃsṛṣṭān ---
---habījaṃ sarvaṃ bastirapohati
yāsāṃ ca garbhāḥ sraṃsante jātā vā na dṛḍhāḥ sutāḥ
sukumāryaśca yā nāryaḥ subhagā nityamaithunāḥ
bahustrīkāśca ye bālā īśvarāṇāmayauvanāḥ
saṃkṣīyante'tisaṅgādvā ye ca ---
--- teṣāṃ praśastamamṛtaṃ yathā
iti ha smāha bhagavān kaśyapaḥ
iti siddhisthāne rājaputrīyā siddhirnāma prathamo'dhyāyaḥ 1
trilakṣaṇā siddhirnāma dvitīyo'dhyāyaḥ
athātastrilakṣaṇaṃ siddhiṃ vyākhyāsyāmaḥ 1
iti ha smāha bhagavān kaśyapaḥ 2
trividhaṃ lakṣaṇaṃ paśyennṛṇāṃ pañcasu karmasu
duryo---
---ca paṇḍitaḥ
śarīrayātrāṃ kāyāgniṃ śaktiṃ varṇaṃ balaṃ svaram
doṣāṃśca vikṛtān dṛṣṭvā yathādoṣaṃ viśodhayet
sarvadoṣāḥ praśāmyanti balamāyurvapurvayaḥ
agniḥ prajāśca ---
virecanena śuddhyanti prasīdantīndriyāṇi ca
dhātavaśca viśuddhyante bījaṃ bhavati kārmukam
medodaurgandhyakaphajai rogairvāntaśca mucyate
rogopaśāntirhrāso vā viśuddhilāghavāsu ---
āmāśayasya pūrṇatvaṃ gauravaṃ hṛdayasya ca
śītajvarāgamādhmānaṃ ṣṭhīvanaṃ ca muhurmuhuḥ
śirograho'rucirjāḍyamagnisādo'tinidratā
ālasyaṃ vyādhivṛddhiśca vidyāddurvāntalakṣaṇam
--- sāṃ ca vyathanamativāntasya lakṣaṇam
yadā tu pittaṃ raktaṃ vā purīṣaṃ miśrameva vā
vamatyavirasaṃ śūlī na sa siddhyati kurvataḥ
āmapakvāśayau pittaṃ gudo garbhāśayāsṛjī
vireka ---
---kṣudvātamūtrānulomatā
lāghavaṃ cāgnidīptiśca samyaksraṃsitalakṣaṇam
kṛcchraviṇmūtratā tvakṣapiḍakā jvarasaṃbhavaḥ
arucirgauravādhmāne durviriktasya lakṣaṇam
mūrcchā śūlaṃ gudabhraṃśo vāta ---
bṛṃhaṇaṃ karśanaṃ caiva dvividhaṃ nasyakarma tu
bṛṃhaṇaṃ vātarukprāye kaphādhikye tu karśanam
bṛṃhaṇaṃ vividhaiḥ snehairmadhurauṣadhasaṃskṛtaiḥ
sūkṣairvā kaṭusiddhairvā snehaiḥ karśanamucyate
te guṇā bṛhaṇa ---
--- nasyamucyate
rogaśāntiḥ pramodaśca dehayātrānuvartanam

unmādavātapittām̐śca ---
--- hṛddravau

---ṇam

---bastayaḥ karmasaṃjñitāḥ
antareṣu nirūhāḥ syurataścordhvaṃ na dāpayet

iti siddhisthāne trilakṣaṇāsiddhirnāma dvitīyo'dhyāyaḥ 2

athāto vamanavirecanīyāṃ siddhiṃ vyākhyāsyāmaḥ 1
iti ha smāha bhagavān kaśyapaḥ 2
mayasvalakṣaṇairvamanenopapādayedvidhivadupasnigdhamagnibalāvekṣī vā yathāśaktisvinnamuṣitamaparedyurvijīrṇabhaktaṃ prātareva dantakāṣṭhādyanu--kaṭaphalaniculaśirīṣādīnāṃ lābhataḥ pallavān sarvaśo vā''hṛtyāpāṃ droṇamātre'dhike vāriṇyutkvāthya grahaghnīkṛtavedhanavacāsaindhavapippalīvatsakatrapusamadanabījā --- upaviṣṭamāsane prāṅmukho bhiṣagāloḍya taṃ kaṣāyaṃ grahaghnyādikalkairnātighanoṣṇaśītamākaṇṭhāt pāyayet yathābalamariṣṭaṃ vā pañcaṣaṭkālottaramatha vidhāyotpalakumuda---na vegamutpannamavagṛhṇīyāt na vegāntare viśramayet upasaṃgṛhītapārśvaḥ
syādīṣannyubjaḥ
vamanaṃ tu dvitrivegaṃ kanīyaḥ catuṣpañcavegaṃ madhyamaṃ ṣaṭsaptavegamuttamamiti kotsaḥ śa--- mahatāṃ kṛśamadhyabalavatāṃ yogyamiti pārāśaryaḥ vyādhyavekṣamiti bhūyāṃsaḥ || athainaṃ vāntamuṣṇābhiradbhirācāmya nivāte prākśirasaṃ śāyayitvā'pāmārgapippalīśirīṣānyatamataṇḍulā --- ritavilagnasya kaphasyākarṣaṇārthaṃ saṃkṣamya tiṣṭhan pratiśyāyaśirorogākṣyabhiṣyandakarṇaśūlakarṇapākamanyāgrahadantapuṣpuṭakadantamūlaśothakaṇṭhagalagraha --- sya maṇḍādidivājāgaroṣṇodakopacāra upadiṣṭaḥ pathyatamaḥ | śṛṅgaveraśṛtamudakaṃ pipāsitaḥ pibedīṣaduṣṇam | alpaśo'pi śītaṃ hyasya pratiśyāyādīn prakopayati --- kuṣṭhahṛllāsajvarārucinidrātandrīrupajanayati tasmādityetat ṣaḍvarṣādīnāmarthe tadupadekṣyāmo'tibālo hyaśakta enaṃ vidhimanuṣṭhānahite cakṣurākhyāti na --- dupagṛhya deyamātaṅkaśamanaṃ viḍaṅgamātraṃ badarāsthimātraṃ badaramātramāmalakamātramauṣadhaṃ sarvameva saṃbhṛtaṃ syāt | vamanopagaṃ virecanopagaṃ vā cācurmāsyāṣṭama ---ṣu sarvāṇi saśarkarāṇīti vṛddhakāśyapaḥ | teṣāṃ palādhyardhapaladvitripalānyāloḍanāni yuktyā vā tato vidhārya bhiṣagdhātrī vā kuśalā kḷptankhayāṅgulyāntarkhāvayavā ---tha sarpeṇa gautamī vā'ṅgulī hīnā syānnasyamekeneti vṛddhakāśyapaḥ atibālasya sakṣaudraśarkaramapāmārgataṇḍuladvayaṃ trayaṃ veti vaideho janakaḥ || atha khalu --- nairatibāle hi bhagavan bhiṣagvamanādīni prayuñjānaḥ kālamātrāvayovyādhibalābalānāmanabhijño bālavināśāyātmadharmavināśāya ca saṃpadyata ityāha --- miti vāryovidaḥ dhātrīgurulaghutvahetoriti vātsyaḥ dhātrīśarmaṇi śiśuśarmeti bhūyāṃsaḥ
atha bhagavān kaśyapo'bravīdasamyagetat sarvamapyasādhakama ----

--- na tu vegān vidhārayet |
hastasvedaṃ ca śūleṣu bālakānāṃ vidhāpayet

atha khalvatibṛṃhaṇādatiraukṣyādatikārśyādatimāṃsamedo 'tyalpauṣadhatvādauṣadhasyātighanatvādatidravatvādatyuṣṇatvādatiśītatvādatimadhuratvādatikaṭutvādatilavaṇatvādatikaṣāyatvādatyamlatvādatikṣāratvādatibībhatsarūparasagandhatvāt --- nnasya pītauṣadhasya vā pracalāyataḥ prasvapato'nyamanasaḥ śītavātaśītagṛhaśītodakaśītāmbaropasevanādupānatpādukāgnivarjanādvegavidhāraṇādvegapreraṇāt---vamanavirecanauṣadhānāṃ duryogātiyogāvutpadyete tayorlakṣaṇāni bhavanti --- ādhmānapratiśyāyavibandhahṛdayopagrahaśūlaparikatikācchardiśirograhapravāhikāhikkāśvāsakāsatāluśoṣakaṇṭha---mukhavairasyaniṣṭhīvikoroghātajvaraviṣādasrotomalaprādurbhāvā duryogalakṣaṇopadravāḥ | śramadaurbalyaviṣādamohasmṛtiśrotrabhraṃśavānktanutvavipralāpajīvādānapakvāśayaśūlāplavana --- mukhahṛdayaśoṣamanyāpārśvākṣepahṛdayakampakeśamukhāṅgarūkṣatākaṭibastivaṅkṣaṇaśūlameḍhradāhagudaśūlapākabhraṃśātīsārorukampajānughātajaṅghāvā---ca mahāgadā atiyogādutpadyante
vamanaṃ ca virekāya vireko vamanāya
yadā bhavati taṃ prāhuratiyogaviparyayam
krūrakoṣṭho'nupasnigdho'lpenauṣadhena mṛdunā pratiśyāyānāhakaphaprasekāḥ | saśleṣmaṇi jvare'tīsāre cauṣadhaṃ kurvato bivandha utpadyate mṛdvalpauṣadhena vā | ativisraṃsanādgudabhraṃśānilaprakopasaṃjñānāśa--- parikartikāḥ | snehasvedahīnasyājīrṇe pibataścauṣadhaṃ pravāhikāśūlacchardihikkādhmānaśvāsakāsārocakahṛllāsagrahāḥ | atisnigdhasya śūlatandrīnidrāgudasrāvaśiro---dāhauṣṭhasaṃveṣṭāgnisādayakṣmāṇaḥ | vegavidhāraṇāddoṣatrayaprakopaścālpajīvādānonmādabhramāḥ | snehasvedopapannaṃ mṛdukoṣṭhamapi bahunauṣadhena ya upakramate tasyauṣadhaṃ jīvādānāya saṃpadyate --- guṇamavāpnotyanilaṃ cāsya prakopayati sa prakupitaḥ pralāponmādahikkāśvāsakāsatāluśoṣatṛṣṇāśūlabādhiryavāggrahabījopaghātatimirapuṣpopaghātāya saṃpadyate --- matilavaṇamatikaṣāyamatipratāntakālamanudīritamavaśeṣitamauṣadhaṃ vamanīyamupakalpitamasya virecanāya saṃpadyate | ajīrṇe saśleṣmaṇi vā'tidravamatiśīta --- yato vā virecanaṃ vamanāya saṃpadyate tamauṣadhaviparyayamācakṣate'tiyogaṃ ca | snehanirūhayoścordhvabhāvaṃ ca doṣāṇāṃ mandapravṛttirduryogo'pravṛttirayogaḥ | tayo --- śodhanamiṣyate nirūho vā | tadasya parikartikādhmānaparisrāvāṭopaśūlanidrātiviṣādarogopaśamāya bhavati | triphalācitrakorupūgadantīśyāmāsiddhaṃ cainaṃ ghṛtaṃ pāyayet prayogeṇa athavā jīvanīyauṣadhasiddhaṃ sarpistailaṃ payo vā bastinā dadyād vibandhāṭopaśūlaparisrāvapravāhikāmārutopaśāntaye --- triphalākāśmaryamṛdvīkāgandhatvaṅmūlaśṛtaṃ vā payo vibandhaparisrāvayorbastau praśasyate | gandharvatailaṃ cāsyānuvāsane praśasyate sarvānilāmayopaśamanaṃ siddhaṃ vā gandharvakaṣāyeṇa hiṅgudāru --- dārubilvaśalāṭupathyāpūtikakalkenāmlakāñjikayopasiddhena pūrvavadevānuvāsanaṃ sarvopadravaśamanamāhuretadgandharvataimityucyate'nuvāsanīyamiti
tatra ślokāḥ |
--- mānasya tathā'timātraṃ śītāmbhasā lehanameva pathyam
tathobhayoḥ śītakaṣāyapāna---
ghṛtena caina saśiraskamāśu digdhaṃ suśītena jalena siñcet
pādau ca dhāvyau śiśirodakena ---
---ṣṭaḥ |
sakaṭphalaṃ padmayavāsamocaṃ sakeśarośīrasamaṅgayuktam
etaiḥsupiṣṭaiḥśiśirāmbuyuktaiḥkalkaistathā śītapayodrumāṇām
pralipyamānaṃ saśiraskapādaṃ saṃ ---
--- śca śayyāśanapānabhojyaiḥ |
saṃsthāpayedatyayamāśu vidvān gṛha yathā prajvalitaikadeśam
dravyaistu taireva yathopapattyā śṛte jale chāgapayo'rdhamiśre |
saṃskṛtya śālyuttamalājapeyāṃ ---
---drute cchardirudīryate hi |
alpālpakaṃ caiva vilambitaṃ ca śītaṃ kaṣāyaṃ tu pibedvariṣṭham
phalāmlavalkaśca rasāñjanaṃ ca lodhraṃ ca tattaṇḍulavāriyuktam
pibedvireke vamanena vṛddhe tenāśu śāntiṃ labhate hi bālaḥ
--- nām |
tat sthāpanaṃ śreṣṭhamudāharanti kapitthasiddhaśca rasaśca madhvā
jambvāmravetasapayodramāgraistoyaṃ vipakvamatha dugdhamiśram
bhūyaḥ śṛtaṃ pravaramāhuretat pāne tathā bastividhau prayuktam
---ghnamocau |
dhātakyathaitairudakaṃ payo vā śṛtaṃ yavāgūśca hitā'tiyoge
māṃsāni mukhyāni ca jāṅgalāni saṃskṛtya yūṣāmrasakāpayaśca
sājye vidaddhyādatiyogaśāntyai ---
--- sā vireko gudaśūlapākau
upadravāścāpi na kīrtitā ye sarve śamaṃ yānti bhavatyarogaḥ
svabhyaktagātrasya tu vātaśūle svedaṃ yathāyogyamuśantivaidyāḥ |
peyāṃ pibeddīpana ---
iti ha smāha bhagavān kaśyapaḥ
iti siddhisthāne vamanavirecanīyāsiddhirnāma tṛtīyo'dhyāyaḥ 3
nastaḥkarmīyāsiddhirnāma caturtho'dhyāyaḥ
athāto nastaḥkarmīyāṃ siddhiṃ vyākhyāsyāmaḥ 1
iti ha smāha bhagavān kaśyapaḥ 2
śodhanaṃ pūraṇaṃ caiva dvividhaṃ nasyamucyate
kaphānilādhikatve ṣa --- kavṛścīkāpippalīkṣvākukṣavakapravarakaśigrabījaśirīṣabījāpāmārgabījanaktamālabījalasunabījamayūrakasaindhavasauvarcalavarāṅgatvagjyotiṣmatīviśvabheṣajādyanyatamaṃ dve trīṇi --- dhautāyāṃ dṛṣadi bījapūrakasvarasamūrcchitamārdrakasvarasamūrcchitaṃ vā tayorvā'nyatamaṃ kṣaudramṛdvīkāsaṃyutamāḍūke samavāpyeṣaduṣṇaṃ kṛtvā''turāya prākśirase śayānāyonna tanāsāgrāya ---galadhamanīmukhalalāṭanāsikāśiraḥśmaśrumukhamanyādeśānarhataḥ svedayitvā bhiṣagbhiṣaganumato vā vāmenāṅguṣṭhenāvanamya nāsikāgraṃ śirasto bhaved dakṣiṇe --- tvanyatrālpaśo'lpaśo dattvā śleṣmāṇamākarṣayedabhīkṣṇaṃ ca hṛdayādīnaṅgāvayavān svedayet parimṛdnīyādanu tvalpakaphaprasecanāt | tricatuṣpañca kṛtveti vā --- taḥ śuṣkacūrṇāni pradhamanāni jighrato vastrapuṭikābaddhāni bhavanti | kṣaudrayutāni tvavapīḍaḥ syāt | mukhanāsikayoralaṃ kaphaṃ vighātayatīti pariṣat || taireva kaṭutailamajjāmūtrasiddha --- | bālāya dhātryaṅkagatāya balādupagṛhya deyaṃ vyādhirhyupekṣyamāṇo viṣavat pariṇamati tasmānnātidrataṃ nātivilambitaṃ nātighanaṃ nātitanuṃ nātyuṣṇaṃ nātiśītaṃ ---dvā pipāsataḥ pītavato vā nāpakve pratiśyāye nājīrṇe na vātaśirorogajvarayorna śrame na śiraḥsnātukāmasya na sadyaḥśiraḥsnātasya na rajasvalāyāḥ --- karma vidadhyādanyatrātyayāt | tasyātidrutaṃ dattamauṣadhaṃ prāṇānuparuṇaddhi khāni cāsyopatapyante śvāsakāsahikkālālāsrāvavāggrahāyāsāścotpadyante | --- vā'tyuṣṇaṃ dāhaṃ vraṇān divākarāvartaṃ cotpādayati | atiśītaṃ viṣṭambhayati | atibahu sakṛdāśu pratyāgacchati | alpaṃ śaśvadāvejayati | atibahuśo vātaprako --- atitīkṣṇamajasraṃ smṛtibhraṃśonmādavātādīn prakopaya
ti | etenaivopacāro vyākhyātaḥ
atha pītavato nasyakarmaṇā nāsāsrāvaśirorogagauravakaphaprasekā --- pakve pratiśyāye ghrāṇopaghātapūtināsasormīraminmina nāsārśāṃsi | ajīrṇapratiśyāye pārśvoparodhakaṇṭhoddhvaṃsakāsaśvāsacchardijvarārocakāratayaḥ | śiraḥsnātasya --- 'rdhāvabhedakajvarāgnināśāḥ | vātajvarādiṣu tāneva rogān saṃtanoti | rajasvalāyā ṛturvyāpadyate | śuddhasnātāyā yonirupaśuṣyati | garbhiṇyā hīnāṅgasya --- paghātārocakau | bubhukṣitasya klamārucī | tṛṣitasya kāsaśvāsakaphacchardayaḥ | atha khalveṣāṃ yathārthamauṣadhamupadekṣyāmaḥ rūkṣaṃ snigdhaṃ vobhayaṃ hi nastaḥkarma --- teṣāṃ svaṃ svaṃ cikitsitamaviruddham | ayaṃ cātra viśeṣaḥ -- mṛdvīkādāḍimajambvāmramustaśṛtaṃ kaṣāyaṃ śītaṃ srutaṃ pūtaṃ sakṣaudraśarkaraṃ pāyayecchardyām | pūrvavacca --- praśasyate | raktaśālimudgamaṇḍasaindhavoṣṇabhojanaṃ ca svedalaṅghanakavalagrahāvapīḍaṣṭhīvanāni ca dhūpanadhūmapāne ca pratiśyāye laṅghanamiti pariṣat || jīrṇe --- kaphapraseke triphalācūrṇaṃ sasaindhavaṃ sakṣaudraṃ vā lihyāt | cakṣuṣoruktaṃ saindhavamarīcarasāñjanamanaḥśilā vā'jākṣīrapiṣṭā vartyaḥ kaṇḍūtimiropadehadūṣikāśamanyo bhavanti | rasakriyā vā sa --- śirovirecanadhūmapānāvapīḍavamanavirecananirūhapathyabhojanāni śasyante | devadārutālīsamāṃsīmustaśigu?ragandharvavāsakapunarnavākalkaiḥ sakṣaudraistailaṃ pakvamabhīkṣṇamupacāryamāṇamasya yo'tinasyā --- rya tṛṣṇāloḥ śītato vepamānasya tīkṣṇaṃ śirovirecanamanilaśaṅkhahanustambhadivākarāvartātimohānupaja nayati jvaraṃ vā sopadravaṃ teṣu kumāratailaṃ yaṣṭīmadhukatailaṃ punarnavātailaṃ ghṛtaṃ vā tadvatsaṃskṛtaṃ vā --- trakaṃ praśasyate jāṅgalaśca saṃskṛto rasaḥ | nidrānāśe matsyamāṃsadadhiyavagodhūmaśāliṣaṣṭikānnaguḍasaṃskṛtāni sneha lavaṇaveṣaṇopadaṃśayuktānyānayanti nidrām | rajasvalāyāḥ snātāyā garbhiṇyāśca puṣpādhyāyayūṣādhyāya --- yebhyo'dhyāyebhyo bheṣajaṃ vidaddhyāt | kṣīraṃ
vā jīvanīyopasiddhamiti pariṣat
tatra ślokāḥ --
kumāratailameteṣāṃ vyādhīnāṃ śamanaṃ param
nasye pāne tathā'bhyaṅge purāṇaṃ ghṛtameva ca
laṅghanaṃ dhūmadhūpau ca svedoṣṇapariṣecanam
upanāho'vapīḍaśca śa ---
yavānnaṃ śālayo mudgadhātrīdāḍimasaindhavam
hitaṃ nasyavidhau bhojyaṃ tadā hyārtasya vibhrame
nasyakarmaṇi bālānāṃ stanapānāṃ viśeṣataḥ
kaṭutailaṃ prayuñjīta ghṛtaṃ vā saindhavānvitam
binduṃ bindumatho dvau dvau trīṃstrīn vā rogadarśanāt
aṅgulyā nāsayordadyādapidadhyāt kṣaṇaṃ tataḥ
tenāsya pacyate śleṣmā śleṣmaṇā na ca bādhyate
snānādīn parihārāṃśca yathoktānupacārayet
iti ha smāha bhagavān kaśyapaḥ
iti siddhisthāne nastaḥkarmīyāsiddhirnāma caturtho'dhyāyaḥ 4
kriyāsiddhirnāma pañcamo'dhyāyaḥ
athātaḥ kriyāsiddhiṃ vyākhyāsyāmaḥ 1
iti ha smāha bhagavān kaśyapaḥ 2
kriyāṇāṃ siddhimanvicchannityaṃ brūyādbhiṣaṅnaraḥ
tailapātramivātmānaṃ --- rogadarśanāt 3
ajīrṇaṃ maithanaṃ yānamuccairbhāṣyaṃ divāśayam
aticaṅkramaṇasthānamasātmyādi ca varjayet 4
ajīrṇe vardhate vyādhiḥ punaḥ kārśyaṃ ca jāyate
kriyāyāṃ maithunācchāṇḍhyaṃ pāṇḍutvaṃ ca nigacchati 5
yo'tīva nityaṃ ramate yānādvātaśca kupyati
agnisādo divāsvapnāt kaphavṛddhirjvaro'ruciḥ 6
manyāstambhaḥ śiraḥśūlaṃ vākpārśvahanusaṃgrahaḥ
kaṇṭhoddhvaṃsaḥ śramo glānirjvaraścātyuñcabhāṣaṇāt 7
kaṭīvaṅkṣaṇapādorujānubastyanilāmayaḥ
śarkarāśmarikhallyādyā aticaṅkramaṇodbhavāḥ 8
suptatā'dharakāyasya tandrījāḍyādivibhramāḥ
vātaśoṇitahṛllāsapre --- 9
vaivarṇyamarucirglāniḥ kaṇḍupāṇḍujvarabhramāḥ
kāmalākuṣṭhavaisarpapāmādyāścāpyasātmyajāḥ 10
teṣāṃ cikitsitaṃ svaṃ svamaviruddhaṃ vidhāpayet
kṛśān saṃbṛṃhayeccāpi karśayet paribṛṃhitān 11
atidīrghamatisthūlaṃ jarjaraṃ sphuṭitaṃ tanu
kuṭilaṃ --- ca varjayet 12
atihrasvaḥ kharaḥ sthūlastanudīrghacirasthitāḥ
chidrī mahānupahato varjitā bastayo nava 13
aprāptamatinītaṃ ca vinyastamatipīḍitam
srutaṃ vilagnaṃ śithilaṃ ruddhavātaṃ cirāciram 14
prajñāparādhajā doṣāḥ praṇeturbastikarmaṇi
--- bhagandaram 15
jīvakarṣabhasiddhena taṃ ghṛtenānuvāsayet
nirūhayet sraṃsayedvā tataḥ saṃpadyate sukhī 16
kṣīraṃ yavānnaśākāni jaṅgalānyāmiṣāṇi ca
bhojayet snehayuktāni gudarogodbhave śiśum 17
iti ha smāha bhagavān kaśyapaḥ
iti siddhisthāne kriyāsiddhirnāma pañcamo'dhyāyaḥ 5
bastikarmīyāsiddhirnāma ṣaṣṭho'dhyāyaḥ
athāto bastikarmīyāṃ siddhi vyākhyāsyāmaḥ 1
iti ha smāha bhagavān kaśyapaḥ 2
--- gude malābhibhūte gude samupasthitānile vopasthitapurīṣe vā saṃsthitaśleṣmaṇi vā netre vā jihme śithilavatyapīḍite na snehaḥ
pakvāśayamanuprāpnoti | tamayogaṃ vidyāt 3
yaḥ --- vātapittakaphapurīṣamūtrābhibhūtasya --- gacchannūrdhvaṃ vā prapadyate viriktasrataviśoṣitatṛṣitabubhukṣitaśrāntacinterṣyāyāsaśokabhayārtasya vā na pratyāgacchati tamatiyogaṃ vidyāt | tayostṛṣṇāmūrcchāhṛllāsajvaradāhahṛdrogaśvayathaśūlārśaḥ pāṇḍukā malā---tistaimityādyā

baddhān hatān viprakṛtāṃstathaiva śītāmbusiktairvyajanai ---

gomūtrayuktāmabhayāṃ pibedvā yuktaṃ trivṛt saindhavasaptalādyaiḥ

nivṛttadoṣasya sapañcamūlamāsthāpane'tyantamuśanti vṛddhāḥ

visraṃsayet pathyabhujaṃ yathoktaṃ --- |
--- gaiśca sakiṇvasiddhārthakamāṣacūrṇaiḥ
sasaindhavaistailaguḍopapannairyavopamāḥ phalavartīvidaddhyāt
ānāhinastāḥ praṇayedapāne ṣaṭ sapta pañceti vayonurūpam
tābhirvirikte labhate sa śarme virecayettadasiddhau tu tīkṣṇaiḥ
iti ha smāha bhagavān kaśyapaḥ
iti siddhisthāne bastikarmīyāsiddhirnāma
ṣaṣṭho'dhyāyaḥ 6
pañca karmīyā siddhirnāma saptamo'dhyāyaḥ
athātaḥ pañcakarmīyāṃ siddhiṃ vyākhyāsyāmaḥ 1
iti ha smāha bhagavān kaśyapaḥ 2
atha khalu bhagavan dehināṃ vyādhayaḥ ke vamanasādhyā ke na ke virecanasādhyāḥ ke na ke'nuvāsanasādhyāḥ ke na ke nirūha sādhyāḥ ke na ---bhagavān kaśyapaḥ--kaphajvarārucimukhavairasyakaphaprasekakaphahṛdrogavisūcikākāsaśvāsagalagrahagalaśuṇḍikāgalagaṇḍagaṇḍamālārohiṇikāvidārikādhoraktapittahṛllāsapramehahalīmaka---skandagrahaskandāpasmāraskandapitṛnaigameṣakṣīragauravakṣīravṛddhikṣīraghanatvājīrṇaparikatikāhṛllāsaśūlāṭopātiviricyamānagaritaviṣapī tādyā vamanasādhyā iti 3
atra ślokaḥ
kaphādhikāśca ye rogā --- |
