daivatejyopahāraiśca dhūpanābhyañjanāñjanaiḥ 80
vātottaraṃ snehapānairabhyaṅgaiḥ sāvagāhanaiḥ
snigdhoṣṇairannapānaiśca balibhiścāpyupakramaiḥ 81
pittottaraṃ tiktaśītaiḥ śamanaiḥ savirecanaiḥ
payasā sarpiṣā caiva śītaiścābhyañjanairjayet 82
vamanaiḥ pācanīyaiśca laṅghanairlaghubhojanaiḥ
rūkṣoṣṇaiścāpyupacaret kaṣāyaiśca kaphottaram 83
romaharṣo'ṅgamardaśca vātapittottare jvare
mahākalyāṇakaṃ sarpiḥ pañcagavyamatho pibet 84
pītvā vā mahatīṃ mātrāṃ sarpiṣaḥ punarullikhet
tadaharvā paredyurvā peyāṃ samaricāṃ pibet 85
rājamūlasya vā kvāthaṃ pibet prajvarakaṃ haviḥ
kritoyaṃ --- mayogaścaturthakamapohati 86
dhāroṣṇaṃ vā payaḥ pītvā tadvamedicchayā na vā
śītaṃ vā madhunā'śītaṃ nimbapatrodakaṃ pibet 87
sarpirvā māhiṣaṃ pītvā hiṅgustokasamanvitam
tasmin vidagdha evānnaṃ dadhnā bhuñjīta kevalam 88
nīlaspandaśiphā vā'pi supiṣṭā taṇḍulāmbunā
deyā jvaratithau nasye mṛgarājasya vā vasā 89
harītakī vacā kuṣṭhaṃ nimbapatraṃ palaṅkaṣā
siddhārthakā yavāḥ sarpirdhūpo jīrṇajvarāpahaḥ 90
mūlāni sahadevāyāḥ kaṭukā rohiṇī vacā
taṇḍulodakapiṣṭo'yaṃ deho dāhajvarāpahaḥ 91
surayā sarvagandhaiśca tailamabhyañjanaṃ pacet
sarṣapā lasunaṃ hiṅgu trivyoṣaṃ rocanā vacā 92
ṛkṣapittaṃ bastamūtraṃ karañjasya phalāni ca
grahajvarābhibhūtānāṃ nasyakarmāñjanaṃ hitam 93
sannipātavibandhe ca vyāladaṣṭe ca śasyate
arśaḥsu yoniśūle ca pralepo'yamanuttamaḥ 94
bhīṣaṇairharṣaṇaiścaiva vicitrādbhūtadarśanaiḥ
vyākṣepaṃ manasaścāsya bhiṣakkuryājjvarāgame 95
pānaṃ caitatpradātavyaṃ snigdhasvinnasya dehinaḥ
tribhaṇṭīmajagandhāṃ ca nīlinīṃ kaṭurohiṇīm 96
kalkaṃ vā kaṭurohiṇyāḥ pibedgomūtrasaṃplutam
cikitsāyāṃ ca yat proktaṃ tacca kuryādvidhānavit 97
iti ha smāha bhagavān kaśyapaḥ
iti khileṣu viṣamajvaranirdeśīyo nāma prathamo'dhyāyaḥ
viśeṣanirdeśīyo nāma dvitīyo'dhyāyaḥ
athāto viśeṣanirdeśīyaṃ nāmādhyāyaṃ vyākhyāsyāmaḥ 1
iti ha smāha bhagavān kaśyapaḥ 2
maharṣiṃ kaśyapaṃ vṛddhaṃ vedavedāṅgapāragam
sukhopaviṣṭamavyagramapṛcchad vṛddhaśīvakaḥ 3
viṣamāṇāṃ jvarāṇāṃ te savikalpaṃ savistaram
yadyathāvacca yāvacca proktaṃ vaktavyamāditaḥ 4
sāmānyenāpi sarveṣāṃ jvarāṇāṃ vigatajvara
vaktumarhasi kārtsnyena pariśeṣamataḥ param 5
iti saṃcoditaḥ prāha prajāpatiridaṃ vacaḥ
samāsābhihitaṃ yacca nābhihitaṃ hitam 6
jvaritānāṃ jvarāṇāṃ ca bhūyo vakṣyāmi tacchṛṇu
avasthālakṣaṇoddeśaviśeṣopakrame kramāt 7
bhaiṣajyamanavasthāyāṃ pathyamapyavacāritam
guṇaṃ na kiñcit kurute doṣāyaiva tu kalpate 8
prayuktaṃ tadavasthāyāmamṛtatvāya kalpate
vātādbāhyādabhīghātāt krodhācchokādbhayāt kṣayāt 9
śramād grahābhiṣaṅgācca vegānāṃ ca vidhāraṇāt
samutpanne jvare jantorvamanaṃ na prayojayet 10
prayuktaṃ kurute kṣipraṃ tīvraṃ sopadravaṃ jvaram
pūraṇāttu samutpanne jvare hyamātrayā śrayet 11
doṣe vṛddhe'timātrā ca samutkliṣṭe tathaiva ca
sannipātajvarabhayāttvaritaṃ vāmayedbhiṣak 12
vāmitaṃ laṅghanādyena krameṇopakramettataḥ
vamanānantaraṃ cāsya kuryācchīrṣavirecanam 13
tathā''śu śirasaḥ śūlaṃ gauravaṃ copaśāmyati
vibandhaścakṣurādīnāmaruciśca nivartate 14
jīrṇajvare'pyathaiteṣu vikāreṣu pradāpayet
bahudoṣe balavatī madhyadoṣeṣu madhyamā 15
alpadoṣe jvare mṛdvī kriyaḥ kāryā vijānatā
svāni rūpāṇi kārtsnyena yadā vyādhau viśeṣataḥ 16
tīvrāṇi vyathitatvaṃ ca śarīrasyopalakṣayet
bahudoṣaṃ tadā vidyādalpadoṣamato'nyathā 17
tathā madhyabaleṣveṣu madhyadoṣaṃ vibhāvayet
vibandhārucitṛṇmūrcchā gātrabhedaḥ śirorujā 18
pralāpālasyahṛllāsatandrīdāhaśramabhramāḥ
mūtrapracuratā glāniḥ purīṣasyāvipakvatā 19
utkleśo gurukoṣṭhatvaṃ liṅgānyāmajvare vadet
nivṛttau prāyaśaścaiṣāṃ saṃjāte jvaramārdave 20
laghutvaṃ cāṣṭarātre ca nirāmajvaramādiśet
āpātalakṣyaḥ syādūṣmā tatkṣaṇādeti mārdavam 21
antarlaghutvamutsāho doṣapaktiḥ prasannatā
tṛṣṇādīnāṃ mṛdutvaṃ ca bahirmārgagate jvare 22
tatrābhyaṅgān pradehāṃśca pariṣekāṃśca kārayet
avipakvā vipakvā vā doṣā yasya na nirhṛtāḥ 23
na ca vyādherupaśamo na cotsāho na lāghavam
glānikārśye na cātyarthaṃ na ca cchando'śanaṃ prati 24
aprakṛṣṭe vā sa kāle doṣadurbalaḥ
ato viparyayāddehī bhavedauṣadhadurbalaḥ 25
pācanairavipakvānāṃ doṣāṇāṃ doṣadurbale
samyakkuryādupaśamaṃ pakvānāṃ ca virecanaiḥ 26
itarasyāmayāvekṣaṃ balāpyāyanamiṣyate
samudīrṇeṣu sahasā doṣeṣvabhinaveṣu ca 27
balavad durbale vā'pi kaṣāyaṃ vā'pi bheṣajam
saṃrakṣan dharmayaśasī na prayuñjyāt kadācana 28
yathā śaratsu mahatoḥ kruddhayorabhiṣaktayoḥ
na dhatte durbalā vegaṃ hastinoragravāraṇī 29
tathā balaṃ balavatostaddoṣaudhayordvayoḥ
saṃkṣubdhayorna sahate saṃtaptā dehinastataḥ 30
puṣṇāti durbalaṃ doṣāḥ kaṣāyaḥ stambhayatyataḥ
bhūyo'bhivṛddhāste prāṇānnighnantyāśu śarīriṇaḥ 31
kaṣāyeṇākulībhūtāściraṃ saṃkleśayanti vā
bhagnavegeṣu doṣeṣu vidhinā laṅghanādinā 32
kāle prayuktaṃ bhaiṣajyaṃ syādvikāropaśāntaye
laṅghanaṃ svedanaṃ peyā trividhā dīpanānvitā
odanastrividho yūṣaḥ kaṣāyastrividho rasaḥ 33
sarpirabhyañjanaṃ bastiḥ pradehaḥ sāvagāhanaḥ
jvarāpahaḥ samuddiṣṭo laṅghanādirayaṃ kramaḥ 34
pacyante saptarātreṇa doṣāḥ saptasu dhātuṣu
tasmāt kaṣāyaṃ saptāhe pācanīyaṃ vidhāpayet 35
śamanaṃ sraṃsanīyaṃ vā yathāvasthamataḥ param
mandoṣmatvāt sthiratvācca gurutvācca kaphajvaraḥ 36
paripākaṃ cirādeti tasmādasyauṣadhakramaḥ
aṣṭāhāt parato vā'pi yathāpākaṃ vidhīyate 37
pakvāvaśeṣastenāśu pacyate'gniśca dīpyate
doṣapaktyā'gnidīptyā ca jvarasyāpacayo dhruvam 38
tadyuktasnehasaṃskārān rasānatrāvacārayet
tataḥ saṃśamanaṃ kuryādvyādhiśeṣopaśāntaye 39
prasādanārthaṃ dhātūnāṃ balasyāpyāyanāya ca
deśadoṣāgnisātmyartuyuktaṃ cātra praśoṣaṇam 40
tryahaṃ caturahaṃ vā'pi nātimātramataḥ param
rūkṣatvājjvaryamāṇāya deyaṃ sarpirjvarāpaham 41
jvarito'nena nirvāti pradīptaṃ cāmbunā gṛham
sarpiḥ pittaṃ śamayati śaityāt snehācca mārutam 42
samānaguṇamapyetat saṃskārājjīyate kapham
atha cedbahudoṣatvāt kṛte'pi śamane sati 43
pakvāśayagurutvaṃ ca stimitatvaṃ ca lakṣyate
svedo viṇmūtrarāgaśca bhaktasyānabhinandanam 44
snigdhāya kṣāmadehāya dadyāttatra virecanam
śūle pakvāśayagate pārśvapṛṣṭhakaṭigrahe 45
vātaviṇmūtrasaṅge ca nirūhaḥ sānuvāsanaḥ
bahirmārgagate cāpi jvare'bhyaṅgādiriṣyate 46
narasya vātaprakṛteryadi syādvātiko jvaraḥ
ṛtau ca vātaprakṛtau sa duḥsādhyastrisaṃkaraḥ 47
tathaiva pittaprakṛteḥ ślaiṣmikasya ca dehinaḥ
viparītaprakṛtayaḥ sukhasādhyā jvarādayaḥ 48
ekadvitriprakṛtayo vyādhayaḥ sarva eva hi
sukhaduḥkhāścikitsyāḥ syuḥ prāyaśaste yathākramam 49
samutkliṣṭeṣu doṣeṣu trividhaṃ karma niścitam
śodhanaṃ śamanaṃ caiva tathā śamanaśodhanam 50
tatra puṃsāṃ balavatāṃ vahnimatāṃ satām
tīvravegāmayānāṃ ca hitaṃ śodhanamauṣadham 51
balināmalpadoṣāṇāṃ nātivṛddhavikāriṇām
nātikleśasahānāṃ ca śamanaṃ hitamucyate 52
tathaiva madhyadoṣāṇāṃ durbalānāṃ śarīriṇām
balavadvyādhijuṣṭānāṃ hitaṃ śamanaśodhanam 53
ūrdhva harati yaddoṣānadhaścobhayataśca yat
dravyaṃ vividhavīryatvāttaddhi saṃśodhanaṃ smṛtam 54
nādho na cordhvaṃ harati yaddoṣāñchamayatyapi
na cograguṇavīryaṃ tad dravyaṃ saṃśamanaṃ viduḥ 55
nātyarthaṃ śodhayati yaddoṣān saṃśamayatyapi
tatra madhyabalopetaṃ dravyaṃ śamanaśodhanam 56
daśamūlaśaṭīrāsnāvayasthāpañcakolakam
śārṅgeṣṭā rohiṇī pāṭhā saralo devadāru ca
mustā'mṛtā vṛścikālī karkaṭākhyā durālabhā 57
trāyantītyevamādīni śamanīyāni nirdiśet
vacākośātakīnimbapippalyaḥ kauṭajaṃ phalam 58
kṣāradvayaṃ sagomūtraṃ madanaṃ lavaṇāni ca
triphalā''ragvadho dantī nīlinī saptalā trivṛt 59
evamādi tu yaccānyad dravyaṃ śodhanamucyate
kāśmaryāmalakaṃ drākṣā śītapākī parūṣakam 60
madhukaṃ pippalīmūlaṃ viḍaṅgaṃ hiṅgu saindhavam
pāṭhā'jagandhā'tiviṣā pathyā mustā kaṭutrikam 61
rohiṇītyevamādi syād dravyaṃ śamanaśodhanam
trividhaṃ karma nirdiṣṭamityetaddoṣaśāntaye 62
doṣaśāntau ca dhātūnāṃ prasāda upajāyate
malāyanānāṃ sarveṣāmavyāghātakriyāsu ca 63
doṣadhātu malaistasmāt sve sve karmaṇyavasthitaiḥ
svāsthyamutpadyate nṝṇāmasvāsthyaṃ tadviparyaye 64
samyagāhāraceṣṭābhyāṃ yadā sātmyaṃ yatheritam
samānāṃ rakṣaṇaṃ kuryāddoṣādīnāṃ vicakṣaṇaḥ 65
kupitānāṃ praśamanaṃ kṣīṇānāmabhivardhanam
kṣapaṇaṃ caiva vṛddhānāmetāvaddhi cikitsitam 66
tadekatvamanekatvamavasthāmudayavyayam
doṣāṇāmāturāṇāṃ ca saṃpradhārya balābalam 67
yathāvadauṣadhānāṃ ca bhinnānāṃ nāmarūpataḥ
rasān guṇāṃśca vīryaṃ ca vipākaṃ ca yathātatham 68
deśaṃ kālamupāyaṃ ca pramāṇaṃ vyādhimeva ca
jñātvā cikitsā bhiṣajā praṇetavyā'pramādataḥ 69
ye yathā ca samuddiṣṭā yogāḥ sve sve cikitsite
te tathaiva prayoktavyā na teṣvasti vicāraṇā 70
ko hi nāma praṇītānāṃ dravyāṇāṃ tattvadarśibhiḥ
nānāvidhānāmekatve tatkarma jñātumarhati 71
kiñcidanyarasaṃ dravyaṃ guṇataḥ kiñcidanyathā
vīryataścānyathā kiñcidvidyādatra vipākataḥ 72
atha caikatvamāgamya prayoge na virudhyate
utpadyate yathārthaṃ ca samavāyaguṇāntaram 73
pṛthakpṛthakprasiddhe'pi gandhe gandhāntaraṃ yathā
gandhāṅgānāṃ manohlādi pratyakṣaṃ sāmavāyikam 74
tasmādārṣaprayogeṣu prakṣepāpacayaṃ prati
na pramādyedavijñāya doṣauṣadhabalābalam 75
vayaḥ śarīrāgnibalaṃ tvavekṣya matimān bhiṣak
mātrāṃ vikalpayedatra pradhānāvaramadhyataḥ 76
iti jvarāṇāmuddiṣṭo viśeṣo'yamupakrame
yaṃ viditvā tu kāryeṣu vividheṣu na saṃbhramet 77
iti ha smāha bhagavān kaśyapaḥ 78
iti khileṣu viśeṣanirdeśīyo nāma dvitīyo'dhyāyaḥ
bhaiṣajyopakramaṇīyastṛtīyādhyāyaḥ
athāto bhaiṣajyopakramaṇīyamadhyāyaṃ vyākhyāsyāmaḥ 1
iti ha smāha bhagavān kaśyapaḥ 2
brāhmayā śriyā prajvalantaṃ brahmarṣimamitadyutim
kaśyapaṃ lokakartāraṃ bhārgavaḥ paripṛcchati 3
ābādhakāraṇaṃ vyādhirbheṣajaṃ sukhakāraṇam
samyagyuktaṃ tadamṛtaṃ syāttadanyadviṣavadbhavet 4
bheṣajopakramaṃ tasmādbhagavan vaktumarhasi
auṣadhaṃ kimadhiṣṭhānaṃ kathaṃ cāsyopadiśyate
auṣadhajñauṣadhatvaṃ ca bheṣajatvamathāpi ca 5
bhaiṣajyatvāgadatvaṃ ca kaṣāyatvaṃ tathaiva ca
yathā ca gauṇyena rasā dravye dravye vyavasthitāḥ 6
prādhānyena yathā dṛṣṭo madhuke madhuro rasaḥ
amlaḥ kapitthe lavaṇaḥ saindhave nāgare kaṭuḥ 7
tiktastiktakarohiṇyāṃ kaṣāyaścābhayāṃ prati
satyevaṃ rasanānātve saṃyogaḥ sarva eva hi 8
kaṣāyatvena nirdiṣṭaḥ kutaḥ kiṃ cātra kāraṇam
kati ke cauṣadhaguṇā bhedāścāsya kati smṛtāḥ 9
kati cauṣadhakālāśca kāle kāle ca ko vidhiḥ
kasthāṃ kasyāmavasthāyāṃ pātavyaṃ bheṣajaṃ na vā 10
kathaṃ ca peyaṃ pītasya parihāryaṃ ca kiṃ bhavet
jīryamāṇasya kiṃ rūpaṃ kiṃ ca jīrṇasya lakṣaṇam 11
vibhajya vayasastritvaṃ kathaṃ mātrā vidhīyate
adhobhāgordhvabhāgānāṃ doṣasaṃśamanī ca yā 12
jīvanīyā ca yā mātrā dīpanīyā ca yā smṛtā
mātrā saṃśodhanī yā ca snehamātrā ca yā bhavet 13
sarvametadyathātattvaṃ kīrtayasva mahāmune
jātamātramupādāya yāvadvarṣaśataṃ param 14
iti śuśrūṣamāṇāya śiṣyāya vadatāṃvaraḥ
ācacakṣe yathānyāyaṃ bhaiṣajyopakramaṃ prati 15
ayaṃ heturidaṃ liṅgamasya cāyamupakramaḥ
iti tāvat paraṃ sūkṣmaṃ vyādhijñānaṃ pracakṣate 16
asmādapyauṣadhajñānamāhuḥ sūkṣmataraṃ budhāḥ
tadahaṃ te'bhidhāsyāmi kārtsnyenaiva nibodha me 17
pathyasevinamārogyaṃ guṇena bhajate naram
apathyasevinaṃ kṣipraṃ rogaḥ samabhimardati 18
sa rogo dvividhaścoktaḥ śārīro mānasastathā
ādhayo mānasāstatra śārīrā vyādhayaḥ smṛtāḥ 19
sādhyo'sādhyaśca yāpyaśca trividhaṃ rogalakṣaṇam
vātapittakaphā doṣāḥ śārīravyādhihetavaḥ 20
sattvetaraṃ ca dvandvaṃ ca mānasāmayahetavaḥ
dhṛtivīryasmṛtijñānavijñānairmānasaṃ jayet 21
śārīraṃ bheṣajaiḥ kālayuktidaivavyapāśrayaiḥ
sa punardvividho vyādhirāganturnija eva ca 22
āganturbādhate pūrvaṃ paścāddoṣān prapadyate
nijastu cīyate pūrvaṃ paścādvṛddhaḥ prabādhate 23
tasmādāganturogāṇāṃ paśyanti nijavat kriyāḥ
nijānāṃ pūrvarūpāṇi dṛṣṭvā saṃśodhanaṃ hitam 24
saṃśodhanaṃ saptavidhaṃ tadāyattaṃ cikitsitam
taccāyattaṃ catuṣpāde pādaścauṣadhamucyate 25
oṣadhaṃ yuktyadhiṣṭhānaṃ daivādhiṣṭhānameva ca
yuktirvamanakarmādi daivaṃ yāgādi kīrtyate 26
oso nāma rasaḥ so'syāṃ dhīyate yattadoṣadhiḥ
osādārogyamādhatte tasmādoṣadhiroṣadhaḥ 27
bhiṣagvijñānaneyatvādbheṣajaṃ bhiṣajo viduḥ
bhiṣagjite hitatvācca bhaiṣajyaṃ paricakṣate 28
agadatvaṃ ca yuktasya gadānāmapunarbhavāt
kaṇṭhasya kaṣāṇāt prāyo rogāṇāṃ vā'pi karṣaṇāt 29
kaṣāyaśabdaḥ prādhānyāt sarvayogeṣu kalpyate
śabdarūparasasparśagandhavaddeśajaṃ navam 30
ajagdhamavidagdhaṃ ca dravyaṃ guṇavaducyate
mātrāvallaghupākaṃ ca hṛdyaṃ doṣapravāhaṇam 31
alpapeyaṃ mahāvīryaṃ prīṇanaṃ balarakṣaṇam
vyāpattāvalpadoṣaṃ ca mandaglāpanameva ca 32
saṃskāraguṇasaṃpannaṃ rājārhaṃ bheṣajaṃ matam
taddhi siddhamasiddhaṃ ca śītamuṣṇaṃ dravaṃ ghanam 33
koṣṇaṃ sasnehamasnehamiti bhinnamanekadhā
tadāmayavayobhedāt saptadhaiva vibhajyate 34
cūrṇaṃ śītakaṣāyaśca svaraso'bhiṣavastathā
phāṇṭaḥ kalkastathā kvātho yathāvattaṃ nibodha me 35
sūkṣmacūrṇīkṛtaṃ cūrṇaṃ nānākarmasu yujyate
grahaṇyāmavikāreṣu braṇavatyañjanādiṣu 36
śītaḥ śītakaṣāyaḥ syādantarikṣāmbusaṃplutaḥ
sa pittajvaradāhāsṛgviṣamūrcchāmadāpahaḥ 37
tadvadeva niśāvyuṣṭo'bhiṣavaḥ sādhu sādhitaḥ
praśāntāgnibalakṣobhaḥ saumyaḥ svarasasaṅgataḥ 38
drākṣekṣvāmalakādīnāṃ pīḍanāt svarasaḥ smṛtaḥ
sa saṃśamanasaṃyoge nānārogeṣu kalpate 39
kvathitastvāntarikṣeṇa vāriṇā'rdhāvaśeṣitaḥ
sakṛdvā phāṇitaḥ phenaṃ kaṣāyaḥ phāṇṭa ucyate 40
so'lpadoṣabale bāle laghuvyādhau ca śasyate
kalkaḥ kalkīkṛto yojyaḥ pānalepāvalehane 41
kevaladravyapeyatvādvikārṣī durjaraśca saḥ
pādasthito bhavet kvātho yukto'bahvagnitejasā 42
sa vayobalasaṃpanne guruvyādhau ca śasyate
saptadhaivaṃ vibhajyaitaddaśadhā pravicārayet
pūrvaṃ bhaktasya madhye'dhaḥ samudgaṃ samuhurmuhuḥ 43
sabhaktaṃ bhaktayormadhye grāsagrāsāntare paraḥ
pūrvaṃ bhaktasya bhaiṣajyaṃ na karoti balakṣayam 44
āmāśayagatān doṣānnihantyāśu ca pacyate
annasaṃstambhite dehe cchardyudgāravyathādayaḥ 45
na bhavanti yatastasmāttaddeyaṃ durbalīyase
madhyebhaktaṃ hyubhayato ruddhamannena bheṣajam 46
tadantarāśaye doṣān sukhenaiva niyacchati
śamayatyāśvadho bhaktamuraḥkaṇṭhaśirogadān 47
vyatyāsena ca sāmudgaṃ doṣe tūrdhvamadhogate
muhurmuhuḥ śvāsakāsahikkātṛṭchardiśāntaye 48
hitaṃ balāgnirakṣārthaṃ sabhaktaṃ durbalātmanām
strībālavṛddhalalitakṣatakṣīṇauṣadhadviṣām 49
vyādhau mande'nale tīkṣṇe bhaktayormadhya iṣyate
kṣīṇakṣīṇālpaśukrāṇāṃ vājīkaraṇamauṣadham 50
grāse vidheyaṃ cūrṇaṃ ca yadagnibalavardhanam
grāsāntare cchardanīyaṃ dhūmapānaṃ ca śasyate 51
abhaktamauṣadhaṃ pītaṃ vyādhimāśu balīyasām
hanyāttadeveha balaṃ balavaddurbalīyasām 52
etānauṣadhakālāṃstu vibhajeddaśadhā daśa
kṣīṇadhātvindriye śānte klānte tānte bubhukṣite 53
bhaiṣajyadagdhakoṣṭhe ca bheṣajaṃ nāvacārayet
kruddhe viṣaṇṇe śokārte rātrau jāgarite tathā 54
vidagdhājīrṇabhakte ca bheṣajaṃ nāvacārayet
karmātibhārābhihate nirūḍhe sānuvāsite 55
upoṣite virikte ca bheṣajaṃ nāvacārayet
yatkiñcidapyupāttānne mūrcchite gharmatāpite 56
sadyaḥ pītodake caiva bheṣajaṃ nāvacārayet
avasthāviparītaṃ ca bheṣajaṃ nāvacārayet 57
ūnadvādaśavarṣāṇāṃ naikāntenāvacārayet
avacāritamekāntenāhanyahani cauṣadham 58
asamatvāgataprāṇadoṣadhātubalaujasām
atyantasukumārāṇāṃ kumārāṇāṃ balāyuṣī 59
kṣīṇātivṛddhakruddhānāṃ kṣīṇadhātvindriyaujasām
ekāntenauṣadhaṃ pītaṃ sūryastoyamivālpakam 60
vyādhidoṣabalāgnibhyo hīnaṃ tebhyo'dhikaṃ ca yat
ajijñāsitapūrvaṃ ca guṇaiścoktairvivarjitam 61
arogasātmyaṃ duryuktamaniṣṭaṃ manasaśca yat
yasya pītasya pākānte doṣaḥ sūkṣmo'pi lakṣyate 62
vyādheśca praśamo na syāttacca varjyaṃ vijānatā
yannāturabalaṃ hanti vyādhivīryaṃ nihanti ca 63
tadevāsyāvacāryaṃ syādāvyādhyucchedadarśanāt
kāmaṃ vyādhau praśānte'pi śamadānācchamauṣadham 64
tadevālpaṃ vidhātavyaṃ sakṛd dvistriryathābalam
puṇye'hani namasyādau devadvijabhiṣaggurūn 65
pūrvāhṇe prāṅmukhāyāsmai sukhāsīnāya sādhave
bubhūṣamāṇāya bhiṣaṅmantravadbheṣajaṃ śuciḥ 66
idaṃ hanta piba ceti saṃprayacchedudaṅmukhaḥ
aticaṅkramaṇatthānaśayanāsanabhāṣaṇam 67
krodhaśokadivāsvapnaviruddhānnahimātapān
pītauṣadho na seveta tathā strīvegadhāraṇam 68
vijṛmbhāḥ śabdavidveṣo mukhaśoṣo'ratiḥ klamaḥ
tandrīrudveṣṭanaṃ sādo liṅgaṃ jīryati bheṣaje 69
sṛṣṭirviṇmūtravātānāṃ śarīrasya ca lāghavam
udgāraśuddhirvaiśadyaṃ vaimalyaṃ hṛdayasya ca 70
prakāṅkṣā kukṣiśaithilyamannakālasya lakṣaṇam
vayastridhā vibhajyādau mātrā vakṣyāmyataḥ param 71
garbhabālakumārākhyamityetattrividhaṃ vayaḥ
yauvanaṃ madhyamaṃ vṛddhametacca trividhaṃ punaḥ 72
varṣāvaraḥ kṣīrapaḥ syādyāvat pibati vā payaḥ
vayastadbālamasmācca yāvat ṣoḍaśavārṣikaḥ 73
annādaḥ sarva eva syāt kaumāre vayasi sthitaḥ
ataḥ paraṃ dhātusattvabalavīryaparākramaiḥ 74
vardhamānaiścatustriṃśadyuvā varṣāṇyudarkataḥ
dhātvādibhiḥ sthirībhūtairyāvadāsaptatirnaraḥ 75
madhyo dhātvādibhiḥ paścāt kṣīyamāṇairyathākramam
vṛddho bhavati mandātmā pravṛttiryāvadāyuṣaḥ 76
vikalpanā'nyā vṛddhasya tasya ṣoḍaśavārṣikī
bhavedbheṣajamātrā tu kṣīyamāṇottarottarā 77
śatike śatādhike vā'pi kṣīrānnādavadiṣyate
jātamātrasya mātrā syāt sarpiṣkolāsthisaṃmitā 78
pañcarātraṃ bhavedyāvaddaśāhamadhikaṃ tataḥ
kolārdhasaṃmitaṃ yāvadviṃśadrātramataḥ param 79
kolamātraṃ bhavedyāvanmāsaṃ māsadvaye'dhikam
dvikolasaṃmitaṃ sarpistṛtīye māsi śasyate 80
śuṣkāmalakamātraṃ tu caturthe māsyudāhṛtam
pañcame māsi ṣaṣṭhe ca hyārdrāmalakasaṃmitam 81
tadevābhyadhikaṃ kiñcidvihitaṃ saptamāṣṭame
kṣīrānnādasya bālasya prāyeṇāhārasaṃkarāt 82
bhavatyaniyato vahniḥ paktau bahvanilātmanaḥ
tasyā'gnyāvekṣikī tasmāt snehamātrā vidhīyate 83
anādasya tu bhūyiṣṭhaṃ samo bhavati pāvakaḥ
tasyāmalakamātrasya sarpiṣaḥ pānamiṣyate 84
tadevāgnibalaṃ vīkṣya vardhamānasya vardhayet
kṣīrapasya kumārasya kṣīrānnadasya cobhayoḥ 85
deyaṃ snehacaturbhāgaṃ bheṣajasya yathāmayam
ghṛtena pāyayedbālaṃ yāvat syādaṣṭamāsikaḥ 86
māsādato'ṣṭamājjantorjalapiṣṭaṃ pradāpayet
ataḥ paraṃ yathāśāstraṃ kumārasyānnasevinaḥ 87
vakṣyāmi vividhāṃ mātrāṃ bheṣajānāṃ vibhāgaśaḥ
mucūṭiṃ vā prakuñcaṃ vā prasṛtaṃ vā'thavāñjalim 88
āturasya pramāṇena sametavyaṃ cikitsite
agraparvāṅguligrāhyā cūrṇamātrā tu pāṇinā 89
cūrṇānāṃ dīpanīyānāmeṣā mātrā vidhīyate
dviguṇā jīvanīyānāṃ tathā saṃśamanasya ca 90
ūrdhvabhāge tvardhamātrā tathaiva ca virecane
vātapittakaphaghnānāṃ kaṣāye tu pradāpayet 91
dvau dāpayeta prasṛtau śarkarāmadhusaṃyutau
prasṛtaṃ chardanīyasya niṣkvāthasya pradāpayet 92
tathā vairecanīyasya prasṛtaṃ nātra saṃśayaḥ
dviguṇāṃ jīvanīyasya tathā saṃśamanasya ca
dīpanīyasya kalkaṃ tu akṣamātraṃ pradāpayet 93
dviguṇaṃ jīvanīyasya tathā saṃśamanasya ca
akṣārdhaṃ chardanīyasya tathā vairecikasya ca 94
snehamātrāmato vakṣye vamane savirecane
vamane vamanīyābhiroṣadhībhiḥ susaṃskṛte 95
mātrāvattu ghṛtaṃ dadyādvamane kaphasaṃbhave
ardhamātrā bhaveddeyā vireke sarpiṣastathā 96
vairecanairvipakvasya pitte prakupite sati
mātrā'dhaścordhvabhāgā ca ślaiṣmikasya praśasyate 97
dīpanaiḥ śamanīyaiśca jīvanīyaiśca sādhitam
tathā'pi kupite vāte doṣe pakvāśaye sthite 98
kukṣigranthiṣu pārśve ca sakte deyaṃ virecanam
śamanairdīpanīyaiśca pācanīyaiśca sādhitam 99
caturbhāgaguṇaṃ dadyānmātrāyāḥ kumbhasarpiṣaḥ
pādārdhahīnaṃ pādonamardhaṃ vā'pi yathākramam 100
sarpirvidadyādbāleṣu saṃpradhārya vayobale
niṣkvāthānāṃ sakalkānāṃ cūrṇānāṃ sarpiṣastathā 101
ityuktā vividhā mātrā mātrāmūlaṃ cikitsitam
tasmādagnimṛtuṃ sātmyaṃ dehaṃ koṣṭhaṃ vayo balam 102
prakṛtiṃ bheṣajaṃ caiva doṣāṇāmudayaṃ vyayam
vijñāyaitadyathoddiṣṭāṃ mātrāṃ samyak prayojayet 103
apramattaḥ sadā ca syādbheṣajānāṃ prayojane
oṣadhīrnāmarūpābhyāṃ jānanti vanagocarāḥ 104
ajapālāśca gopāśca na tu karmaguṇaṃ viduḥ
yogaṃ tu tāsāṃ yogajñā bhiṣajaḥ śāstrakovidāḥ 105
mātrābalavidhānajñā jānate guṇakarma ca
karmajño vā'pyarūpajñastāsāṃ tattvaviducyate 106
kiṃ punaryo vijānīyādoṣadhīḥ sarvathā bhiṣak
yathā viṣaṃ yathā śastraṃ yathā'gniraśaniryathā 107
tathauṣadhamavijñātaṃ vijñātamamṛtopamam
auṣadhaṃ cāpi duryuktaṃ tīkṣṇaṃ saṃpadyate viṣam 108
viṣaṃ ca vidhinā yuktaṃ bhaiṣajyāyopakalpate
agniryathā prajvalitaḥ kruddhaścāśīviṣo yathā 109
asidhārā yathā tīkṣṇā prabhinno vā'pi kuñjaraḥ
tathauṣadhamasaṃyuktamavaidyenāvacāritam 110
viparyayeṇa mātrāyā niruṇaddhyasya jīvitam
dṛṣṭvā spṛṣṭvā tathā pṛṣṭvā kāryākāryakriyāṃ tataḥ 111
auṣadhāni prasiddhāni yāni syurbahuśo bhiṣak
rasato vīryataścaiva tāni tatrāvacārayet 112
ato'nyathā hyamātrajño yuktyāgamabahiṣkṛtaḥ
kṛśaṃ rogaparidhvastaṃ sukumāraṃ samātvikam 113
tīkṣṇauṣadhaprayogeṇa hanti cāpyatimātrayā
mahārogaṃ mahāhāraṃ mahāsattvaṃ mahābalam 114
mṛdvalpauṣadhayogena kleśayatyāturaṃ bhiṣak
upakramyo balī tasmād durbalo nirupakramaḥ 115
madhyaṃ yuktairupakramya na cāhārānnivartayet
kṛśaṃ viśrāmya viśrāmya pathyairauṣadhasādhanaiḥ 116
dhārayedvardhayedagnimagnau vṛddhe hi jīvati
yathā'nilaḥ pittakaphāsṛjaśca
nityāḥ śarīre nihitā narāṇām |
tathaiva bāleṣvapi sarvameta
ddvayostu rūpaṃ tu tadalpamalpam 117
yathā'lpadehasya tadalpamalpaṃ
tathā'nnapānauṣadhamalpamalpam |
buddhyā vimṛśyeha bhiṣagvidaddhyāt
mātrā hi dehāgnivayaḥ pradhānāḥ 118
iti ha smāha bhagavān kaśyapaḥ
iti khileṣu bhaiṣajyopakramaṇīyādhyāyastṛtīyaḥ 3
atha yūṣanirdeśīyo nāma caturthodhyāyaḥ
athāto yūṣanirdeśīyaṃ nāmādhyāyaṃ vyākhyāsyāmaḥ 1
iti ha smāha bhagavān kaśyapaḥ 2
yūṣādivyañjanopetaṃ bhojyaṃ pathyataraṃ bhavet
svasthānāmāturāṇāṃ ca viśeṣārogyakārakam 3
ataśca sarvabhūtānāmāhāraḥ sthitikāraṇam
na tvāhārādṛte'styanyat prāṇināṃ prāṇadhāraṇam 4
na cāhārasamaṃ kiñcidbhaiṣajyamupalabhyate
śakyate'pyannamātreṇa naraḥ kartuṃ nirāmayaḥ 5
bheṣajenopapanno'pi nirāhāro na śakyate
tasmādbhiṣagbhirāhāro mahābhaiṣajyamucyate 6
sa hyāharaṇasāmānyāddṛṣṭaṃ ekavidho budhaiḥ
dvividho vīryabhedena trividho doṣabhedataḥ 7
bhakṣyabhojyādibhedena tathaivoktaścaturvidhaḥ
pañcabhūtātmakatvācca punaḥ pañcavidhaḥ smṛtaḥ 8
sa eva punaruddiṣṭaḥ ṣaḍvidhaḥ ṣaḍrasāśrayāt
punardvādaśadhā bhinno dvādaśapravicārataḥ 9
caturviṃśatidhā bhūyaḥ kālādīnāṃ vikalpataḥ
pravartate tamāśritya dharmārthādricatuṣṭayam 10
svasthayātrā cikitsā ca tamevāśritya vartate
tuṣṭiḥ puṣṭirdhṛtirbuddhirutsāhaḥ pauruṣaṃ balam 11
sausvaryamojastejaśca jīvitaṃ pratibhā prabhā
āhārādeva jāyante evamādyā guṇā nṛṇām 12
tadātmavān hitamitaṃ kāle bhuñjīta ṣaḍrasam
yathā ca yacca bhoktavyaṃ ye ca bhogyaguṇāguṇāḥ 13
tatte bhojyavibhāgīye sarvaṃ vakṣyāmyataḥ param
atra te saṃpravakṣyāmi nānādravyopasaṃskṛtān 14
nānārogopaśamanān yūṣān sthavirajīvaka
rocano dīpano vṛṣyaḥ svaravarṇabalāgnikṛt 15
prasvedajanano mukhyastuṣṭipuṣṭisukhāvahaḥ
yūṣaḥ snehoṣṇabhāvācca vātaṃ snehakaṣāyataḥ 16
pittaṃ kaphaṃ kaduṣṇatvāt saṃskārācca niyacchati
yūṣadhātuṃ vadanti jñā dravīkaraṇapākayoḥ 17
dravīkaroti bhojyāni pakvaḥ sadyūṣa ityataḥ
dravyairbahuvidhairdravyaistathā cānyairataṇḍulaiḥ 18
yūṣa ityucyate siddho yavāgūstaṇḍulaiḥ saha
madgayūṣo virasikā yūṣo dāḍimakastathā 19
citrakāmalakānāṃ ca dvau yūṣau parikīrtitau
pañcakolakayūṣau dvau saṃgrāhī dīpanastathā 20
dhānyayūṣo'tha kaulatthaḥ phalayūṣaśca bhārgava
puṣpayūṣaḥ patrayūṣo valkayūṣastathaiva ca 21
mukhyaḥ pallavayūṣaśca mahāyūṣastathaiva ca
rāsnāyūṣo mahāyūṣaścāṅgeryā mūlakasya ca 22
punarnavātibalayorguḍakambalikastathā
mukhyatrikaṭuyūṣaśca laśunairvāstukena ca 23
pañcaviṃśatirityete yūṣāḥ kaśyapanirmitāḥ
yūṣāḥ kaṣāyamadhurā kaṣāyāmlāśca bhārgava 24
dvividhā vihitāḥ sarve sarve ca dravayonayaḥ
kṛtā'kṛtā'kṛtakṛtāḥ pittaśleṣmānilātmasu 25
rogeṣu snehayogācca te yūṣāstrividhāḥ smṛtāḥ
ta eva pācanāḥ proktāḥ karṣaṇā bṛṃhaṇāstathā 26
śītoṣṇamiśravīryatvānnānādravyopasaṃśrayāt
lavaṇavyoṣaṇasnehapaktisaṃskārayuktayaḥ 27
siddhā yūṣeṣu viduṣo na vakṣyāmi punaḥ punaḥ
doṣabhedena yūṣāste saṃkhyātāḥ pañcasaptatiḥ 28
tathaiva yāpanāditvāt pañcāśattu rasāśrayāt
eke yūṣāstathaikeṣāṃ yatkiñcidvyañjanaṃ dravam 29
agnau siddhamasiddhaṃ tu rāgakhāḍavapānakam
yamakasnehasiddhāstu te yūṣā ghṛtatailayoḥ 30
śaṣyante vātarogeṣu varcaḥ śoṣābhighātayoḥ
dīptāgnīnāmanidrāṇāṃ bhārādhvaśramamaithunaiḥ 31
klāntānāṃ patanādyaiśca yūṣo'yameka iṣyate
dadhikāñjikaśuktāni vargo yaścāpi dīpanaḥ 32
niryūhaḥ sarvayūṣāṇāmanyasmāt pāñcakarmikāt
kvātho niryūha ādānaṃ kaṣāyaśceti tat samam 33
garbhaḥ kalkastathā''vāpaḥ pākaḥ saṃskāra ucyate
nistuṣāṇāṃ purāṇānāṃ mudgānāṃ dīpanāmbunā 34
mudgamaṇḍastanutvāt sa mudgayūṣo ghano'lpaśaḥ
mudgatakrāmlasiddhastu yūṣo virasikā smṛtaḥ 35
sa eva dāḍimodaśvitkṛto rocana ucyate
smṛto dāḍimayūṣaśca mudgadāḍimasaṃskṛtaḥ 36
mudgāmalakaniryūho dhātrīyūṣo'bhidhīyate
ityete pañca yūṣāstu vihitāḥ pāñcakarmikāḥ 37
kāmyāṃstvanyān pravakṣyāmi yūṣānāmayadarśanāt
siddhaścitrakaniryūhe samūlaskandhapatrake 38
khyātaścitrakayūṣastu grahaṇīdoṣaśūlanut
plīhārśogulmakuṣṭhaghno hṛdrogakaphavātajit 39
tadvanmūlakayūṣo'pi sa vai saṃskāramīkṣate
śaṭīkarkaṭakībilvamājapauṣkaradhātakī 40
dadhitthaṃ dāḍimaphalaṃ cāṅgerīsasamaṅgayoḥ
pañcakolakayūṣo'yaṃ paraḥ sāṃgrāhikaḥ smṛtaḥ 41
sa eva dīpanopeto lavaṇaiścāpi dīpanaḥ
akhaṇḍitānāṃ dhānyānāṃ sarveṣāṃ samabhāginām 42
niryūhaḥ syādṛte māṣatilaniṣpāvasarṣapāt
dhānyayūṣaḥ smṛto mukhyo dvīpadāḍimasaṃskṛtaḥ 43
dadhimaṇḍe'tha vā siddhastakre vā rogadarśanāt
śiraḥkarṇāttirogeṣu hṛdroge'vardhābhedake 44
arucau cātisāre ca kāryaḥ satilamāṣakaḥ
kulatthānāṃ tu niryūhe kaulattho yūṣa ucyate 45
sannipātānilakaphavyādhīn hanti virūkṣaṇaḥ
kapitthabilvabadaradvākadāḍimacūtajaiḥ 46
phalayūṣaḥ phalairāmairjīrṇātīsāranāśanam
śaṇaśālmalidhātakyaḥ padmasaugandhikaiḥ saha 47
kovidārāt karbudārāt puṣpairyūṣaṃ prakalpayet
asṛgdare raktapitte dāhe codaracakṣuṣoḥ 48
tailāmlābhyāmṛte siddhaḥ puṣpayūṣaḥ sadāḍimaḥ
bilvaśobhāñjanairaṇḍabalārāsnāmravāriṇā 49
patraniṣkvāthayūṣaḥ syāt patrayūṣo'nilāpahaḥ
dāḍimāmrātajambūnāṃ cirabilvasya ca tvacaḥ 50
niṣkvāthya dadhimaṇḍena valkayūṣo'tisāranut
nyagrodhodumbarāśvatthaplakṣakālāpalāśajaiḥ 51
pallavaiḥ kamalānāṃ ca ghṛtadāḍimasaṃskṛtaḥ
pittarogeṣu sarveṣu garbhacyavanadāhayoḥ 52
mukhyaḥ pallavayūṣo'yaṃ hitaḥ kaṭukinīṣu ca
punarnavāyā rāsnāyāścāṅgerībalayostathā 53
pṛthagyūṣāḥ samākhyātā vātaghnā dadhisarpiṣā
rohitāpotamatsyānāṃ niryūhaṃ sādhayejjale 54
taṃ kvāthaṃ sādhayedbhūyaḥ śuktakāñjikamastubhiḥ
dravāṇi kuḍabījāni guḍapañcapale śṛtaḥ 55
eṣa kāmbaliko rūkṣaḥ kaṭutailena vā kṛtaḥ
vātarogapraśamano bṛṃhaṇo balavardhanaḥ 56
ratinidrārucikarastilatailena vā kṛtaḥ
dīpanaṃ pañcamūlaṃ ca phalāni madhurāṇi ca 57
pūrvavat sarvadhānyāni dhānyakaṃ maricāni ca
kākolīkṣīrakākolīkāśmaryāṇi parūṣakam 58
badarāṇi kulatthāśca rāsnairaṇḍapunarnavāḥ
dve pale gokṣuraḥ śigrupalāśataruṇāni ca 59
jaladroṇe pacedetaṃ niryūhaṃ pādaśeṣitam
dadhikāñjikaśuktāni prasthaśastailasarpiṣī 60
mūlakānāmapatrāṇāṃ taruṇānāṃ śataṃ bhavet
eṣa siddho mahāyūṣo vyoṣasaṃskārasaṃskṛtaḥ 61
sarvarogeṣu bhūyiṣṭhaṃ saṃsṛṣṭeṣu praśasyate
atyagniṣu vinidreṣu stabdhāṅgacchubukākṣiṣu 62
niryūheṇa samaṃ dadyānmāṃsaniryūhameva tu
kāryaḥ satilakalko vā jīrṇātīsāraśāntaye 63
ukto laśunayūṣastu svakalpe vātanāśanaḥ
sūpāśca rasakāścaiva trividhāḥ prāṅnidarśitāḥ 64
svinnāni mūlakānyapsu niṣpīḍya taruṇo vibhuḥ
paribhṛjya tataḥ snehe tata ādānamāvapet 65
eṣa mūlakayūṣastu sarvarogavināśanaḥ
anamloladakarasaḥ saṃskṛto jalasarpiṣoḥ 66
bhavet pittopaśamanastailabhṛṣṭo'nilāpahaḥ
ete yūṣāḥ svatantrotthā uktā vyāsasamāsataḥ 67
yavāgūrapi vakṣyāmi nānādravyopasaṃskṛtāḥ
nānārogopaśamanīḥ śṛṇu vṛddhaikaviṃśatim 68
odanasya vileplyāśca yavāgvāśca kimantaram
śuśrūṣe bhagavannetadityuktaḥ prāha kaśyapaḥ 69
odano viśadaḥ siddhaḥ susvinno nisruto mṛduḥ
taṇḍulaiḥ sakalaprāyairakṣīṇaiścāpi paṭhyate 70
asvinnatvamavakledastvasākalyamanisravaḥ
viraso'viśadaḥ śīta odanasya viparyayāḥ 71
dravādviṃśatibhāgena taṇḍulaiḥ saha sādhayeta
tathā pañcadaśākhyena yavāgūrdaśakena vā 72
viṃśateḥ sphuṭitaiḥ sikthaistulyādhomadhyatopari
ahastahāryā peyā syādyavāgūḥ saparigrahāḥ 73
ghanā viśīrṇā śītā ca na cāvakṣīṇataṇḍulā
picchilā viśadā'hṛdyā yavāgvā doṣasaṃgrahaḥ 74
takrasiddhā yavāgūstu dadhisiddhā ca te ghane
saṃskṛte hastahārye te pratiṣiddhe kriyāvatām 75
uṣṇā ghanā praśithilā dalitaistaṇḍulaiḥ kṛtā
vilepyā guṇadoṣāṃstu yavāgvā iva nirdiśet 76
dīrghopavāsināṃ nṝṇāṃ kṣīrapeyā praśasyate
śītapittopaśamanī bṛṃhaṇī varcabandhanī 77
śūlaghnī dīpanī peyā dīpanīyopasādhitā
pācanī pacanī coktā kaṣāyairvarcabandhanī 78
bilvaṃ dadhitthaṃ saha dāḍimena
savyoṣacāṅgerikṛtā yavāgūḥ
sāṃgrāhiṇī dīpanapācanī ca
sapañcamūlā'nilapīḍite tu 79
balā vṛṣatparṇyatha śālaparṇī
syāddāḍimaṃ bilvaśalāṭuyuktam
peyā hitā pittakaphātisāre
toyaṃ ca tattatra vadanti peyam 80
eṣaiva dadhnā rucivardhanī syānnirvāhikāṃ hanti tilopasiddhā
raktātisāraṃ śamayatyudīrṇamasṛgdaraṃ garbhaparisravaṃ ca 81
sadāḍimā sātiviṣā'tha sāmlā
peyā bhavedāmavipācanīyā
syāt kaṇṭakārīrasagokṣurābhyāṃ
saphāṇitā mūtragade yavāgūḥ 82
suvarcikākṣāraviḍaṅgaśigrusapippalīmūlakṛtā yavāgūḥ |
takropasiddhā krimināśanī syādgulme'tha kāse grahaṇīgade ca 83
mṛdvīkalājāmadhupippalībhiḥ
sasārivā tṛḍśamanī yavāgūḥ
viṣaṃ nihantyāśu tu somarājñyā
varāhaniryūhakṛtā tu balyā 84
kārśyārthamiṣṭā tu gavedhukānāṃ
sarpiṣmatī saindhavayugbalāya
dvipañcamūlodakasādhitā tu
śvāsaṃ ca kāsaṃ ca kaphaṃ ca hanti 85
śākaiḥ samāṃsaiḥ satilaiḥ samāṣaiḥ
sarpiṣmatī snehanabhedanī tu |
jambvāmrayorasthidadhitthabilvaistairamlayugvarcavibandhanī tu 86
takropasiddhā tu ghṛtāmaye syāt
piṇyākayuk saiva tu tailaroge
upodikādadhyupasādhitā tu
madaṃ vidāhaṃ ca nayet prasādam 87
śākairabhṛṣṭaiḥ paribhṛṣṭakaiśca rogāturāvekṣyupakalpayettāḥ
loke prasiddhaṃ yanmānaṃ tarkamānaṃ tulādhṛtam
tattantre'smin pramāṇaṃ syādvaktavya tatra nāsti me 88
iti ha smāha bhagavān kaśyapaḥ
iti khileṣu yūṣanirdeśīyo nāma caturthe'dhyāyaḥ
bhojyopakramaṇīyādhyāyaḥ pañcamaḥ
athāto bhojyopakramaṇīyaṃ nāmādhyāyaṃ vyākhyāsyāmaḥ 1
iti ha smāha bhagavān kaśayapaḥ 2
atha khalvasmābhiḥ pūrvaṃ yadrasavimāne'bhihitaṃ kālādicaturviṃśatividhamāhāramānaṃ tasyedānīṃ prativikalpaviśeṣānupadekṣyāmaḥ | kiṃ kāraṇam na hyāhārādṛte prāṇināṃ prāṇādhiṣṭhānaṃ kiñcidapyupalabhāmahe | sa samyagupayujyamāno jīvayati sarvendriyāṇi hlādayati dhātūnāpyāyayati smṛtimatisarvabalaujāṃsyūrjayati vaṇaprasādaṃ copajanayati asamyagupayujyamānastvasukhenopayojayati | tasmāt kāle sātmyaṃ mātrāvaduṣṇaṃ snigdhamavirodhi śucau deśe śuciṣu pātreṣu śuciparicareṇopanītaṃ prāṅmukhastūṣṇīṃstanmanā āsvādayannātidrutaṃ nātivilambitaṃ nātyuṣṇaṃ nātiśītaṃ nātirūkṣaṃ nātisnigdhaṃ nātibahu nātistokaṃ nātidravaṃ nātiśuṣkaṃ nākāṃkṣito na pratānto naikarasaṃ vā''rogyāyurbalārthī samaśnīyāt 3
bhavanti cātra--
ārogyaṃ doṣasamatā sarvābādhanivartanam
tadarthamṛṣayaḥ puṇyamāyurvedamadhīyate 4
rasāyanāni vidhivattadarthaṃ copayuñjate
dharmārthakāmamokṣāṇāmavāptiśca tadāśrayā 5
tadātmavāṃstadarthāya prayateta vicakṣaṇaḥ
annābhilāṣo bhuktasya paripākaḥ sukhena ca 6
sṛṣṭaviṇmūtravātatvaṃ śarīrasya ca lāghavam
suprasannendriyatvaṃ ca sukhasvapnaprabodhanam 7
balavarṇāyuṣāṃ lābhaḥ saumanasyaṃ samāgnitā
vidyādārogyaliṅgāni viparīte viparyayam 8
ārogyaṃ bhojanādhīnaṃ bhojyaṃ vidhimavekṣate
vidhirvikalpaṃ bhajate vikalpastu pravakṣyate 9
svasthānastheṣu doṣeṣu srotaḥsu vimaleṣu ca
jātāyāṃ ca prakāṅkṣāyāmannakālaṃ vidurbudhāḥ 10
kāle'śnato'nnaṃ svadate tuṣṭiḥ puṣṭiśca vardhate
sukhena jīryate na syuḥ pratāntājīrṇajā gadāḥ 11
sātmyaṃ nāmāhuraucityaṃ sātatyenopasevitam
āhārajātaṃ yadyasya cānuśete svabhāvataḥ 12
sātmyāśī sātmyasādguṇyācchataṃ varṣāṇi jīvati
na cāpyanucitāhāravikārairupasṛjyate 13
laghūnāṃ nātisauhityaṃ gurūṇāmalpaśastathā
mātrāvadaśnato bhuktaṃ sukhena paripacyate 14
svāsthya yātrāgniceṣṭānāmavirodhi ca tadbhavet
uṣṇaṃ hi bhuktaṃ svadate śleṣmāṇaṃ ca jayatyapi 15
vātānulomyaṃ kurute kṣiprameva ca jīryate
annābhilāṣaṃ laghutāmagnidīptiṃ ca dehinām 16
snigdhaṃ prīṇayate dehamūrjayatyapi pauruṣam
karoti dhātūpacayaṃ balavarṇau dadhāti ca 17
sumṛṣṭamapi nāśnīyādviruddhaṃ taddhi dehinaḥ
prāṇānasyā''śu vā hanyāttulyaṃ madhughṛtaṃ yathā 18
aviruddhānnabhuk svāsthyamāyurvarṇaṃ balaṃ sukham
prāpnoti viparītāśī teṣāmeva viparyayam 19
śucipātropacaraṇaḥ śucau deśe śuciḥ svayam
bhuñjāno labhate tuṣṭi puṣṭiṃ tenādhigacchati 20
nāniṣṭairamanasyairvā vighātaṃ manasorcchati
tasmādaniṣṭe nāśnīyādāyurārogyalipsayā 21
prāṅmukho'śnannaro dhīmān dīrghamāyuravāpnute
tūṣṇīṃ sarvendriyāhlādaṃ manaḥsātmyaṃ ca vindati 22
etadeva ca mātrāṃ ca pakti yuktiṃ ca tanmanāḥ
tasmāttatpravaṇo'jalpan svastho bhuñjīta bhojanam 23
āsvādyāsvādya yo'śnāti śuddhajihvendriyo rasān
sa vetti rasanānātvaṃ viśeṣāṃścādhigacchati 24
atidrutaṃ hi bhuñjāno nāhārasthitimāpnuyāt
bhojyānupūrvīṃ no vetti na cānnarasasaṃpadam 25
nātidrutāśī tatsarvamanūnaṃ pratipadyate
prasādamindriyāṇāṃ ca tathā vātānulomatām 26
śītīkaroti cānnādyaṃ bhuñjāno'tivilambitam
bhuṅkte bahu ca śītaṃ ca na tṛptimadhigacchati 27
śaityādbahutvādvairasyād bhuktaṃ kleśena pacyate
atyuṣṇabhojanājjihvākaṇṭhauṣṭhahṛdayodaram 28
dahyate na rasaṃ vetti rogāṃścāpnoti dāruṇān
mukhākṣipākavaisarparaktapittabhramajvarān 29
atiśītāśinaḥ śūlaṃ grahaṇīmārdavaṃ ghṛṇā
kaphavātābhivṛddhiśca kāso hikkā ca jāyate 30
rūkṣaṃ karoti viṣṭambhamudāvartaṃ vivarṇatām
glāniṃ bahvaśitaṃ vāyoḥ prakopaṃ mūtranigraham 31
atisnigdhāśinastandrītṛṣṇājīrṇodarāmayāḥ
bhavanti kaphamedotthā rogāḥ kaṇṭhodbhavāstathā 32
viṣṭambhodveṣṭanakleśaceṣṭāhānivisūcikāḥ
jñeyā vikārā jantūnāmatibahvaśanodbhavāḥ 33
atistokāśino'tyagnivikārāḥ kṛśatā bhramaḥ
atṛptirlaghutā nidrāśakṛnmūtrabalakṣayaḥ 34
atidravāśanājjantorutkleśo bahumūtratā
pārśvabhedaḥ pratiśyāyo viḍbhedaścopajāyate 35
atiśuṣkāśanaṃ cāpi viṣṭabhya paripacyate
pūrvajātarasaṃ jagdhvā kuryānmūtrakaphakṣayam 36
mohāt pramādāllaulyādvā yo bhuṅkte hyaprakāṅkṣitaḥ
avipākārucicchardiśūlānāhān samṛcchati 37
pratāntabhoktustṛṇmūrcchā vahnisādo'ṅgasīdanam
jvaraḥ kṣayo'tisāro vā mandatvaṃ darśanasya ca 38
daurbalyamadṛḍhatvaṃ ca bhavatyekarasāśanāt
doṣāpravṛddhirdhātūnāṃ sāmyaṃ vṛddhirbalāyuṣoḥ 39
ārogyaṃ cāgnidīptiśca jantoḥ sarvarasāśanāt
tasmādekarasābhyāsamārogyārthī vivarjayet 40
kālasātmyādinā'nena vidhinā'śnāti yo naraḥ
sa prāpnoti guṇāṃstajjānna ca doṣaiḥ prabādhyate 41
sthiratvaṃ svasthatā'ṅgānāmindriyopacayaṃ balam
kaphamedo'bhivṛddhiṃ ca kuryānmadhurasātmyatā 42
dantākṣikeśadaurbalyaṃ kaphapittāmayodbhavam
laghutāmagnidīptiṃ ca janayedamlasātmyatā 43
raktaprakopaṃ taimiryaṃ tṛṣṇāṃ durbalaśukratām
pālityaṃ balahāniṃ ca kuryāllavaṇasātmyatā 44
pakterupacayaṃ kārśyaṃ raukṣyaṃ śukrabalakṣayam
pittānilapravṛddhiṃ ca kuryāt kaṭukasātmyatā 45
kledālpatāṃ vātavṛddhiṃ dṛṣṭihāniṃ kaphakṣayam
tvagvikāropaśāntiṃ ca janayettiktasātmyatā 46
kaphapittakṣayaṃ vāyoḥ prakopaṃ paktimārdavam
kuryādraktopaśāntiṃ ca kaṣāyarasasātmyatā 47
ojastejo balaṃ varṇamāyurmedhā dhṛtiḥ smṛtiḥ
jāyate saukumāryaṃ ca ghṛtasātmyasya dehinaḥ 48
tathaiva kṣīrasātmyasya paraṃ caitadrasāyanam
dṛḍhopacitagātraśca nirmedasko jitaśramaḥ 49
balavān tailasātmyaḥ syāt kṣīṇavātakaphāmayaḥ
cakṣuṣmān balavāñchleṣmī dṛḍhasattvo dṛḍhendriyaḥ 50
dṛḍhāśrayo mandarujo māṃsasātmyo bhavennaraḥ
ahitaṃ yasya sātmyaṃ syādasātmyaṃ ca hitaṃ bhavet 51
sa śanairhitamādadyādahitaṃ ca śanaistyajet
ādau tu snigdhamadhuraṃ vicitraṃ madhyatastathā 52
rūkṣadravāvasānaṃ ca bhuñjāno nāvasīdati
bhāgadvayamihānnasya tṛtīyamudakasya ca 53
vāyoḥ saṃcaraṇārthaṃ ca caturthamavaśeṣayet
tato muhūrtamāśvasya gatvā pādaśataṃ śanaiḥ 54
svāsīnasya sukhenānnamavyathaṃ paripacyate
vīṇāveṇusvanonmiśraṃ gītaṃ nāṭyaviḍambitam 55
vicitrāśca kathāḥ śṛṇvan bhuktvā vardhayate balam
sukhasparśavihāraṃ ca samyagāpnotyato'nyathā 56
atisnigdhātiśuṣkāṇāṃ gurūṇāṃ cātisevanāt
jantoratyambupānācca vātaviṇmūtradhāraṇāt 57
rātrau jāgaraṇāt svapnāddivā viṣamabhojanāt
asātmyasevanāccaiva na samyak paripacyate 58
hitāhitaṃ yadaikadhyaṃ bhuktaṃ samaśanaṃ tu tat
pūrvabhakte'pariṇate vidyādadhyaśanaṃ bhiṣak 59
kṣuttṛṣṇoparame jāte śānte'gnau pramṛtāśanāt
viṣamaṃ guṇasaṃskārāt kramasātmyavyatikramāt 60
viruddhaṃ payasā matsyā yathā vā guḍamūlakam
syādajīrṇāśanaṃ nāma vyuṣṭājīrṇe caturvidhe 61
tathaivātyaśanaṃ jñeyamatimātropayogataḥ
sa --- tānyāmayotpattau mūlahetuṃ pracakṣate 62
āhārasātmyaṃ deśeṣu yeṣu yeṣa yathā yathā
proktaṃ tathopadeṣṭavyaṃ teṣu teṣu tathā tathā 63
caturviṃśatirityete vikalpāḥ samudāhṛtāḥ
bhiṣajā hyupadeṣṭavyā rājño rājopamasya vā 64
anyeṣāṃ vā vasumatāṃ yaśodharmārthasiddhaye
iti ha smāha bhagavān kaśyapaḥ
iti khileṣu bhojyavibhāgīyo nāma pañcamo'dhyāyaḥ 5
atha rasadoṣavibhāgīyādhyāyaḥ ṣaṣṭhaḥ
athāto rasadoṣavibhāgīyaṃ nāmādhyāyaṃ vyākhyāsyāmaḥ 1
iti ha smāha bhagavān kaśyapaḥ 2
rasadoṣavibhāgajñaḥ prakopopaśamaṃ prati
bhiṣagbhiṣaktvaṃ labhate viparyayamathānyathā 3
tasmāddoṣavikalpāṃśca vikalpāṃśca rasāśrayān
pravakṣyāmi yathāśāstraṃ saviśeṣaṃ savistaram 4
vyāsatastu jvarādīnāṃ vyādhīnāṃ doṣabhedataḥ
dviṣaṣṭidhā kalpanoktā sthūlasaṃkhyā tvataḥ param 5
ekaikaśastrayo dvandvairnava sarve trayodaśa
kṣīṇādhikasamaiścānyairdaśa dvau ca prakīrtitāḥ 6
teṣāṃ vibhāgaṃ vakṣyāmi vistareṇa yathākramam
ekaikaśastrayo jñeyā vātapittakaphairgadāḥ 7
samairdvandvaistrayaḥ ṣaṭ tu viṣamairnava te smṛtāḥ
dvyadhikaikādhikaiḥ ṣaṭ ca hīnamadhyādhikaiśca ṣaṭ 8
ekaḥ samaistribhirdoṣairityātaṅkāstrayodaśa
doṣairetairvivṛddhaiḥ syurvikalpāḥ pañcaviṃśatiḥ 9
doṣaiḥ kṣīṇairapi gadā dṛṣṭvaivaṃ pañcaviṃśatiḥ
dvikṣīṇairekavṛddhaiḥ syurekakṣīṇairdvirudbalaiḥ 10
ṣaṭ ṣaṭ kṣīṇādhikasamairdaśa dvau cāpare gadāḥ
iti dviṣaṣṭisaṃkhyaiṣā vikārāṇāṃ vikalpaśaḥ 11
vātapittakaphaireko daśa syāt prakṛtisthitaiḥ
rasānāṃ tu vikalpāḥ syurekaikaśyena ṣaṭ smṛtāḥ 12
pūrvaḥ pūrvaḥ parairyukto dvikāḥ pañcadaśāpare
raseṣu triṣu pūrveṣu vikalpāḥ syustrayo'dhikāḥ 13
dvikeṣu triṣvathaikaikaṃ saṃyojya kaṭukādibhiḥ
kaṭvādiṣu tathā pūrvaiḥ svādvamlalavaṇaiḥ pṛthak 14
aṣṭādaśaite dvābhyāṃ ca trikābhyāṃ viṃśatistrikāḥ
pūrvottarābhyāṃ madhuravyoṣādibhyāṃ yathākramam 15
ye dvikāstriṣu pūrveṣu yojyāste tribhiruttaraiḥ
pratyekaśo navaite syurvikalpāḥ ṣaḍihānyathā 16
yuktāḥ svādvamlalavaṇāḥ pṛthak kaṣṭvādibhistribhiḥ
kaṭvādayastathā pūrvairityete daśa pañca ca 17
catuṣkāḥ pañcakāḥ ṣaṭ syurekaikasya vivarjanāt
ṣaḍbhireko rasaisteṣāṃ kalpaneyaṃ triṣaṣṭidhā 18
saṃyogāḥ saptapañcāśadasaṃyuktāstu ṣaḍrasāḥ
ṣaḍeva triṣu yujyante rasā doṣeṣu yogataḥ 19
kvacidekaḥ kvacid dvau ca doṣavṛddhyā kvacittrayaḥ
hīnamadhyātivṛddhānāṃ doṣāṇāṃ tu yathākramam 20
hīnamadhyādhikairevaṃ rasaiḥ kuryādupakramam
dvyudbalaikodbalānāṃ ca samānāṃ caiva tadvidhaiḥ 21
vṛddhānāṃ kṣapaṇaṃ kāryaṃ madhyānāṃ yāpanaṃ tathā
kṣīṇānāṃ vardhanaṃ caiva rasavṛddhipramāṇataḥ 22
viśeṣo'tra yathāyogaṃ kāryo rasaviparyayāt
evaṃ dviṣaṣṭidoṣāṇāṃ rasairepāṃ dviṣaṣṭibhiḥ 23
sāmyaṃ taiḥ ṣaṭkairevaikaḥ svasthavṛttau prayujyate
kaṭutiktakaṣāyāṃstu rasān prājño yathākramam 24
yogataḥ kaphaje vyādhau bhaiṣajyamavatārayet
prayuktaḥ kaṭukaḥ pūrvaṃ paicchilyaṃ gauravaṃ ca yat 25
śleṣmaṇastaṃ nihantyāśu tiktastasmādanantaram
hrāsayatyāsyamādhuryaṃ kaphaṃ saṃśoṣayatyapi 26
saṃgṛhṇāti kaṣāyaśca snehaṃ cāsyāvakarṣati
tiktasvādukaṣāyāḥ syuḥ kramaśaḥ paittike hitāḥ 27
āmānvayatvāt pittasya pūrvaṃ tikto'vacāritaḥ
pācayatyāśu taṃ pakvaṃ tatastu madhuro rasaḥ 28
śaityādgurutvātsnehācca mādhuryācca niyacchati
taddravatvavighātārthaṃ kaṣāyaścāvacāritaḥ 29
raukṣyādviśoṣibhāvācca viśoṣayati taijasam
vātike lavaṇaḥ pūrvaṃ saṃyogādavacāritaḥ 30
prakledibhāvājjayati vibandhaṃ mātariśvanaḥ
nihanti śaityamuṣṇatvādgurutvāccāpi lāghavam 31
tathaivāmlo rasaḥ paścāttasminnevāvacāritaḥ
jaḍīkṛtāni srotāṃsi taikṣṇyādudghāṭya mārutam 32
anulomayati kṣipraṃ snigdhoṣṇatvādvimārgagam
amlādanantaraṃ paścāt prayukto madhuro rasaḥ 33
vāyorlaghutvaṃ vaiśadyaṃ rūkṣatvaṃ ca vyapohati
gurutvāt picchilatvācca snigdhatvācca yathābalam 34
ityuktāḥ sarvarogeṣu rasānāṃ pravicāraṇāḥ
dṛśyante prāyaśo yogā rogeṣūktā jvarādiṣu 35
kaṭutiktakaṣāyāśca rasato madhurāstathā
vātajvare yathāpūrvaṃ peyāyūṣarasādiṣu
lavaṇo'mlaśca yujyete rasau saṃskārayoginau 36
tato vidārigandhādirmadhuraḥ saṃprayujyate
pittajvare yathā tiktaḥ śāṅgiṣṭhādiḥ prayujyate 37
madhuraḥ sārivādiśca kaṣāyaścābhayādikaḥ
pippalyādiryathāpūrvaṃ kaṭukaḥ kaphaje jvare 38
āragvadhādikastiktaḥ kaṣāyastriphalādikaḥ
sarvatraivānayā yuktyā yathoddiṣṭānrasān budhaḥ 39
yathādoṣaṃ yathāyogaṃ prayuñjīta yathepsitam
prakṣepairavakarṣaiśca dravyāṇāmanyathānyathā 40
yathā vā vīṇayā vīṇī tantrīṇāṃ svarakovidaḥ
utkarṣaṃścāvakarṣaṃśca svarān samyak prayojayet 41
vindan nānāvikalpaiśca grāmarāgāṃśca darśayet
svaramaṇḍalatattvajño vikalpairbahuvistaraiḥ 42
tathaiva śāstratattvajño yogajñaḥ pratipattimān
vyādhau balābalajñaśca prakṛtijñaśca yogavān 43
utkarṣaṃścāvakarṣaṃśca prayogāñchāstrakovidaḥ
kevalaiḥ śītavīryaiśca tathaivoṣṇaiśca vīryataḥ 44
śītairuṣṇaiśca saṃpṛktairdravyairyogān prayojayet
kārtsnyataśca prayuñjīta yathāvadanupūrvaśaḥ 45
samuccayairvikalpaiśca nānārasasamucchrayaiḥ
yuñjīta bahudhā yogānnṛttaṃ gītavaśādiva 46
samasyoktaṃ pṛthakcoktaṃ saṃpradhārya balābalam
dviṣaṣṭirdoṣabhedā ye nirdiṣṭāstānataḥ param 47
sthānāni daśa saṃgṛhya pravakṣyāmi savistaram
traya ekaikaśasteṣāṃ ye ca vṛddhāstrayaḥ samāḥ 48
samairekaśca saptaite kṣayavṛddhyā caturdaśa
sthānavṛddhyā bhavetteṣāṃ catvāriṃśottaraṃ śatam 49
vyudasyaitānataḥ śeṣā ye triṃśaddaśa cāṣṭa ca
te bhedaṃ yāntyameyatvācchataśo'tha sahasraśaḥ 50
dvandvānāṃ viṣamāṇāṃ tu ṣaṇṇāmanyatamaṃ budhaḥ
svasthānādvardhayettāvadyāvat syāt sthānamaṣṭamam 51
ekaikaśo dviśaścaiva caturviṃśadbhavanti te
hrāsenaikaikaśaścāpi viṃśannava ca bhedataḥ 52
evamete tripañcāśaddvandvenaikena darśitāḥ
eṣaiva yuktiḥ śiṣṭānāṃ dvandvānāṃ syānna saṃśayaḥ 53
śatatrayaṃ bhavatyevameṣāmaṣṭādaśottaram
dvyudbalaikodbalānāṃ ca tāvadeva vinirdiśet 54
hīnamadhyādhikā doṣāḥ sve sve sthāne vyavasthitāḥ
svasthānāt saptamaṃ sthānamekaikaśyena yānti te 55
dvau ca dvau ca samastāśca teṣāṃ ṣaṭ saptakāstathā
bhavanti hrāsayettāṃśca yathānamadhaḥ kramāt 56
pañcāśītimatasteṣāṃ bhedānāṃ paricakṣate
eṣa eva vikalpaḥ syāccheṣāṇāṃ pravibhāgaśaḥ 57
śatāni sapta ṣaṣṭiśca dvau ca bhedā bhavanti te
evaṃ sahasraṃ bhedānāṃ vṛddhaistrīṇi śatāni ca 58
doṣairnavatiraṣṭau ca kṣīṇaistāvanta eva te
dvikṣīṇairekavṛddhaiḥ syuḥ ṣaṭ ca trīṇi śatāni ca 59
yāvantyeva ca bhedānāmekakṣīṇairdvirudbalaiḥ
kṣīṇādhikasamaiḥ ṣaṭ ca ṣaṭ ca ṣaṭ ca śatāni ca 60
krama evātra bhāgaḥ syādyo dvandveṣu nidarśitaḥ
caturdaśa vinirdiṣṭāḥ savikalpāstayoḥ pṛthak 61
taiḥ sārdhameṣāṃ sarveṣāṃ kārtsnyenaiva vibhāvayet
sahasrāṇi ca catvāri śataṃ ṣaṣṭyuttaraṃ tathā 62
etāvanto jvarādyānāṃ bhedāḥ proktā yathāgamam
athaiteṣu caturyogavibhāgagatikarmataḥ 63
sahasraṃ sannipātānāṃ vidyāt sanavakaṃ bhiṣak
ata ūrdhvaṃ rasānāṃ tu vakṣyate bhedavistaraḥ 64
karmasthānāni doṣāṇāṃ bhāvavṛddhyā yathākramam
tathā rasānāṃ ṣaṭkuryāt sthānānīti viniścayaḥ 65
tadeva karma sarveṣāṃ dvikādīnāṃ kṣayaṃ vinā
bhavatyevaṃ dvikānāṃ tu pañcaṣaṣṭyuttaraṃ śatam 66
trikānāṃ ca saviṃśāni ṣaṭśatāni vinirdiśet
catuṣkānāṃ sahasraṃ ca pañcaṣaṣṭyuttaraṃ vadet 67
pañcakāṇāṃ śatānyaṣṭau syācchataṃ ca ṣaḍuttaram
ṣaṣṭhakānāṃ śate dve tu śataṃ caikādaśottaram 68
saṃyuktānāṃ vikalpo'yaṃ savikalpāḥ ṣaḍekakaḥ
samānāṃ rasabhedānāmekamekaṃ tu piṇḍitam 69
trisaptatirbhavatyeṣāṃ sahasratrayameva ca
rasadoṣavikalpānāmatisaukṣmyādataḥ param
na vakṣyāmi mahābhāga na tu buddhiparikṣayāt 70
ataḥ paramidānīṃ rasabhedān vistareṇopadekṣyāmaḥ | tadyathā--madhurāmlalavaṇakaṭukatiktakaṣāyāḥ ṣaḍrasāḥ | eṣāmidānīṃ rasānāṃ vikalpāstriṣaṣṭirbhavanti | tatraikaikaśyena ṣaṭ | tadyathā--madhura eva amla
eva lavaṇa eva kaṭuka eva tikta eva kaṣāya eva 71
pūrvaḥ pūrvaḥ parairyukto dvikaḥ te pañcadaśa bhavanti | tatra madhuraḥ pañcabhiramlādibhiryujyate | tadyathā--madhurāmlaḥ madhuralavaṇaḥ madhurakaṭukaḥ madhuratiktaḥ madhurakaṣāya iti | amlaścaturbhirlavaṇādibhiḥ tadyathā--amlalavaṇaḥ amlakaṭukaḥ amlatiktaḥ amlakaṣāya iti | lavaṇastribhiḥ kaṭukādibhiḥ tadyathā--lavaṇakaṭuḥ lavaṇatiktaḥ lavaṇakaṣāya iti | kaṭukastiktakaṣāyābhyāṃ dvābhyāṃ tadyathā--kaṭutiktaḥ kaṭukaṣāya iti | tiktaḥ kaṣāyeṇa ca | tadyathā--tiktakaṣāya iti | te pañcadaśa eva | te dvikāḥ pañcadaśavikalpā bhavanti madhurasaṃyogena pañca bhavanti amlasaṃyogena catvāraḥ lavaṇasaṃyogena trayaḥ kaṭusaṃyogena dvau tiktasaṃyogenaika-- iti | pūrveṣu triṣu raseṣu madhurāmlalavaṇeṣu trayo'dhikā niṣpadyante | madhurāmlaḥ madhuralavaṇaḥ amlalavaṇa iti
72
eṣāṃ trayāṇāṃ dvikānāmekaiko dvikastribhiritaraiḥ kaṭutiktakaṣāyai rasairyojyaḥ tatastrikā niṣpadyante | tadyathā--madhurāmlakaṭuḥ madhurāmlatiktaḥ madhurāmlakaṣāyaḥ madhuralavaṇakaṭuḥ madhuralavaṇatiktaḥ madhuralavaṇakaṣāyaḥ amlalavaṇakaṭuḥ amlalavaṇatiktaḥ amlalavaṇakaṣāya iti | uttareṣu triṣu raseṣu kaṭutiktakaṣāyeṣu trayo dvikā niṣpadyante tadyathā--kaṭutiktaḥ kaṭukaṣāyaḥ tiktakaṣāya iti eṣāṃ trayāṇāṃ dvikānāmekaiko dvikastribhiritarairmadhurāmlalavaṇai rasairyojayitavyaḥ tatra trikā niṣpadyante | tadyathā--kaṭutiktamadhuraḥ kaṭutiktāmlaḥ kaṭutiktalavaṇaḥ kaṭukaṣāyamadhuraḥ kaṭukaṣāyāmlaḥ kaṭukaṣāyalavaṇaḥ tiktakaṣāyamadhuraḥ tiktakaṣāyāmlaḥ tiktakaṣāyalavaṇa iti | pūrve cottare ca | pūrve ca trayaḥ madhurāmlalavaṇatrika ekaḥ uttare ca trayaḥ kaṭukatiktakaṣāyatrika ekaḥ | ta ete trikā viṃśatirbhavanti prathamena
sūtreṇoktā nava nava ca dvitīyena tṛtīyena dvāviti 73
pūrveṣu triṣu raseṣu madhurāmlalavaṇeṣu trayo'dhikā ye pūrvoktāste pareṣāṃ trayāṇāṃ rasānāṃ kaṭukatiktakaṣāyāṇāṃ pūrvoktaistribhirdvikaiḥ pratyekaikaśyena yojayitavyāḥ tena catuṣkā niṣpadyante | tadyathā--madhurāmlakaṭutiktaḥ madhurāmlakaṭukaṣāyaḥ madhurāmlatiktakaṣāyaḥ madhuralavaṇakaṭutiktaḥ madhuralavaṇakaṭukaṣāyaḥ madhuralavaṇatiktakaṣāyaḥ amlalavaṇakaṭutiktaḥ amlalavaṇakaṭukaṣāyaḥ amlalavaṇatiktakaṣāya iti | pūrve madhurāmlalavaṇā uttaraiḥ kaṭukatiktakaṣāyairekaikaśo yujyante | tadyathā--madhurāmlalavaṇakaṭuḥ madhurāmlalavaṇatiktaḥ madhurāmlalavaṇakaṣāya iti | uttare trayaḥ kaṭukatiktakaṣāyāḥ pūrvairmadhurāmlalavaṇairekaikaśo yujyante tataścatuṣkā niṣpadyante | tadyathā--kaṭutiktakaṣāyamadhuraḥ kaṭutiktakaṣāyāmlaḥ kaṭutiktakaṣāyalavaṇa iti | ete catuṣkāḥ pañcadaśa pūrvasūtroktā nava dvitīyasūtroktāstrayaḥ tṛtīyasūtroktāstrayaḥ ityete catuṣkāḥ pañcadaśa 74
ṣaṭ pañcakāḥ | ṣaṇṇāṃ rasānāṃ madhurāmlalavaṇakaṭutiktakaṣāyāṇāmekaikamapanayitavyāḥ ṣaṭ pañcakā niṣpadyante | tadyathā--amlalavaṇakaṭutiktakaṣāyaḥ madhuralavaṇakaṭutiktakaṣāyaḥ madhurāmlakaṭutiktakaṣāyaḥ madhurāmlalavaṇatiktakaṣāyaḥ madhurāmlalavaṇakaṭukaṣāyaḥ madhurāmlalavaṇakaṭutikta iti | ta ete ṣaṭ pañcakāḥ | ṣaḍbhirmadhurāmlalavaṇakaṭutiktakaṣāyairekaḥ | tadyathā--madhurāmlalavaṇakaṭutiktakaṣāya iti | ta evamete rasāstriṣaṣṭidhā bhinnāḥ tatra
saṃyuktāḥ saptapañcāśat asaṃyuktāḥ ṣaṭ 75
iti ha smāha bhagavān kaśyapaḥ 76
iti khileṣu rasadoṣavibhāgīyo nāma ṣaṣṭho'dhyāyaḥ 6
atha saṃśuddhiviśeṣaṇīyo nāma saptamo'dhyāyaḥ
athātaḥ saṃśuddhiviśeṣaṇīyaṃ vyākhyāsyāmaḥ 1
iti ha smāha bhagavān kaśyapaḥ 2
siddhau viśodhanākhyāyāmanuktaṃ yadviśeṣaṇam
ūrdhvānulomayoḥ sarvaṃ tat pravakṣyāmyataḥ param 3
cayaprakopapraśamāḥ pittasya prāvṛḍādiṣu
śleṣmaṇaḥ śiśirādyeṣu vāyorgrīṣmādiṣu triṣu 4
prāvṛṭśaraddhemantākhyā visargastvṛtavastrayaḥ
śiśiraśca vasantaśca grīṣmaścādānasaṃjñitāḥ 5
visargādānayormadhye balaṃ madhyaṃ śarīriṇām
ādyantayostu daurbalyamantādyoruttamaṃ balam 6
hemante snigdhaśītābhiradbhiroṣadhibhistathā
cito'pi śaityāt praskannaḥ kapho nātra prakupyati 7
sa kupyati himāpāya saṃtapto bhāskarāṃśubhiḥ
tasmāt saṃśodhanaṃ tatra kartavyaṃ vamanottaram 8
grīṣme niṣpītasāratvādrasānāṃ vicito'pi saḥ
auṣṇyātirekāt kālasya vāyuratra na kupyati 9
raverambudasaṃrodhāt prakupyatyambudāgame
bastikarmottaraṃ tatra pratikarma viśiṣyate 10
apāṃ caivauṣadhīnāṃ ca varṣāsvamlavipākataḥ
citamapyatra tat pittaṃ varṣāśaityānna kupyati 11
divākarāṃśusaṃtaptaṃ śaratkāle prakupyati
virecanottaraṃ cātra vidhātavyaṃ viśodhanam 12
doṣaprakope sarvasmin kāle kāryamanantaram
na hi hetvīrito doṣaḥ kañcitkālamudīkṣyate 13
aucityādārtavo doṣaprakopo na tathā bhṛśaḥ
yathā hetukṛtastasmāt kṣipramenamupakramet 14
vamanaiśca virekaiśca nirūhaiḥ sānuvāsanaiḥ
tathā svāsthyamavāpnoti rogebhyaśca pramucyate 15
sthaulyāmapāṇḍutākarṇakoṭhāruḥpiḍakodbhavaḥ
nidrānāśo'ratistandrīrutkleśaḥ kaphapittayoḥ 16
śramadaurbalyadaurgandhyamālasyaṃ sīdanaṃ klamaḥ
bhaktadveṣo'vipākaśca klaibyaṃ buddherupaplavaḥ 17
bṛṃhaṇaistṛpyato'pi syādbalavarṇaparikṣayaḥ
duḥsvapnadarśanaṃ ceti bahudoṣasya lakṣaṇam 18
balinaḥ sthiradehasya tasya saṃśodhanaṃ bhiṣak
kuryāt saṃśamanaṃ caiva madhyadoṣabalasya tu 19
alpadoṣabalasyāpi yathākālaṃ viśoṣaṇam
atha saṃśodhanārhe tu snehasvedopapādite 20
vamanaṃ sraṃsanaṃ vā'pi yathāvadupakalpayet
snehaḥ pīto'nilaṃ hanti kurute dehamārdavam 21
saṅgaṃ malānāṃ nighnanti svedaḥ snigdhasya dehinaḥ
srotaḥsu līnaṃ sūkṣmeṣu doṣaṃ dravayati dravam 22
śodhanaṃ harati kṣipraṃ yathāvat saṃprayojitam
mantrapūtamabībhatsaṃ hṛdyaṃ kāryaṃ virecanam 23
sabībhatsaṃ tu vamanaṃ tathā tadyogamṛcchati
snigdho vamettṛtīye'hni caturthe sraṃsanaṃ pibet 24
vikārajāte tadyuktaṃ svasthavṛttau tu kāmataḥ
kaphavṛddhikaraṃ bhojyaḥ śvaḥ pātā vamanaṃ naraḥ 25
virecanaṃ dravaprāyaṃ snigdhoṣṇaviśadaṃ laghu
tathotkliṣṭakaphatvācca purīṣasya ca lāghavāt 26
ūrdhvaṃ cādhaśca doṣāṇāṃ pravṛttiḥ syādayatnataḥ
viṣa visarpe śvayathau vātarakte halīmake 27
kāmalāpāṇḍuroge ca nātisnigdhaṃ virecayet
nātisnigdhaśarīrāya vidadhyāt snehasaṃyutam 28
snigdhāya rūkṣaṃ rūkṣāya kāmaṃ snehavirecanam
snigdhābhāraṃ rathāt ko vā sraṃsanaṃ sahate naraḥ 29
ghṛtakumbhādyathā toyamayatnena nirasyate
nirasyate tathā doṣaḥ snigdhāddehādvirecanaiḥ 30
snigdhaṃ viṣyandayatyaṅgaṃ svedo snigdhārdramindhanam
tataḥ svinnaśarīrasya doṣān harati bheṣajam 31
yathā hi malinaṃ vāsaḥ kṣāreṇotkleśya vāriṇā
śodhyate śodhanaistadvadutkleśya vidhivadbalāt 32
vāmitaṃ laṅghayellaṅghyaṃ laghu bhojayet
tasya vāntaviriktasya kramaḥ peyādiriṣyate 33
tenāgnirvardhate sūkṣmairindhanāraṇayo yathā
samutthite'gnau saṃjāte male dehe ca nirmale 34
vāsasīvārpito rāgaḥ siddhiṃ yātyuttaro vidhiḥ
yaścātra vā yadābādhaḥ syādayogādiyogataḥ 35
samāsavyāsatastasya siddhau siddhirudāhṛtā
tato'nnapravicārasya vikalpaḥ saṃpravakṣyate 36
sarvatra trividhā peyā saṃsargādau vidhīyate
akṛtādivikalpena tato yūṣastato rasaḥ 37
vyapetalavaṇā pūrvā dīpanīyāmbusādhitā
tāni kṣudrā dvitīyā syāt kiñcillavaṇadīpanā 38
tadvadeva tṛtīyā tu saṃskṛtā snehamātrayā
avyaktalavaṇasneho yūṣastvakṛtako mataḥ 39
mandāmlalavaṇasnehasaṃskāraḥ syātkṛtākṛtaḥ
vyaktasnehāmlalavaṇaḥ kṛtayūṣaḥ susaṃskṛtaḥ 40
evameva rasaṃ vidyādimaṃ peyādikaṃ kramam
sakṛjjaghanyā dvirmadhyā triḥ śreṣṭhā śuddhimarhati 41
imāṃ peyādisaṃsargīṃ sarvasaṃśodhanopagām
ārogyakāmaḥ seveta svāsthye prakṛtibhojanam 42
varjayitvā viruddhānnaṃ gurvasātmyaṃ ca yadbhavet
ataḥ paraṃ pravakṣyāmi pramāṇādiprayojanam 43
vamanīyakaṣāyasya catvāro'ñjalayaḥ parā
mātrā madhyā trayo hrasvā dvau tadardhā virecane 44
puruṣaṃ puruṣaṃ prāpya doṣāṇāṃ ca balābalam
madanasya phalakvāthaḥ pippalīsarṣapānvitaḥ 45
grahaghnyā vā sa evā'tha paṭolāriṣṭavatsakaiḥ
yukto vā'tha priyaṅgūnāṃ kalkena madhukasya ca 46
vamanārthe vidheyaḥ syānmadhusaindhavamūrcchitaḥ
na tvajīrṇe hitaṃ tvatra lavaṇoṣṇāmbu kevalam 47
taddhi sarvaṃ samutkliśya mukhenāśu vinirharet
yadyevamāmaṃ viṣṭambhānnāpaiti tata uttaram 48
vacājagandhāmadanapippalībhistaduddharet
anyairvā kalkavihitairbībhatsodvejanauṣadhaiḥ 49
aṅgalyutpalanālādyairgalāvatudanaiḥ sukhaiḥ
tatpārśvodarapṛṣṭhānāṃ pīḍanonmadanairapi 50
pitavantaṃ ca vamanaṃ muhūrtamanupālayet
vidāhapūrvaḥ svedo'sya yadā bhavati sarvataḥ 51
viṣyandamānaṃ jānīyāttadā doṣaṃ bhiṣagvaraḥ
lomaharṣeṇa cānvakṣaṃ sthānebhyaścalitaṃ tathā 52
ādhmānodveṣṭanābhyāṃ ca nirdiśet koṣṭhamāśritam
hṛllāsāsyaparisrāvaiścāmukhī bhūtamuddharet 53
uṣṇāmbu lavaṇopetaṃ pītvā''kaṇṭhaṃ punaḥ punaḥ
yāvat syuraṣṭau ṣaḍvā'pi vegāścatvāra eva vā 54
āpittadarśanādvā'pi doṣocchitterathāpi vā
mānapramāṇato vaikādhyardhadviprasthasaṃmitam 55
pītādabhyadhikaṃ yat syāt sadoṣastadvinirgame
nirāmagandhaṃ sodgāraṃ yāvat pītamapicchilam 56
yadā vikaluṣaṃ vāntaṃ dṛśyate'mbu pratigrahe
kukṣyuraḥkaṇṭhaśirasāṃ lāghavaṃ rogamārdavam 57
klamakārśye na cātyarthamutsāho viśadātmatā
sadyo nirhṛtadoṣasya liṅgānyetāni nirdiśet 58
śirogataṃ tataścāsya tailaiḥ svinnasya dehinaḥ
doṣāvaśeṣaṃ nasyena dhūmapānena vā haret 59
yathāśuddhiṃ tataścainaṃ saṃsargeṇopapādayet
harītakyā grahaghnyā vā kalpoktaṃ syādvirecanam 60
pippalīsaindhavopetā pathyā vā trivṛtāyutā
śṛtaṃ cāragvadhaphalaṃ kṣīreṇātha rasena vā 61
triphalā vā trivṛdyuktā saghṛtavyoṣasaindhavā
tathā gandharvatailaṃ vā śreṣṭhaṃ snehavirecanam 62
daśamūlakaṣāyeṇa jāṅgalānāṃ rasena vā
drākṣākvāthena vā yuktamathavā dīpanāmbunā 63
trivṛddrākṣābhayānāṃ vā gavāṃ mūtreṇa saṃyutā
sadrākṣā trivṛtā vā syādathavā trivṛtāṣṭākam 64
pathyā trijātakaṃ vyūṣaṃ viḍaṅgāmalakaṃ ghanam
samāni ṣaḍguṇā tvatra śarkarā'ṣṭaguṇā trivṛt 65
cūrṇaṃ jvaraśramaśvāsakāsapāṇḍvāmayakṣayān
hanyātkrimiviṣārśāṃsi mūtrakṛcchraṃ ca dehinām 66
daśāvare pañcadaśa madhyame triṃśaduttame
vegā dvitricatuṣprasthapramāṇāḥ syurvirecane 67
viṭpattikaphasaṃmiśrāḥ savātāḥ syuryathākramam
pittāvasānā vamane kaṣāyakaphamūrcchitāḥ 68
samyagyoge'tiyoge'tipravṛttiḥ śoṇitottarā
ayoge tvapravṛttiḥ syādviparītā'lpaśo'pi vā 69
vibhraṃśaḥ karmaṇo bhraṃśaḥ kevalauṣadhanirgamaḥ
sarpiṣpānaṃ vikāre syādatiyogānubandhaje 70
madhukādivipakvaṃ vā tailaṃ tatrānuvāsanam
durvāntaṃ durviriktaṃ vā snigdhadehaṃ balānvitam 71
bahudoṣaṃ dṛḍhāgniṃ ca pāyayedapare'hani
durbalaṃ kramaśo bhūyaḥ snigdhasvinnaṃ viśodhayet 72
na tu duśchardanaṃ jātu krarakoṣṭhamathāpi vā
tayoḥ saṃśamanairdoṣān bastibhirvā śamaṃ nayet 73
ahṛdyamatidurgandhamajīrṇe cāti vā bahu
yasyānulomikaṃ pītamūrdhvaṃ yāti kaphāvṛtam 74
taṃ vāmitaṃ laṅghitaṃ vā parisnigdhaṃ virecayet
atyarthasnigdhadehasya viśuddhāmāśayasya vā 75
mārutasyānulomyasya yasyādho vamanaṃ vrajet
tasya saṃsargamātreṇa pariśuddhirvidhīyate 76
durbalasyālpadoṣasya mṛdu saṃśodhanaṃ hitam
vigṛhītācirāddoṣaiḥ stokaṃ stokaṃ vrajatyadhaḥ 77
uṣṇāmbupānaṃ tatra syādānulomyakaraṃ param
doṣo bhavedvā sodgāro nordhvaṃ nādhaśca gacchati 78
saśūle bheṣaje jantoḥ svedaṃ tatrāvacārayet
mātrāvirikte sodgāramauṣadhaṃ kṣipramuddharet 79
atipravṛttau jīrṇe'smin stambhanīyairupakramet
dīptāgneḥ krūrakoṣṭhasya bahudoṣasya dehinaḥ 80
sodāvartasya nirvāhya purīṣaṃ phalavartibhiḥ
susnigdhasvinnagātrasya bhiṣagdadyādvirecanam 81
yadasaktaṃ mahāvegaiḥ sukhenāśu pravartate
anābādhakaraṃ nātiglapanaṃ doṣaśodhanam 82
avyāpannaguṇodarkaṃ mātrāyuktaṃ susaṃskṛtam
pītamekāgramanasā samyakcchuddhikṛdāvahet 83
dīptāgnayaḥ karmanityā ye narā rūkṣabhojinaḥ
śaśvaddoṣāḥ kṣayaṃ yānti teṣāṃ vāyvagnikarmabhiḥ 84
viruddhādhyaśanājīrṇadoṣānapi sahanti te
svasthavṛttau na te śodhyā rakṣyā vātavikārataḥ 85
vijñāyaivaṃvidhaṃ vaidyaḥ saṃśuddhiṃ karmamarhati
iti ha smāha bhagavān kaśyapaḥ
iti khileṣu saṃśuddhiviśeṣaṇīyo nāma saptamo'dhyāyaḥ 7
atha bastiviśeṣaṇīyo nāmāṣṭamo'dhyāyaḥ
athāto bastiviśeṣaṇīyaṃ vyākhyāsyāmaḥ 1
iti ha smāha bhagavān kaśyapaḥ 2
bastidānātparaṃ nāsti cikitsā'ṅgasukhāvahā
śākhākoṣṭhagatā rogāḥ sarvārdhāṅgagatāśca ye 3
teṣāṃ samudbhave heturvātādanyo na vidyate
tathā kaphasya pittasya malānāṃ ca rasasya ca 4
vikṣepaṇe saṃharaṇe vāyurevātra kāraṇam
jetā cāsya pravṛddhasya bastitulyo na kaścana 5
tadupārdhaṃ cikitsāyāḥ sarvaṃ vātacikitsitam
karma kālaśca yogaśca tisraḥ saṃjñā yathākramam 6
vakṣye niruktanirdeśasaṃkhyādoṣavikalpataḥ
bāhu --- teḥ karmasaṃjñitam 7
atyudīrṇabale jāte prayojyaṃ tadyathāvidhi
tadardhakalanāt kālaḥ sa hi madhyabalānvaye 8
pavane pittasaṃsṛṣṭe vidhātavyo vijānatā
alpatvāt snehabastīnāṃ yukteryogaḥ sa lāghavāt 9
prayojyaḥ kaphasaṃsṛṣṭe nātitīvrabale'nile
anvāsanāścaturviṃśatirnirūhāḥ ṣaṭ ca karmaṇi 10
dvādaśā'nvāsanāḥ kāle nirūhāścātra vai trayaḥ 11
traya eva nirūhāḥ syuryoge pañcānuvāsanāḥ
karmādīnāṃ tripañcāśadbastisaṃkhyā nidarśitā 12
pañcādau karmaṇi snehāścatvāro'nte tathā'nayoḥ
madhye ṣaṇṇāṃ nirūhāṇāmantareṣu trayastrayaḥ 13
ādāvante'ntare caiva kāle snehāstrayastrayaḥ
yoge nirūhāntaritāstrayo'nte dvāviti kramāt 14
prokto vibhāganirdeśastadvikalpamataḥ śṛṇu
sapta pañca trayo vā''dau vāte garhitāḥ 15
jaghanyau pittakaphayoretāveva kadācana
tāṃ tāmavasthāmanvīkṣya doṣakālabalāśrayām 16
utkarṣeccāvakarṣecca bastīn dravyāṇi vā bhiṣak
kuryādyoge tathotkarṣamapakarṣaṃ tu karmaṇi 17
kāle tadubhayaṃ caiva vīkṣya doṣabalābalam
vāte samāṃśaḥ snigdhoṣṇo nirūhaḥ pānatailikaḥ 18
ṣaḍbhāgasnaihikau pitte sakṣīrau svāduśītalau
trayaḥ samūtrāstīkṣṇoṣṇaḥ śleṣmaṇyaṣṭāṅgatailikāḥ 19
sakṛtpraṇihito vātamāśayasthaṃ nirasyati
sapittaṃ sakaphaṃ dvistrirata ūrdhvaṃ na śasyate 20
śasyate'tra rasakṣīrayūṣāśanavidhiḥ kramāt
athavā balakālāgnideśaprakṛtisātmyataḥ 21
etā doṣavikalpāntā nirdiṣṭā bastiyuktayaḥ
eṣa cāpyaparaḥ kalpaścaturbhadra iti smṛtaḥ 22
catvāro bastayaḥ pūrvamante catvāra eva ca
tayorāsthāpanaṃ madhye kalpaḥ so'yaṃ niratyayaḥ 23
dvistrirvā'rthavaśādeṣa kriyamāṇaḥ sukhāvahaḥ
jvarādibhiḥ parikliṣṭe hīnavarṇabalaujasi 24
jātānuvāsanāvasthe balapuṃstvāgnivardhanāḥ
ayugmā bastayo deyā na tu yugmāḥ kathañcana 25
viṣamā viṣamaireva hanyante bastibhirgadāḥ
ekastrayo vā kaphaje paittike pañca sapta vā 26
vāte navaikādaśa vā yo yadāpnoti vā samam
iti sūkṣmavicitrārthamuktaṃ vyāsasamāsataḥ 27
vijñāyaitat prayoktavyaṃ yathā vakṣyāmyataḥ param
gambhīrānugatā yasya krameṇopacitā malāḥ 28
kupitā vātabhūyiṣṭhā bastisādhyā viśeṣataḥ
saṃpannasya sahiṣṇośca karma tasya parāyaṇam 29
ato madhyasya kālaḥ syādavarasyāvarastathā
snehasvedopapannasya vāmitasya yathākramam 30
snigdhasvinnasya tu punarviriktasya krame gate
dattānuvāsanasyāsya yathāyogaṃ tatastryahāt 31
kṣaṇikasya praśāntasya nirūhamupalakṣayet
tribhiranvāsitasyātaḥ saptāhaḥ karmakālayoḥ 32
punarāsthāpanaṃ kāryaṃ yogaḥ syāt pañcame'hani
svabhyaktasvinnagātrasya kālyamaprātarāśinaḥ 33
koṣṭhānu sā''maṃ śākhābhyaḥ samyaksaṃvāhitasya ca
nirūhaṃ yojayet prājñaḥ sarvopakaraṇānvitaḥ 34
haime raupye'thavā kāṃsye sumṛṣṭe bhājane same
prakṣipyaikaikaśo dravyaṃ yat krameṇopadekṣyate 35
bhiṣaṅnirūhaṃ mṛdnīyāt prāṅmukhaḥ susamāhitaḥ
pūrvamevātra nikṣepyaṃ madhunaḥ prasṛtadvayam 36
saindhavasyārdhakarṣaṃ ca tailaṃ ca madhunaḥ samam
tataśca kalkaprasṛtaṃ kvāthaṃ kalkacaturguṇam 37
prasṛtau māṃsaniryūhānmūtraprasṛtameva ca
dvādaśaprasṛto bastirityevaṃ khajamūrcchitaḥ 38
yathārthaṃ ca yathāvacca praṇidheyo vijānatā
syāccedvivakṣā dravyāṇāṃ prakṣepaṃ prati kasyacit 39
tatra vācyamidaṃ vyastakramasaṃyogakāraṇam
maṅgalyaṃ maṅgalārthāya madhu pūrvaṃ niṣicyate 40
paicchilyaṃ bahulatvaṃ ca kaṣāyatvaṃ ca mākṣike
bhinatti lavaṇaṃ taikṣṇyāt saṅghātaṃ ca niyacchati 41
madhuno'nantaraṃ tasmāllavaṇāṃśo niṣicyate 25
tatastailaṃ vinikṣiptamekībhāvāya kalpate 42
kalkaḥ saṃsṛjyate cāśu kvāthaśca samatāṃ vrajet
snehakalkakaṣāyāṇāmevaṃ saṃmūrcchane kṛte 43
mūtraṃ paṭutvaṃ kurute vīryaṃ codbhāvayatyapi
samyagevaṃ vimṛditaḥ srotobhyaḥ kaphamārutau 44
viṣyandayati pittaṃ ca kṣipraṃ caiva haratyapi
ato'nyathā mṛdyamāno na śleṣamadhigacchati 45
asamyaṅmathitaḥ śliṣṭo bastirnārthāya kalpate
tata eṣa kramo dṛṣṭo nirūhasyopayojane 46
pramāṇaṃ ca prakṛṣṭasya prasṛteryadudāhṛtam
tasmāt pramāṇādutkarṣo vayobalavadiṣyate 47
apakarṣastu kartavyaḥ saṃpradhārya vayobalam
guṇatastūbhayatvena dṛṣṭvā vyādhibalābalam 48
utkarṣaṇaṃ yadaṅgena tadaṅgenāpakarṣayet
śītoṣṇasnigdharūkṣāṇāṃ dravyāṇāmupakalpayet 49
svādvamlalavaṇoṣṇānāmutkarṣaṃ nātimātraśaḥ
vātavyādhau bhiṣakkuryāt snehasya tu vidhāpayet 50
rūkṣāṇāṃ śītavīryāṇāmapakarṣaṃ ca yuktitaḥ
svādutiktakaṣāyāṇāṃ vyādhau pittottare bhiṣak 51
utkarṣamapakarṣaṃ tu kuryāttikṣṇoṣṇayogyathā
tīkṣṇoṣṇarūkṣadravyāṇāmutkarṣaṃ tu kaphottare 52
viparyayaṃ viparyaye guṇānāṃ ca prakalpayet
saṃsṛṣṭadoṣe saṃsṛṣṭaguṇadravyāṇi yojayet 53
āsthāpanaṃ duṣprayuktaṃ bhavatyāśīviṣopamam
suprayuktaṃ tadeveha prāṇināmamṛtopamam 54
prāyo yatra guṇādhikyaṃ samyagyogena lakṣyate
tadapramādaṃ kurvīta bastikarmaṇi buddhimān 55
na hi tādṛgvidhaṃ kiñcit karmānyadupapadyate
kṣipraṃ rogābhighātāya rogāṇāṃ copapattaye 56
vyādhyāturāgnibhaiṣajyabalaṃ prakṛtimeva ca
vayaḥ śarīramaucityaṃ saukumāryaṃ sāhaṣṇutām 57
pradhārya buddhyā matimām̐stattatkarmāvacāraṇam
avasthāyāmavasthāyāṃ kuryāt samyagatandritaḥ 58
nātiśītaṃ na cātyuṣṇaṃ nātitīkṣṇaṃ nacetarat
nātirūkṣamatisnigdhaṃ nātisāndraṃ na ca dravam 59
nātimātraṃ na cātyalpaṃ nirūhamupakalpayet
atiśīto'tiśaityena skanno bātabalāvṛtaḥ 60
bhṛśaṃ stambhayate gātraṃ kṛcchreṇa ca nivartate
atyuṣṇaḥ kurute dāhaṃ mūrcchāṃ cāśu nireti ca 61
atitīkṣṇastathaivāsya jīvādānaṃ karoti vā
mando na doṣān harati dūṣayatyeva kevalam 62
karṣayatyatirūkṣaśca mārutaṃ ca prakopayet
snigdho'tijāḍyaṃ kurute vyāpādayati cānalam 63
kṣapayatyatisāndrastu na vā netrādviniṣkramet
atidravo'lpavīryatvādayogāyopapadyate 64
alpamātro na cāpyeti kṛcchrādvā'pi nivartate
atimātro'tiyogāya tasmādete vigarhitāḥ 65
yathāvanmūrcchito mṛtsno bhojyoṣṇalavaṇaḥ samaḥ
bālakāṣṭhauṣṭhajihvānāṃ yonidāhapravartakaḥ 66
śroṇibastikaṭīpārśvanābhimūlodarāśritaḥ
samyaksamucchrayaṃ kṛtsnaṃ vīryataḥ pratipadyate 67
ūrdhvabhāgairbalān kṣipto mārutairiva pāvakaḥ
pittasthānamatikramya svalpamākṣipate kapham 68
tīkṣṇo mātrāśatādūrdhvaṃ nātitīkṣṇaḥ prayujyate
na tiṣṭhati mṛdustiṣṭhatyadhikaṃ vā'pi yāpanaḥ 69
ānulomyādapānasya gudasyāropaṇādbhṛśam
taddvitīyastṛtīyo vā kālamalpataraṃ tathā 70
doṣān sthūlāṃśca sūkṣmāṃśca gambhīrānugatānapi
viṣyandayati viṣṭabdhān sapurīṣān prakarṣati 71
na kuryādvyāpadaḥ kāścit sukhena ca nivartate
yukto yuktena bhiṣajā sa bastiḥ saṃpraśasyate 72
vayasaḥ sthāpano vṛṣyaḥ svaravarṇabalāgnikṛt
vātapittakaphānāṃ ca malānāṃ cāpakarṣaṇaḥ 73
bālavṛddhavayasthānāṃ kṣipramūrjaskaraḥ param
sarvendriyāṇāṃ vaiśadyaṃ kurute cāṅgamārdavam 74
evamete samākhyātā nirūhasya guṇāguṇāḥ
purīṣaṃ mārutaḥ pittaṃ kaphaśca kramaśo yadā 75
pravartante ca phenaṃ ca śaṅkhasphaṭikasannibham
samyaṅgirūḍhagātrāṇāṃ mārdavaṃ janayet param 76
annābhilāṣo vaiśadyaṃ laghutā vā'tha mārdavam
sṛṣṭaviṇmūtravātatvamindriyāṇāṃ prasannatā 77
ayoge viparītaṃ syādatiyoge'tivartanam
kaphapittāsṛjāṃ māṃsaprakṣālananibhasya vā 78
hikkā kampastṛṣā glānirgātrabhedastamaḥ klamaḥ
nidrānāśaḥ pralāpaśca yatra cāpyupajāyate 79
samyaṅnirūḍhamāśvastaṃ pariṣiktaṃ sukhāmbunā
tanu vā bhojayenmātrāṃ jāṅgalānāṃ rasena vā 80
bhuktavantaṃ ca tailasya prasṛtenānuvāsayet
vāyuḥ praśāmyate tena nirūheṇa pracālitaḥ 81
āsthāpano bastirayaṃ gudanirvāpaṇaṃ naraḥ
ekāntaraṃ tataścordhvaṃ yathoktamanuvāsanam 82
dīptāgnerdṛḍhadehasya sodāvarte vimārgate
śroṇivaṅkṣaṇasaṃsthe ca vāte śastaṃ dine dine 83
tasya pakvāśayagataḥ snehamātrāṃ prabhañjanaḥ
balavān balavatyagnau vārivat sa viśodhayet 84
na ca tailāt paraṃ kiñciddravyamastyanilāpaham
snehādraukṣyaṃ gurutvācca laghutvaṃ mārutasya tu 85
auṣṇyācchaityaṃ nihantyāśu tailaṃ puṣṭi karoti ca
manaḥprasādaḥ snehaṃ ca balavarṇamathāpi ca 86
syātsnigdhaviṭapaskandhaḥ komalāṅkurapallavaḥ
mūle sikto yathā vṛkṣaḥ kāle puṣpaphalapradaḥ 87
snehabasternarastadvad dṛḍhakāyo dṛḍhaprajaḥ
vātā makairvikāraiśca pūrvoktairnābhibhūyate 88
pañcamūlāḍhake'dhyardhaṃ phalānāmāḍhakaṃ bhavet
yavakolakulatthānāṃ kuḍavāḥ syustrayaḥ pṛthak 89
caturbhāgāvaśiṣṭaṃ tu paścādaṣṭaguṇe jale
mastunaścāḍhakenaitattailaprasthaṃ vipācayet 90
kuṣṭhasya śatapuṣpāyā vacāyā madhukasya ca
kuṭajasya ca bījānāṃ bījānāṃ madanasya ca 91
yavānyāḥ pippalīnāṃ ca hareṇvā devadāruṇaḥ
bilvasya devapuṣpasya rāsnāyā mustakasya ca 92
sūkṣmailāyāḥ priyaṅgvāśca bhāgairakṣasamaiḥ pṛthak
siddhaṃ sulavaṇaṃ pūtaṃ nidadhyādbhājane śucau 93
etanmandanirūḍhānāṃ bastivyāpatsu cottamam
phalatailamiti khyātamudāvartanivartanam 94
tathaivodariṇāṃ siddhaṃ gulimināṃ krimikoṣṭhinām
pṛṣṭhaśroṇyūrujaṅghāsu vāteṣvapraguṇeṣu ca 95
nirūhasādhyā ye kecidvikārāḥ samudāhṛtāḥ
tāñjayedbastinā'nena mūtrāghātāṃśca nāśayet 96
eraṇḍamūlatriphalābalārāsnāpunarnavāḥ
guḍūcyāragvadho dāru palāśo madanaṃ phalam 97
mūlaṃ turaṅgagandhāyāḥ pañcamūlaṃ kanīyasam
palapramāṇānyetāni jaladroṇe vipācayet 98
aṣṭabhāgāvaśeṣaṃ taṃ paripūtaṃ samāharet
karṣapramāṇānyetāni ślakṣṇapeṣyāṇi kārayet 99
śatāhvā madhukaṃ mustā priyaṅgurhapuṣā vacā
rasāñjanaṃ tārkṣyaśailaṃ pippalyaḥ kauṭajaṃ phalam 100
khajena mathitaḥ koṣṇaḥ satailamadhusaindhavaḥ
samūtramāṃsaniryūho nirūhaḥ sādhuyojitaḥ 101
lekhano dīpano balyo grahaṇyarśovikāranut
pārśvapṛṣṭhakaṭīśūlaṃ pārśvajaṅghorujā rujaḥ 102
eraṇḍabastiḥ śamayenmārutaṃ ca kaphāvṛtam
yuktamātroṣṇalavaṇaḥ snehabastirvidhīyate 103
samāsataḥ sa dvividhastasya mātrā pracakṣyate
prakuñcaḥ kanyasī mātrā tato'dhyardhā tu madhyamā 104
uttamā dvipalā mātrā mātrābastau tu bhārgava
apastanasyārdhapalaṃ parihāryā niratyayā 105
karvatrayaṃ trivarṣasya caturvarṣasya vai palam
ṣaḍvarṣasya tu bālasya sva eva prasṛtaḥ smṛtaḥ 106
dvau dvau dvādaśavarṣāṇāṃ catvāraḥ prasṛtāstathā
deyāḥ ṣoḍaśakādīnāṃ pūrvāhṇe vā'ntareṣu ca 107
yāvanmadhyaṃ vayo vārdhe tvapakarṣedyathākramam
samīkṣya dehadoṣāgnibalaṃ prakṛtimeva ca 108
snehapramāṇaṃ yadbastau nirūhastriguṇastataḥ
ativyavāyavyāyāmapānayānādhvasaṅginaḥ 109
vayasthāḥ snehasātmyāśca yeṣāṃ cāgnibalaṃ dṛḍham
yeṣāṃ cādhaḥ prakupito vāyurvātātmakāśca ye 110
teṣūttamāṃ praṇidadhet snehamātrāṃ vicakṣaṇaḥ
ya ebhyo madhyamāvasthāḥ puruṣāsteṣu madhyamām 111
vayovyādhibalāvekṣāmitarāmitareṣu ca
iti karmādibastīnāṃ tritayaṃ samudāhṛtam 112
nirdeśaśca vikalpaśca pravibhāgaśca kārtsnyataḥ
yacca yasmin vidhātavyaṃ yā mātrā yeṣu yujyate 113
nirūhayuktiḥ snehaśca nirūhaśca prakīrtitaḥ
iti ha smāha bhagavān kaśyapaḥ 114
iti khileṣu bastiviśeṣaṇīyo nāmāṣṭamo'dhyāyaḥ 8
atha raktaśulmaviniścayādhyāyo navamaḥ
athāto raktagulmaviniścayamadhyāyaṃ vyākhyāsyāmaḥ 1
iti ha smāha bhagavān kaśyapaḥ 2
bhagavantamṛṣiśreṣṭhaṃ sarvaśāstravidāṃ varam
kaśyapaṃ bhārgavo dhīmān paryapṛcchat prajāpatim 3
raktagulmaḥ kathaṃ strīṇāṃ jāyate durupadravaḥ
atha kasmātkumārāṇāṃ kanyānāṃ ca na jāyate 4
raktagulmaḥ kathaṃ cāsau raktagulma iti smṛtaḥ
kasmānniścetanatve'pi garbhaceṣṭā viceṣṭate 5
dūrāntaraṃ na tvanayoścetanācetanāvatoḥ
viprakṛṣṭāntare'pyasmin garbhaśoṇitagulmayoḥ 6
kecidviśeṣaṃ necchanti kecidicchanti liṅgataḥ
tayorviśeṣo yadyasti kimarthaṃ sa upekṣyate 7
yukto garbhe dohadasya kṣīrasya ca samudbhavaḥ
āpāṇḍugaṇḍatādīnāṃ liṅgānāṃ ca samudbhavaḥ 8
na yuktamiva paśyāmi tasminneṣāṃ samudbhavam
raktagulme'tha dṛśyante liṅgānyetāni tat katham 9
kasmādādaśamānmāsāt paripākaṃ niyacchati
ekadvitricatuṣpañcaṣaṭsaptāṣṭanavādiṣu 10
māseṣu bhedaṃ nāpnoti prāyo garbhavadāsthitaḥ
nārīṇāṃ sukumārīṇāṃ sa kaṣṭa iti me matiḥ 11
upakramyaḥ kathamayaṃ kaścāsyopakramaḥ smṛtaḥ
kasyāṃ kasyāmavasthāyāṃ kā kā vā'syāvacāraṇā 12
kasmin kāle ca nirbhedyo bhedanīyaṃ ca kiṃ bhavet
vinirbhinne ca kiṃ kāryametadācakṣva me vibho 13
iti pṛṣṭaḥ sa śiṣyeṇa provāca vadatāṃ varaḥ
raktagulmastu nārīṇāṃ jāyate yena hetunā 14
yena caiva kumārāṇāṃ kanyānāṃ ca na jāyate
tat sarvamabhidhāsyāmi vistareṇa nibodha me 15
viṇmūtrakrimipakvāmakaphavātāśayāḥ pṛthak
saptaite dehināṃ koṣṭhe strīṇāṃ garbhāśayo'ṣṭamaḥ 16
rajovahāḥ sirā yasmin rajaḥ pravisṛjantyataḥ
puṣpabhūtaṃ hi taddaivānmāsi māsi pravartate
viparyayāttadeveha tatraiva tu nicīyate 17
anena hetunā strīṇāṃ raktagulmo hi jāyate
tadāśayasya cābhāvātpuruṣāṇāṃ na jāyate 18
hīnayonyāstu bālāyāḥ kāyaṃ gacchati śoṇitam
atha pūrṇasvabhāvāyāḥ kāyaṃ yoni ca gacchati 19
garbhamaṅge bhāvayati kiñcit stanyāya kalpate
paktaye śoṇitādyastu śeṣaḥ kāyaṃ samindhati 20
tathaiva garbhaḥ sūtāyāḥ sadyaḥ stanyāya kalpate
śeṣaṃ tu rudhirībhūtaṃ kāyaṃ yoniṃ ca sarpati 21
dhātuṣu pratipūrṇeṣu śarīre samavasthite
saṃcitaṃ rudhiraṃ yoniḥ punaḥ kālena muñcati 22
yadā raktavahā raktaṃ pradoṣānnānupadyate
vimārgādyonimanveti vikṛtistena jāyate 23
tathaiva raktagulmo'pi hetunā'nena jāyate
yadā ṛtumatī nārī prāptān vegān vidhārayet 24
hriyā trāsādvyavāyādvā vartamānānadhogatān
evamādibhirapyanyairudāvṛttaiḥ prakopitaḥ 25
vāyuḥ śoṇitamādāya pratisrotaḥ prapadyate
garbhāśayamudāvṛttastasyā vahati goṇitam 26
mārutaścyutagarbhāyā yadā mithyopacaryate
tasyāḥ sa vāyurudvṛttaḥ pratighātāt saśoṇitaḥ 27
gatvā garbhāśayaṃ ruddhaḥ sthiratvamupapadyate
saṃvṛttaṃ śoṇitaṃ tatra māruto viṣamaṃ gataḥ 28
rajovahāḥ samāvṛttaḥ saṃstambhayati garbhavat
sa gulmaḥ spandate'bhīkṣṇaṃ mārutena samīritaḥ 29
darśayan yāni rūpāṇi tāni vakṣyāmi sarvaśaḥ
kāsate śūlyate caiva jvaryate'thātisāryate 30
manyate sarvagātrāṇi mūrcchitāni gurūṇi ca
tamo'syā jāyate'bhīkṣṇaṃ kārśyaṃ caiva nigacchati 31
vamatyabhīkṣṇaśo bhuktamannaṃ cāsyai na rocate
jāyante codare gaṇḍā nīlaṃ cāsyāḥ pradṛśyate 32
stanāntaraṃ ca nābhiśca lomarājī ca mūrcchitā
oṣṭhau ca kṛṣṇau bhavatastathaiva stanacūcukau 33
payodharau prasicyete dohadaṃ ca nigacchati
nānārasān prārthayate niṣṭhīvati muhurmuhuḥ 34
śubhādudvijate gandhādvarṇaścāsyāḥ prasīdati
garbhiṇyā yāni rūpāṇi tāni saṃdṛśya tattvataḥ 35
varṣāṇi harati vyādhiṃ garbho'yamiti duḥkhitā
kenacittvatha kālena nirbhedaṃ yadi gacchati 36
tato gulmapramuktā sā jñātimadhye prabhāṣate
garbhiṇyahaṃ ciraṃ bhūtvā pracyute garbhaśoṇite 37
garbharūpaṃ na paśyāmi tatra me saṃśayo mahān
tāmidaṃ pratibhāṣante sarvagrāmakutūhalām 38
divyo garbhā vyatikrānto naigameṣeṇa te hṛtaḥ
ityenāmabudhāḥ prāhurhṛtaṃ sarvamaśobhanam 39
paripluta iti prāhuḥ kuśalā ye manīṣiṇaḥ
gulmaścaya iti prokto raktaṃ rudhiramucyate 40
raktasya saṃcayastena raktagulma iti smṛtaḥ
garbhavacceṣṭate nāyaṃ kintu sādṛśyadarśanāt 41
garbho'yamiti manvānā manasā tadvibhāvinī
nārī viceṣṭate tāstā garbhaceṣṭāḥ pṛthagvidhāḥ 42
dohadaṃ yat karotīti śṛṇu tatrāpi kāraṇam
ya eva hi rasāḥ prāyo dhātūnāṃ vṛddhihetavaḥ 43
teṣāmevābhilāṣaḥ syādyonisādharmyatattvataḥ
vātapittānvitaṃ raktaṃ cīyamānaṃ vikāravat 44
kaṣṭvamlalavaṇādīnāṃ rasānāṃ gṛddhimāvahet
garbhiṇyasmīti tatprītipremasaṃkalpasaṃbhṛtaḥ 45
prasruto jāyate nāryāstena stanyaṃ pravartate
sarvā rasavahā nāḍyaḥ samantānnābhimāśritāḥ 46
garbho vivardhamānaśca saṃpīḍayati tāḥ striyāḥ
tadvacca raktagulmo'pi pīḍayannupacīyate 47
tābhiśca pīḍyamānābhirna samyagvartate rasaḥ
āpāṇḍugaṇḍatādīni lakṣaṇāni bhavantyataḥ 48
kathaṃ prakarṣate kālamiti tatrāpi me śṛṇu
vivṛddheriha sārūpyādgarbho'yamiti niścitā 49
saṃrakṣate'bhighātebhyaḥ kukkuṭyaṇḍamivāṅganā
tadapāyakarān hetūnna kathaṃcana sevate 50
śramopavāsatīkṣṇoṣṇakṣārādīni ca sarvaśaḥ
sa evaṃ yāpyamānastu yathākālaṃ prakarṣate 51
vyāpattihetumāsādya kālenālpena vā punaḥ
bhedaṃ gacchatyadhastāddhi jalakumbha iva kṣataḥ 52
kecidicchanti gulmasya māsādādaśamātparam
paripākaṃ phalasyeva svakālapariṇāmataḥ 53
tasmiṃśca kāle sa vyādhiḥ syānnātidurupakramaḥ
tatropakramamicchanti tasya kartumato budhāḥ 54
aprāptakālo yāpyaḥ syādgarbhavadyuktikovidaiḥ
nāpakvo bhidyate vyādhiriti mattvā yathābhavam 55
viśeṣaṃ raktagulmasya garbhasya ca nibodha me
aṅgapratyaṅgavān garbhastaireva ca viceṣṭate 56
raktagulmastu vṛttaḥ syālloṣṭavacca viceṣṭate
sthānātsthānaṃ vrajan garbho vyāviddhaṃ parivartate 57
nābheradhastādgulmo'yamavyāviddhaṃ vivartate
ānupūrvyeṇa garbhaśca ahanyahani vardhate 58
viparītaṃ hi gulmastu mandaṃ mandaṃ vivardhate
tāṃ tāmavasthāṃ garbhastu māsi māsi prapadyate 59
garbhiṇī nānimittaṃ ca jvaryate dahyate'pi vā
gulminī hyanimittaṃ tu jvaryate dahyate'pi vā 60
asmin viśeṣe'pi sati saṃdeho jāyate mahān
nānāgarbhavikārāṇāṃ saṅkarādbhiṣaje mataḥ 61
saṃbhūya saha saṃmantrya bhiṣagbhiḥ śāstrakovidaiḥ
kāle cikitsāṃ kurvīta yathā vakṣyāmyataḥ param 62
alpāntarāvubhāvetau garbho gulmaśca raktajaḥ
tadyathāvadviditvā''dau kriyāṃ kuryādbhiṣagvaraḥ 63
yo hi gulme garbha iti garbhe vā gulma ityapi
kriyāṃ kuryādayaśasā enasā caiva yujyate 64
atastu saṃśaye jāte kuryāt sādhāraṇīḥ kriyāḥ
nopakramedaviditaṃ rogaṃ kañciccikitsakaḥ 65
atha kāle tvasaṃpūrṇe saṃdigdhe cāpi darśane
hetunā kenacidraktaṃ sravettaṃ cāśu vārayet 66
pūrṇe prasavakāle tu na raktaṃ prativārayet
tatrānuvāsanaṃ dadyād dravaṃ snigdhaṃ ca bhojanam 67
vidhinā'nena garbhaścet sukhena prasaviṣyati
athavā raktagulmaḥ syāt so'pyakṛtsnena bhetsyate 68
etasmātkāraṇādraktaṃ pravṛttaṃ na nivāryate
raktagulme prathamato yuktyā snehopapādanam 69
śastaṃ bāhusirāyāśca vedhanaṃ pākavāraṇam
tathā saṃśamanīyaṃ ca doṣaśeṣāvakarṣaṇam 70
kalyāṇakaṃ pañcagavyaṃ ṣaṭpalaṃ tiktameva vā
sarujāṃ pāyayennārīṃ doṣavit karmakovidaḥ 71
tīkṣṇairāsthāpayedenāṃ yuktitaścānuvāsayet
pathyāni bhojayeccaiva kṣīrayūṣarasādibhiḥ 72
annapānāni rūkṣāni vidāhīni gurūṇi ca
vyāyāmaṃ maithunaṃ cintāṃ gulminī tu vivarjayet 73
jvarāruciśvāsakāsaśoṣakārśyārativyathāḥ
śophaścopadravā gulme tāṃścikitset svabheṣajaiḥ 74
bilvaśyonākaniryūhe sādhitairjāṅgalaiḥ rasaiḥ
śaithilyakaraṇārthaṃ ca raktagulmasya bhojayet 75
yūṣeṇa vā kulatthānāṃ lāvasaṃskārikeṇa vā
cālanārthaṃ virekaṃ ca trivṛttriphalayā pibet 76
vāyorupaśamārthaṃ ca phalatailānuvāsitām
āsthāpayet sakṛd dvirvā śūlāṭopanivṛttaye 77
tulyaṃ madhu ca tailaṃ ca tābhyāmuṣṇodakaṃ samam
dvau karṣau śatapuṣpāyāḥ karṣārdhaṃ saindhavasya ca 78
etenāsthāpayennārīṃ daśamūlādikena vā
balaṃ cāpyāyayettasyā rasaiḥ kṣīraiśca saṃskṛtaiḥ 79
upakramettataścūrṇairetaiḥ śodhanapātanaiḥ
harītakī vacā hiṅgu saindhavaṃ sāmlavetasam 80
yavānī yāvaśūkaṃ ca cūrṇamuṣṇāmbunā pibet
harītakīyavakṣārasauvarcalamiti trayam 81
ghṛtayuktaṃ pibedyuktyā raktagulmasya bhedanam
patrailāpippalīśuṇṭhīcūrṇaṃ vā viḍasaṃyutam 82
nāgaraṃ śukticūrṇaṃ vā pibedgomūtrasaṃplutam
sūkṣmailākuñcikācavyapippalīcitrakasya vā 83
kalkaṃ balvajayūṣādyaiḥ pibenmaṇḍodakena vā
aparāpātanoddiṣṭairauṣadhaiścāpi bhedayet 84
atipravṛttaṃ rudhiraṃ glāniṃ janayate yadi
vinirhṛte gulmadoṣe sāvaśeṣe'pi vā bhiṣak 85
punarāsthāpanoktena tatra kuryādbhiṣagjitam
anubandhabhayāccaiva śanaistadanuśodhayet 86
padmādīni samūlāni dagdhvā tadbhasma saṃharet
gāḍhayitvā ca tatkvāthaṃ cūrṇairetairvipācayet 87
śuṇṭhīpippalikuṣṭhaiśca cavyacitrakadārubhiḥ
darvipralepitaṃ siddhamabhyasettena śuddhyati 88
śilājatvabhayāriṣṭaṃ kalpenābhyasya mucyate
yaccāpi pañcagulmīye cikitsitamudāhṛtam 89
tadihāpi prayoktavyaṃ prasamīkṣya balābalam
iti ha smāha bhagavān kaśyapaḥ 90
iti khileṣu raktagulmaviniścayo nāma navamo'dhyāyaḥ
atha antarvatnīcikitsitādhyāyo daśamaḥ
athāto'ntarvatnīcikitsitamadhyāyaṃ vakṣyāmaḥ 1
yathovāca bhagavān kaśyapaḥ 2
sūkṣmāṃ cikitsāṃ vakṣyāmi garbhiṇīnāṃ vibhāgaśaḥ
tathā garbhaśca nārī ca vardhate rakṣyate'pi ca 3
garbhiṇīnāṃ jvaraḥ kaṣṭaḥ sarvavyādhiṣu pārthiva
jvaroṣmaṇā'bhitaptastu garbho yātyeva vikriyām 4
tasmājjvaracikitsāṃ tu pūrvameva nibodha me
kṣucchramābhyañjanādraukṣyādauṣṇyāpakvavidhāraṇāt 5
snehasvedauṣadhānāṃ ca vibhramāttejaso'pi ca
santāpānmanasaścāpi parvatānāṃ tathaiva ca 6
gandhācca tṛṇapuṣpāṇāṃ garbhiṇyā jāyate jvaraḥ
garbhiṇīṃ jvaritāṃ nārīmekāhamupavāsayet 7
tato dadyādalavaṇāṃ peyāṃ snehavivarjitām
tīkṣṇāni tvannapānāni svedamāyāsameva ca 8
varjayejjvaritā nārī yavāgūṃ kevalāṃ pibet
yavāgvā hrasite doṣe yūṣairannāni dāpayet 9
yūṣaistu hrasite doṣe rasaṃ vā kṣīrameva vā
dāpayenmatimān prājño na tvauṣadhavidhirhitaḥ 10
anubandhe tu doṣasya garbhakālamapekṣya ca
māsāccaturthāt prabhṛti bhiṣagbheṣajamācaret 11
śārīraṃ tu jvaraṃ jñātvā vātapittakaphātmakam
madhyāṃ kriyāṃ prayuñjīta saṃcintya gurulāghavam 12
upadravabalaṃ jñātvā sattvaṃ cāpi samīkṣya tu
garbhāvasthāṃ tu vijñāya lekhanāni pradāpayet 13
utpannāyāṃ tu tṛṣṇāyāṃ nātyuṣṇaṃ prapibejjalam
vātaśleṣmasamutthe tu jvare nīraṃ viṣāyate 14
atha pittakṛte cāpi śṛtaśītaṃ praśasyate
kupyapāṣāṇaniṣpakvaṃ śītaṃ tṛṣṇānibarhaṇam 15
jvare tu taruṇe dṛṣṭo vidhireṣa viśeṣataḥ
bhagnavege tu kartavyaṃ tṛṣṇāprajamanaiḥ śṛtam 16
jvaraṃ jvaraṃ samāsādya śītaṃ vā yadi vetaram
śiroroge tu kartavyo yathāvadbheṣajakramaḥ
bhagnavege jvare kṛtsne guru naiva praśasyate 17
taruṇe tu jvare nāryā abhyaṅgo na praśasyate
garbhe tu taruṇe datto garbhaghātāya kalpate 18
garbhiṇīnāṃ tu nārīṇāṃ nastato nānusecayet
nasyadānena garbhiṇyāḥ prāṇāstu parihīyate 19
kuṇirvā yadi vā'ndhaśca jāyate durbalendriyaḥ
dhūmapānena garbhiṇyā dhūmatejo'hato bhṛśam 20
vivarṇo jāyate garbhaḥ patedvā'pi viśāṃpate
śirovireke garbhiṇyāḥ saṃkṣobhāttu bhayena vā 21
mārutaḥ kupito dehe garbhaghātāya kalpate
athavā vātarogī tu garbho bhavati pārthiva 22
svedena taruṇe garbhe pittaṃ prakupitaṃ bhṛśam
cyāvayedāśu garbhaṃ tu tasmāt svedaṃ vivarjayet 23
svedaḥ sthire tu vihito garbhavaivarṇyakārakaḥ
vamanaṃ taruṇe garbhe svairguṇairgarbhaghātakam 24
nābhiprapīḍanatkarāt saṃkṣobhācca viśeṣataḥ
garbhiṇyāstaruṇe garbhe sraṃsanaṃ na praśasyate 25
gurutvāduṣṇatīkṣṇatvādvāhanāccāsya ghātakam
āsthāpanaṃ tu taruṇe garbhe nāryā na śasyate 26
anuvāsanaṃ ca matimāniti śāstraviniścayaḥ
āsthāpanaṃ sānuvāsaṃ karoti svena tejasā 27
hīnāṅgaṃ srāviṇaṃ vā'pi garbhamityeṣa niścayaḥ
tasmādetāni matimān garbhiṇyā na pradāpayet 28
imāni dadyāt saṃcintya rogāvasthāviśeṣavit
vidārigandhāṃ kalaśīṃ tathā gandharvahastakam 29
madhukaṃ bhadradāruṃ ca kvāthaḥ śarkarayā yutaḥ
vātajvaraharo deyo mātuluṅgarasāplutaḥ 30
vargo vidārigandhādiḥ kvathito nātiśītalaḥ
bhadradārusamāyukto vātajvaraharo mataḥ 31
eraṇḍo varuṇaścaiva bṛhatyau madhukaṃ tathā
vātajvaraharaḥ kvātho rāsnākalkasamāyutaḥ 32
dvipañcamūlaniṣkvāthaḥ koṣṇo vā yadi vā himaḥ
rāsnākalkasamāyukto vātajvarahito mataḥ 33
jīrṇe tu bhojane peyā tanvī lavaṇavarjitā
kuṣṭhaṃ sayaṣṭīmadhukaṃ rāsnā girikadambakaḥ 34
śatāhvā padmakaṃ caiva sārivośīramutpalam
mustā śṛgālavinnā ca karavindī tathā vacā 35
payasyā haṃsapādī ca tathā punnāgameva ca
karṣapramāṇānyetāni dadhimaṇḍena peṣayet 36
tatastameṣāmaṅgānāṃ niṣkvāthaṃ kvāthayedbhiṣak
bhāgāśca daśamūlasya kāryā dvipalasaṃmitāḥ 37
balātibalayoścaiva kuryādardhapalaṃ bhiṣak
koraṇḍamadhuśigrūṇi madayantī ca te trayaḥ 38
yavakolakulatthānāṃ bhāgāḥ syuḥ prasthasaṃmitāḥ
niṣkvāthyaitānapāṃ droṇe śeṣamāḍhakasaṃmitam 39
tatra dadyāt pratīvāpaṃ yat pūrvamupakalpitam
kṣīraṃ tathaiva gomūtraṃ vāruṇīṃ dadhi cottamam 40
bhiṣakkuḍavamātrāṇi tilatailena yojayet
avahṛtyāgninā siddhamīṣatkṣodāyitaṃ yadi 41
uṣṇenaitena tailena sarvagātrāṇi mrakṣayet
vātajvaraṃ nihantyetanmrakṣaṇaistribhireva tu 42
eṣo'bhyaṅgaḥ sthire garbhe yathāvat saṃpraśasyate
kṣīraṃ kṣīrayavāgūrvāṃ raso vā jāṅgalo hitaḥ 43
jīrṇajvare sadā nāryā vātaghnairauṣadhaiḥ śṛtaḥ
atha pittakṛte cāpi kvathitaṃ sārivādikam 44
śarkarāmadhusaṃyuktaṃ pāyayet kalyamutthitam
payasyā kṣīrakākolī mṛdvīkā madhukāni ca 45
śarkarāmadhusaṃyuktaṃ pānakaṃ paittike jvare
nīlotpalaṃ payasyā ca sārivā madhukaṃ madhu 46
pippalyo maricośīraṃ lodhraṃ lājā saśarkarā
etat kṣīrasamāyuktaṃ khajena mathitaṃ pibet 47
garbhiṇī jvaritā kṣipraṃ pittāttena praśāmyati
nalavañjulamūlāni gundrāmūlāni cāharet 48
sahāṃ ca sahadevāṃ ca mārkavaṃ pāṭaliṃ tathā
kṣīriṇāṃ ca pravālāni tathā jambvāmrayorapi 49
utpalaṃ sārivośīraṃ candanaṃ padmapatrakam
ślakṣṇānyetāni piṣṭāni pradehaḥ śītalo bhavet 50
pittajvaraharo nāryāstarpaṇo ghṛtasaṃyutaḥ
yavapiṣṭasya kuḍavo mañjiṣṭhārdhapalaṃ tathā 51
amlaprasthaśate tailaṃ tailaprasthaṃ vipācayet
dāhajvaraharaṃ tailaṃ praśastaṃ jvaranāśanam 52
atha śleṣmajvare nārīṃ rāsnākvāthaṃ suśītalam
kṣaudreṇa saha saṃyuktaṃ pāyayediti kaśyapaḥ 53
bhadradārukaniṣkvātho rāsnākṣaudrasamāyutaḥ
athavā candanakvāthaḥ pippalīkṣaudrasaṃyutaḥ 54
śleṣmajvaraharaḥ peyo rāsnāvāsā'mṛtāśṛtaḥ
śrīparṇikāmṛtānāṃ tu niṣkvātho madhuyojitaḥ 55
peyaḥ sayaṣṭīmadhuko jvare ślaiṣmikapaittike
mahataḥ pañcamūlasya kvāthaḥ ślaiṣmikavātike 56
rāsnākalkasamāyuktaḥ peyaḥ kalyamiti sthitiḥ
kvātho vidārigandhādeḥ śarkarāmadhuyojitaḥ 57
vātapittajvare peya iti ha smāha kaśyapaḥ
pittajvare himā peyā pathyā kṣīramathāpi ca 58
jīrṇe pittaharaiḥ pakvo yūṣastu caṇakaistathā
śleṣmajvare sukhoṣṇā tu peyā nāryāḥ praśasyate 59
tathaiva mudgayūṣo'tha maulako rasa eva ca
susātmyaśceti kartavyo vyādhāvasmin viśeṣataḥ 60
saṃsṛṣṭe tu bhiṣak prājño yojayeta yathābalam
sannipātasamutthāne tridoṣaśamanaṃ hitam 61
atha yā madyapā nārī śṛṇu tasyāścikitsitam
vātike paittike vā'pi ślaiṣmike ca viśāṃpate 62
jvare dadyāt surāṃ nāryā jalenārdhena yojitām
surayā vāsayitvaināṃ tataḥ kalpaṃ pradāpayet 63
hareṇumudgacuccūnāṃ karkaṭyāśca rasena tu
rasena kiñcidamlena laghūnyannāni bhojayet 64
lavaṇasnehahīnāni mṛdūni surabhīṇi ca
āhāreṇa gade bhagne madyasyoparame kṛte 65
yathoktā tu kriyā pathyā yathāsvamiti kaśyapaḥ
atisāre tu garbhiṇyāḥ samutpanne bhiṣagjitam 66
vātike paittike caiva ślaiṣmike ca pravakṣyate
viruddhādhyaśanājīrṇaistathaivātyaśanādapi 67
bhayodvegavighātādvā saṃghātāt pūraṇāt kṣayāt
kandamūlaphalairāmairduṣṭatoyaniṣevaṇāt 68
raukṣyādbubhukṣayā chokādgurvabhiṣyandibhojanāt
abdhātośca sadrekādatīsāraḥ pravartate 69
āmātisāre saṃjāte pācanāni pradāpayet
kuṭajasya ca bījāni mustā pāṭhā tathaiva ca 70
ajamodā'tha saralaṃ tathā cātiviṣā śubhā
āme śleṣmānvite peyametat piṣṭaṃ sukhāmbunā 71
pāṭhācandanabhāgaśca kuṭajasya phalāni ca
tathā cātiviṣā mukhyā piṣṭametaddhitāmbunā 72
āme pittānvite peyamiti ha smāha kaśyapaḥ
hiṅgusaindhavanāgāśca bṛhatyau kauṭajaṃ phalam 73
tathā pippalimūlaṃ ca mukhyā cātiviṣā nṛpa
āme vātotthite peyametat piṣṭaṃ sukhāmbunā 74
bṛhatyādistu pātavyaḥ sannipātasamutthite
pakvasaṃgrahaṇe pathyaḥ sarveṣāmiti niścayaḥ 75
ślaiṣmike madhusaṃyuktastaṇḍulodakasaṃyutaḥ
ambaṣṭhādigaṇaḥ peyo bhinnavarcovibandhanaḥ 76
athavā kauṭajaṃ piṣṭvā phalaṃ kṣaudreṇa saṃyutam
